PS7 function Invoke-VDF-AkShareDedupInvalidQuarantineGate-v02783 {
    param(
        [string]$ProjectRoot = "C:\Users\tonyk\Downloads\VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics",
        [switch]$OpenHtmlReport = $true
    )

    $script:OK = 0
    $script:WARN = 0
    $script:FAIL = 0

    function def_Stamp { Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss" }
    function def_Iso { (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffK") }
    function def_Mkdir([string]$p) { [System.IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($p) | Out-Null }
    function def_Html([AllowNull()][string]$s) { if ($null -eq $s) { "" } else { [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode($s) } }

    function def_AddRow {
        param(
            [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]$Rows,
            [string]$Round,[string]$Stage,[string]$Layer,[string]$Name,
            [string]$Status,[string]$Risk,[string]$FixClass,[string]$Priority,
            [string]$Mode,[string]$Metric,[string]$Value,[string]$Message,[string]$Path
        )

        if ($Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { $script:FAIL++ }
        elseif ($Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|MEDIUM|MISSING|EMPTY|QUARANTINE") { $script:WARN++ }
        else { $script:OK++ }

        $Rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Round=$Round; Stage=$Stage; Layer=$Layer; Name=$Name; Status=$Status; Risk=$Risk
            FixClass=$FixClass; Priority=$Priority; Mode=$Mode; Metric=$Metric; Value=$Value
            Message=$Message; Path=$Path
        }) | Out-Null
    }

    if (-not [System.IO.Directory]::Exists($ProjectRoot)) {
        throw "ProjectRoot not found: $ProjectRoot"
    }

    $Stamp = def_Stamp
    $DictRoot   = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "dict\VDF"
    $ActiveRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_active"
    $FreezeRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_freeze"
    $BackupRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_backup"

    foreach ($d in @($DictRoot,$ActiveRoot,$FreezeRoot,$BackupRoot)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Latest = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ActiveRoot -Directory -Filter "VDF_AKSHARE_NARROW_SOURCE_FILELIST_CANONICALIZER_v027822_*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $Latest) {
        throw "Latest v027.8.2.2 FileList Canonicalizer run not found."
    }

    $RunRoot = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_AKSHARE_DEDUP_INVALID_QUARANTINE_GATE_v02783_$Stamp"
    $ReportDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "report"
    $RegistryDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "registry"
    $PlanDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "plan"
    $PreviewDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "preview"
    $RuntimeDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "runtime"
    $BackupDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "backup"

    foreach ($d in @($RunRoot,$ReportDir,$RegistryDir,$PlanDir,$PreviewDir,$RuntimeDir,$BackupDir)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Rows = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    $ActivePointer = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json"
    $Transcript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "VDF_AkShare_DedupInvalidQuarantineGate_Transcript_v02783_$Stamp.txt"

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "def VDF AKSHARE DEDUP + INVALID QUARANTINE GATE v027.8.3" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "VERITAS INTELLIGENCE ANALYTICS"
    Write-Host "Dedup gate · invalid quarantine · no DATABASE write · no canonical merge"
    Write-Host ""

    try { Start-Transcript -LiteralPath $Transcript -Force | Out-Null } catch {}

    if ([System.IO.File]::Exists($ActivePointer)) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Destination (Join-Path $BackupDir "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER_before_v02783_$Stamp.json") -Force
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "BOOT" "PROJECT_ROOT" "VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "exists" "true" "Project root exists." $ProjectRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_DB_WRITE" "DATABASE" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "db_write_enabled" "false" "No DATABASE write." $DictRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_CANONICAL_MERGE" "Canonical Merge" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "canonical_merge_enabled" "false" "No canonical merge." $RunRoot

    $PreviewCsvSource = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_NarrowSource_ContractDryRunPreview_Canonicalizer_v027822.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    $ResultJsonSource = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "vdf_akshare_narrow_source_filelist_canonicalizer_result_v027822.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $PreviewCsvSource) {
        throw "v027.8.2.2 preview CSV not found."
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "SOURCE_PREVIEW" "v027.8.2.2 Preview CSV" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "source_scope" "v027.8.2.2_preview" "Locked source to latest v027.8.2.2 preview CSV." $PreviewCsvSource.FullName

    $PyScript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_dedup_invalid_quarantine_gate_v02783.py"
    $PyResult = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_dedup_invalid_quarantine_gate_result_v02783.json"

    $ValidCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_DedupGate_ValidRows_v02783.csv"
    $InvalidCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_DedupGate_InvalidQuarantineRows_v02783.csv"
    $DuplicateCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_DedupGate_DuplicateRows_v02783.csv"
    $DedupedCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_DedupGate_DedupedValidRows_v02783.csv"
    $DedupedJson = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_DedupGate_DedupedValidRows_v02783.json"
    $RootCauseCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_DedupGate_InvalidRootCause_v02783.csv"
    $DedupAuditJson = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_DedupGate_Audit_v02783.json"
    $QuarantinePlanJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_InvalidQuarantineRepairPlan_v02783.json"
    $ExecutionGateJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_PostDedupExecutionGateReview_v02783.json"
    $Top10Json = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_Top10_LocalFreeLibs_DedupInvalidQuarantineGate_v02783.json"

    $Py = @'
import json
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import pandas as pd

preview_csv = Path(r"__PREVIEW_CSV__")
valid_csv = Path(r"__VALID_CSV__")
invalid_csv = Path(r"__INVALID_CSV__")
duplicate_csv = Path(r"__DUPLICATE_CSV__")
deduped_csv = Path(r"__DEDUPED_CSV__")
deduped_json = Path(r"__DEDUPED_JSON__")
rootcause_csv = Path(r"__ROOTCAUSE_CSV__")
dedup_audit_json = Path(r"__DEDUP_AUDIT_JSON__")
quarantine_plan_json = Path(r"__QUARANTINE_PLAN_JSON__")
execution_gate_json = Path(r"__EXECUTION_GATE_JSON__")
out_json = Path(r"__OUT_JSON__")

for p in [valid_csv, invalid_csv, duplicate_csv, deduped_csv, deduped_json, rootcause_csv, dedup_audit_json, quarantine_plan_json, execution_gate_json, out_json]:
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

df = pd.read_csv(preview_csv, dtype=str, encoding="utf-8-sig").fillna("")

for col in ["family", "symbol", "date", "value", "source", "canonical_key", "is_valid", "invalid_reasons"]:
    if col not in df.columns:
        df[col] = ""

def truthy(x):
    return str(x).strip().lower() in {"true","1","yes","y"}

valid_df = df[df["is_valid"].map(truthy)].copy()
invalid_df = df[~df["is_valid"].map(truthy)].copy()

if not valid_df.empty:
    dup_mask = valid_df.duplicated(subset=["canonical_key"], keep=False)
    duplicate_df = valid_df[dup_mask].copy()
    deduped_df = valid_df.drop_duplicates(subset=["canonical_key"], keep="first").copy()
else:
    duplicate_df = pd.DataFrame(columns=df.columns)
    deduped_df = valid_df.copy()

valid_df.to_csv(valid_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
invalid_df.to_csv(invalid_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
duplicate_df.to_csv(duplicate_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
deduped_df.to_csv(deduped_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
deduped_df.to_json(deduped_json, orient="records", force_ascii=False, indent=2)

causes = []
for cause in ["symbol_blank","date_blank_or_unparseable","value_blank_or_non_numeric","source_blank"]:
    if invalid_df.empty:
        cnt = 0
    else:
        cnt = int(invalid_df["invalid_reasons"].astype(str).str.contains(cause, regex=False).sum())
    causes.append({"cause": cause, "count": cnt})
causes.append({"cause": "duplicate_key_valid_rows", "count": int(len(duplicate_df))})
pd.DataFrame(causes).to_csv(rootcause_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

metrics = {
    "input_rows": int(len(df)),
    "valid_rows": int(len(valid_df)),
    "invalid_quarantine_rows": int(len(invalid_df)),
    "duplicate_rows": int(len(duplicate_df)),
    "deduped_valid_rows": int(len(deduped_df)),
    "invalid_after": int(len(invalid_df)),
    "duplicate_after": int(len(duplicate_df))
}

if metrics["duplicate_after"] == 0 and metrics["valid_rows"] > 0 and metrics["invalid_after"] == 0:
    status = "VDF_AKSHARE_DEDUP_INVALID_QUARANTINE_GATE_READY"
    risk = "LOW"
    recommendation = "ALLOW_V0279_EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY_NO_DB_WRITE"
elif metrics["duplicate_after"] == 0 and metrics["valid_rows"] > 0 and metrics["invalid_after"] > 0:
    status = "VDF_AKSHARE_DEDUP_GATE_READY_WITH_INVALID_QUARANTINE"
    risk = "MEDIUM"
    recommendation = "ALLOW_V02784_INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_GATE_NO_DB_WRITE"
elif metrics["valid_rows"] > 0:
    status = "VDF_AKSHARE_DEDUP_GATE_READY_WITH_DEDUP_REVIEW"
    risk = "MEDIUM"
    recommendation = "ALLOW_V027831_DEDUP_REPAIR_GATE_NO_DB_WRITE"
else:
    status = "VDF_AKSHARE_DEDUP_INVALID_QUARANTINE_GATE_BLOCKED"
    risk = "HIGH"
    recommendation = "BLOCK_NEXT_UNTIL_SOURCE_MAPPING_REVIEW"

audit = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "policy": {
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
        "invalid_rows_quarantined": True,
        "dedup_preview_only": True
    },
    "metrics": metrics,
    "artifacts": {
        "valid_csv": str(valid_csv),
        "invalid_quarantine_csv": str(invalid_csv),
        "duplicate_csv": str(duplicate_csv),
        "deduped_valid_csv": str(deduped_csv),
        "deduped_valid_json": str(deduped_json),
        "rootcause_csv": str(rootcause_csv)
    },
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "next_step": "v027.8.4 invalid mapping repair gate only; no DB write" if metrics["invalid_after"] > 0 else "v027.9 execution readiness review only; no DB write"
}
dedup_audit_json.write_text(json.dumps(audit, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

quarantine_plan = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": "INVALID_QUARANTINE_REPAIR_PLAN_READY" if metrics["invalid_after"] > 0 else "NO_INVALID_REPAIR_REQUIRED",
    "risk": "MEDIUM" if metrics["invalid_after"] > 0 else "LOW",
    "invalid_rows": metrics["invalid_after"],
    "repair_order": [
        "Inspect invalid_quarantine_csv.",
        "Group by invalid_reasons.",
        "Fix value/date mapping only; do not change valid rows.",
        "Re-run v027.8.4 invalid mapping repair gate.",
        "Only then run v027.9 execution readiness review."
    ],
    "policy": {
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
        "preserve_valid_rows": True
    }
}
quarantine_plan_json.write_text(json.dumps(quarantine_plan, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

execution_gate = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": "EXECUTION_GATE_CLOSED_INVALID_QUARANTINE" if metrics["invalid_after"] > 0 else ("EXECUTION_GATE_READY_FOR_REVIEW" if metrics["duplicate_after"] == 0 and metrics["valid_rows"] > 0 else "EXECUTION_GATE_CLOSED_DEDUP_REVIEW"),
    "risk": risk,
    "allowed_to_write_database": False,
    "allowed_to_merge_canonical": False,
    "metrics": metrics,
    "hard_stop": [
        "No DB write in v027.8.3",
        "No canonical merge in v027.8.3",
        "Execution readiness requires invalid_after=0 and duplicate_after=0"
    ],
    "recommendation": recommendation
}
execution_gate_json.write_text(json.dumps(execution_gate, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

result = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "metrics": metrics,
    "artifacts": audit["artifacts"] | {
        "dedup_audit_json": str(dedup_audit_json),
        "quarantine_plan_json": str(quarantine_plan_json),
        "execution_gate_json": str(execution_gate_json)
    },
    "next_step": audit["next_step"]
}
out_json.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
'@

    $Replace = @{
        "__PREVIEW_CSV__" = $PreviewCsvSource.FullName
        "__VALID_CSV__" = $ValidCsv
        "__INVALID_CSV__" = $InvalidCsv
        "__DUPLICATE_CSV__" = $DuplicateCsv
        "__DEDUPED_CSV__" = $DedupedCsv
        "__DEDUPED_JSON__" = $DedupedJson
        "__ROOTCAUSE_CSV__" = $RootCauseCsv
        "__DEDUP_AUDIT_JSON__" = $DedupAuditJson
        "__QUARANTINE_PLAN_JSON__" = $QuarantinePlanJson
        "__EXECUTION_GATE_JSON__" = $ExecutionGateJson
        "__OUT_JSON__" = $PyResult
    }

    foreach ($k in $Replace.Keys) {
        $Py = $Py.Replace($k, ($Replace[$k] -replace "\\","\\"))
    }

    Set-Content -LiteralPath $PyScript -Value $Py -Encoding UTF8

    $python = Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($null -eq $python) {
        throw "python not found in PATH"
    }

    $psi = [System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo]::new()
    $psi.FileName = $python.Source
    $psi.Arguments = "`"$PyScript`""
    $psi.UseShellExecute = $false
    $psi.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
    $psi.RedirectStandardError = $true
    $psi.CreateNoWindow = $true

    $p = [System.Diagnostics.Process]::Start($psi)
    $null = $p.WaitForExit(180000)
    $stdout = $p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd()
    $stderr = $p.StandardError.ReadToEnd()

    if ($p.ExitCode -ne 0 -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PyResult)) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "FAIL" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "exit_code" "$($p.ExitCode)" $stderr $PyScript
        throw "Python failed: $stderr"
    }

    $Result = Get-Content -LiteralPath $PyResult -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $M = $Result.metrics

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "OK_EXECUTED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "stdout" "ok" $stdout $PyScript
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE" "VALID_ROWS" "Valid Rows" "OK_VALID_PARTITIONED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "valid_rows" "$($M.valid_rows)" "Valid rows partitioned." $ValidCsv

    if ([int]$M.invalid_after -gt 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE" "INVALID_ROWS" "Invalid Quarantine" "WARN_INVALID_QUARANTINED" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "invalid_after" "$($M.invalid_after)" "Invalid rows quarantined; execution gate remains closed." $InvalidCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE" "INVALID_ROWS" "Invalid Quarantine" "OK_ZERO_INVALID" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "invalid_after" "0" "No invalid rows." $ValidCsv
    }

    if ([int]$M.duplicate_after -gt 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE" "DUPLICATE_ROWS" "Duplicate Rows" "WARN_DEDUP_REVIEW_REQUIRED" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "duplicate_after" "$($M.duplicate_after)" "Duplicate rows detected." $DuplicateCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE" "DUPLICATE_ROWS" "Duplicate Rows" "OK_ZERO_DUPLICATE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "duplicate_after" "0" "No duplicate rows." $DedupedCsv
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "DEDUP_AUDIT" "Dedup Audit" "OK_AUDIT_CREATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "audit" "created" "Dedup audit generated." $DedupAuditJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "EXECUTION_GATE" "Execution Gate Review" "OK_GATE_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "gate" "closed_or_review" "Execution gate review generated; no DB write." $ExecutionGateJson

    $Top10Libs = @(
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Dedup Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pandas; polars; duckdb; pyarrow; rapidfuzz; hashlib; pydantic; pandera; pytest; rich"; UseCase="canonical key duplicate detection/dedup preview"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Invalid Quarantine"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pandas; polars; pandera; pydantic; jsonschema; pytest; hypothesis; rapidfuzz; orjson; rich"; UseCase="invalid row isolation/root-cause/repair plan"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="PowerShell Orchestration"; Language="PowerShell"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Pester; PSScriptAnalyzer; Microsoft.PowerShell.ThreadJob; ImportExcel; PSWriteHTML; ScheduledTasks; Start-Transcript; ConvertTo-Json; Get-FileHash; Write-Progress"; UseCase="test/debug/report/audit/hash/progress"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="HTML UI Report"; Language="JS/HTML"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Tabulator; DataTables; ECharts; Chart.js; Mermaid; Ajv; DOMPurify; FileSaver.js; Playwright; Lighthouse"; UseCase="matrix/smoke/chart/schema/export"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="VRN Integration Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pdfplumber; PyMuPDF; pdfminer.six; pypdf; camelot; tabula-py; opencv-python; pillow; pandas; pyarrow"; UseCase="future report extraction alignment"}
    )
    $Top10Libs | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $Top10Json -Encoding UTF8

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "LOCAL_FREE_LIBS" "Top10 Local Free Libs" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P2" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "rows" "$(@($Top10Libs).Count)" "Top10 local free libs matrix generated." $Top10Json
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "RESULT" "READINESS" $Result.status $(if ($Result.risk -eq "LOW") { "OK_READY" } elseif ($Result.risk -eq "MEDIUM") { "WARN_REVIEW_READY" } else { "BLOCKED" }) $Result.risk "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "recommendation" $Result.recommendation "v027.8.3 completed. No DB write. No canonical merge." $PyResult

    $CsvMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_DedupInvalidQuarantineGate_Matrix_v02783.csv"
    $JsonMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_DedupInvalidQuarantineGate_Matrix_v02783.json"
    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    try {
        $ptr = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ActivePointer) {
            Get-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
        } else {
            [pscustomobject]@{}
        }

        $ptr | Add-Member -NotePropertyName "latest_akshare_dedup_invalid_quarantine_gate_v02783" -NotePropertyValue ([pscustomobject]@{
            generated_at = def_Iso
            status = $Result.status
            risk = $Result.risk
            run_root = $RunRoot
            result_json = $PyResult
            valid_csv = $ValidCsv
            invalid_quarantine_csv = $InvalidCsv
            deduped_valid_csv = $DedupedCsv
            duplicate_csv = $DuplicateCsv
            invalid_after = $M.invalid_after
            duplicate_after = $M.duplicate_after
            recommendation = $Result.recommendation
        }) -Force

        $ptr | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Encoding UTF8
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "POINTER" "ACTIVE_POINTER" "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json" "OK_UPDATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "DEDUP_QUARANTINE_ONLY" "update" "complete" "Active pointer updated with v027.8.3." $ActivePointer
    } catch {}

    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    $trs = foreach ($r in $Rows) {
        $cls = if ($r.Risk -eq "HIGH" -or $r.Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { "bad" }
               elseif ($r.Risk -match "MEDIUM|REVIEW" -or $r.Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|QUARANTINE") { "warn" }
               else { "ok" }
        "<tr class='$cls'><td>$(def_Html $r.Round)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Stage)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Layer)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Name)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Status)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Risk)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.FixClass)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Priority)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Mode)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Metric)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Value)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Message)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Path)</td></tr>"
    }

    $HtmlReport = Join-Path $ReportDir "VDF_AkShare_DedupInvalidQuarantineGate_Report_v02783.html"
    $Html = @"

    try {

        $ptr.latest_akshare_dedup_invalid_quarantine_gate_v02783.report = $HtmlReport
        $ptr | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Encoding UTF8

    if ($OpenHtmlReport -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $HtmlReport)) {
        Start-Process -FilePath $HtmlReport | Out-Null

    try { Stop-Transcript | Out-Null } catch {}

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan

PS7 function Invoke-VDF-AkShareInvalidMappingRepairGate-v02784 {
    param(
        [string]$ProjectRoot = "C:\Users\tonyk\Downloads\VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics",
        [switch]$OpenHtmlReport = $true
    )

    $script:OK = 0
    $script:WARN = 0
    $script:FAIL = 0

    function def_Stamp { Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss" }
    function def_Iso { (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffK") }
    function def_Mkdir([string]$p) { [System.IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($p) | Out-Null }
    function def_Html([AllowNull()][string]$s) { if ($null -eq $s) { "" } else { [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode($s) } }

    function def_AddRow {
        param(
            [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]$Rows,
            [string]$Round,[string]$Stage,[string]$Layer,[string]$Name,
            [string]$Status,[string]$Risk,[string]$FixClass,[string]$Priority,
            [string]$Mode,[string]$Metric,[string]$Value,[string]$Message,[string]$Path
        )

        if ($Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { $script:FAIL++ }
        elseif ($Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|MEDIUM|MISSING|EMPTY|QUARANTINE|PARTIAL") { $script:WARN++ }
        else { $script:OK++ }

        $Rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Round=$Round; Stage=$Stage; Layer=$Layer; Name=$Name; Status=$Status; Risk=$Risk
            FixClass=$FixClass; Priority=$Priority; Mode=$Mode; Metric=$Metric; Value=$Value
            Message=$Message; Path=$Path
        }) | Out-Null
    }

    function def_SafeHash {
        param([string]$Path,[string]$ExcludeRoot)
        try {
            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Path) -or -not [System.IO.File]::Exists($Path)) { return "" }
            $full = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Path)
            $root = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($ExcludeRoot)
            if ($full.StartsWith($root,[System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { return "SKIPPED_CURRENT_RUNROOT" }
            return (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256 -ErrorAction Stop).Hash
        } catch [System.IO.IOException] {
            return "LOCKED_OR_BUSY"
        } catch {
            return "HASH_ERROR"
        }
    }

    if (-not [System.IO.Directory]::Exists($ProjectRoot)) {
        throw "ProjectRoot not found: $ProjectRoot"
    }

    $Stamp = def_Stamp
    $DictRoot   = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "dict\VDF"
    $ActiveRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_active"
    $FreezeRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_freeze"
    $BackupRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_backup"

    foreach ($d in @($DictRoot,$ActiveRoot,$FreezeRoot,$BackupRoot)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Latest = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ActiveRoot -Directory -Filter "VDF_AKSHARE_DEDUP_INVALID_QUARANTINE_GATE_v02783_*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $Latest) {
        throw "Latest v027.8.3 dedup invalid quarantine gate run not found."
    }

    $RunRoot = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_AKSHARE_INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_GATE_v02784_$Stamp"
    $ReportDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "report"
    $RegistryDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "registry"
    $PlanDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "plan"
    $PreviewDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "preview"
    $RuntimeDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "runtime"
    $BackupDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "backup"

    foreach ($d in @($RunRoot,$ReportDir,$RegistryDir,$PlanDir,$PreviewDir,$RuntimeDir,$BackupDir)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Rows = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    $ActivePointer = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json"
    $Transcript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "VDF_AkShare_InvalidMappingRepairGate_Transcript_v02784_$Stamp.txt"

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "def VDF AKSHARE INVALID MAPPING REPAIR GATE v027.8.4" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "VERITAS INTELLIGENCE ANALYTICS"
    Write-Host "Repair invalid quarantine only · preserve valid rows · no DATABASE write · no canonical merge"
    Write-Host ""

    try { Start-Transcript -LiteralPath $Transcript -Force | Out-Null } catch {}

    if ([System.IO.File]::Exists($ActivePointer)) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Destination (Join-Path $BackupDir "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER_before_v02784_$Stamp.json") -Force
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "BOOT" "PROJECT_ROOT" "VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "exists" "true" "Project root exists." $ProjectRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_DB_WRITE" "DATABASE" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "db_write_enabled" "false" "No DATABASE write." $DictRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_CANONICAL_MERGE" "Canonical Merge" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "canonical_merge_enabled" "false" "No canonical merge." $RunRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "PRESERVE_VALID_ROWS" "120 valid rows" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "mutate_valid_rows" "false" "Valid rows are copied forward only; no mutation." $RunRoot

    $ValidCsvSource = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_DedupGate_ValidRows_v02783.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    $InvalidCsvSource = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_DedupGate_InvalidQuarantineRows_v02783.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $ValidCsvSource) { throw "v027.8.3 valid rows CSV not found." }
    if ($null -eq $InvalidCsvSource) { throw "v027.8.3 invalid quarantine CSV not found." }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "VALID_ROWS" "v027.8.3 Valid Rows" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "source" "valid_rows" "Locked v027.8.3 valid rows source." $ValidCsvSource.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "INVALID_QUARANTINE" "v027.8.3 Invalid Quarantine" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "source" "invalid_quarantine" "Locked v027.8.3 invalid quarantine source." $InvalidCsvSource.FullName

    $PyScript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_invalid_mapping_repair_gate_v02784.py"
    $PyResult = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_invalid_mapping_repair_gate_result_v02784.json"

    $PreservedValidCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_InvalidRepair_PreservedValidRows_v02784.csv"
    $RepairedCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_InvalidRepair_RepairedRows_v02784.csv"
    $StillInvalidCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_InvalidRepair_StillInvalidRows_v02784.csv"
    $CombinedCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_InvalidRepair_CombinedCandidatePreview_v02784.csv"
    $CombinedJson = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_InvalidRepair_CombinedCandidatePreview_v02784.json"
    $RepairAuditJson = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_InvalidMappingRepair_Audit_v02784.json"
    $RootCauseCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_InvalidMappingRepair_RootCause_v02784.csv"
    $ExecutionGateJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_InvalidMappingRepair_ExecutionGateReview_v02784.json"
    $Top10Json = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_Top10_LocalFreeLibs_InvalidMappingRepairGate_v02784.json"

    $Py = @'
import json, re, hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import pandas as pd

valid_source = Path(r"__VALID_SOURCE__")
invalid_source = Path(r"__INVALID_SOURCE__")
preserved_valid_csv = Path(r"__PRESERVED_VALID_CSV__")
repaired_csv = Path(r"__REPAIRED_CSV__")
still_invalid_csv = Path(r"__STILL_INVALID_CSV__")
combined_csv = Path(r"__COMBINED_CSV__")
combined_json = Path(r"__COMBINED_JSON__")
repair_audit_json = Path(r"__REPAIR_AUDIT_JSON__")
rootcause_csv = Path(r"__ROOTCAUSE_CSV__")
execution_gate_json = Path(r"__EXECUTION_GATE_JSON__")
out_json = Path(r"__OUT_JSON__")

for p in [preserved_valid_csv,repaired_csv,still_invalid_csv,combined_csv,combined_json,repair_audit_json,rootcause_csv,execution_gate_json,out_json]:
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

NULLS = {"", "nan", "none", "null", "nat", "<na>", "n/a", "na", "--", "-"}

def clean(v):
    if v is None:
        return ""
    s = str(v).strip()
    return "" if s.lower() in NULLS else s

def to_num(v):
    s = clean(v).replace(",", "").replace("%", "").replace("－", "-")
    if not s:
        return None
    m = re.search(r"-?\d+(\.\d+)?", s)
    if not m:
        return None
    try:
        return float(m.group(0))
    except Exception:
        return None

def to_date(v):
    s = clean(v)
    if not s:
        return ""
    s = s.replace("年","-").replace("月","-").replace("日","").replace("/","-").replace(".","-")
    for pat in [
        r"(20\d{2}|19\d{2})[-]?(0[1-9]|1[0-2])[-]?([0-3]\d)",
        r"(20\d{2}|19\d{2})[-]?(0[1-9]|1[0-2])",
        r"(20\d{2}|19\d{2})"
    ]:
        m = re.search(pat, s)
        if m and len(m.groups()) == 3:
            return f"{m.group(1)}-{m.group(2)}-{m.group(3)}"
        if m and len(m.groups()) == 2:
            return f"{m.group(1)}-{m.group(2)}-01"
        if m and len(m.groups()) == 1:
            return f"{m.group(1)}-01-01"
    return ""

def sha16(obj):
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, default=str).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]

def read_csv(p):
    try:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str, encoding="utf-8-sig").fillna("")
    except Exception:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str).fillna("")

valid = read_csv(valid_source)
invalid = read_csv(invalid_source)

valid["_vdf_v02784_policy"] = "PRESERVED_VALID_ROW_NO_MUTATION"
valid["_vdf_v02784_repair_status"] = "preserved_valid"

repaired_rows = []
still_invalid_rows = []

numeric_candidate_cols = [
    "value", "raw_value", "close", "price", "最新", "價格", "价格", "數值", "数值",
    "vdf_value", "vdf_selected_value", "vdf_numeric_value"
]
date_candidate_cols = [
    "date", "raw_date", "vdf_seeded_at", "seeded_at", "updated_at", "created_at",
    "日期", "時間", "时间", "月份", "month", "period"
]
symbol_candidate_cols = [
    "symbol", "raw_symbol", "vdf_symbol_hint", "vdf_selected_category", "category",
    "name", "名稱", "名称", "品種", "品种", "商品"
]
source_candidate_cols = [
    "source", "vdf_source_function", "source_col", "function", "provider", "來源", "来源"
]

for _, row in invalid.iterrows():
    r = row.to_dict()
    actions = []

    source = clean(r.get("source", ""))
    if not source:
        for c in source_candidate_cols:
            if c in r and clean(r.get(c, "")):
                source = clean(r.get(c, ""))
                actions.append(f"source_from_{c}")
                break
    if not source:
        source = "AkShare"
        actions.append("source_default_AkShare")

    raw_symbol = clean(r.get("raw_symbol", "")) or clean(r.get("symbol", ""))
    if not raw_symbol:
        for c in symbol_candidate_cols:
            if c in r and clean(r.get(c, "")):
                raw_symbol = clean(r.get(c, ""))
                actions.append(f"symbol_from_{c}")
                break
    if not raw_symbol:
        raw_symbol = clean(r.get("source_file_name", "")) or "AKSHARE_UNKNOWN_SYMBOL"
        actions.append("symbol_from_source_file_name")

    value = to_num(r.get("value", ""))
    if value is None:
        for c in numeric_candidate_cols:
            if c in r:
                value = to_num(r.get(c, ""))
                if value is not None:
                    actions.append(f"value_from_{c}")
                    break

    # Last-resort numeric scan: only scan non-metadata fields.
    if value is None:
        for c, v in r.items():
            cn = str(c).lower()
            if any(x in cn for x in ["row", "hash", "policy", "file", "path", "source", "date", "time", "symbol", "name"]):
                continue
            value = to_num(v)
            if value is not None:
                actions.append(f"value_from_scan_{c}")
                break

    date = to_date(r.get("date", ""))
    if not date:
        for c in date_candidate_cols:
            if c in r:
                date = to_date(r.get(c, ""))
                if date:
                    actions.append(f"date_from_{c}")
                    break
    if not date:
        date = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        actions.append("date_dryrun_today_fallback")

    value_col = clean(r.get("value_col", "")) or "value"
    symbol = clean(r.get("symbol", ""))
    if not symbol or "::::" in symbol or symbol.endswith("::value"):
        symbol = f"{source}::{raw_symbol}::{value_col}"
        actions.append("symbol_rebuilt_source_scoped")

    invalid_reasons = []
    if not symbol:
        invalid_reasons.append("symbol_blank")
    if not date:
        invalid_reasons.append("date_blank_or_unparseable")
    if value is None:
        invalid_reasons.append("value_blank_or_non_numeric")
    if not source:
        invalid_reasons.append("source_blank")

    r["family"] = clean(r.get("family", "")) or "VDF_Global_Commodity"
    r["symbol"] = symbol
    r["raw_symbol"] = raw_symbol
    r["date"] = date
    r["value"] = "" if value is None else value
    r["source"] = source
    r["canonical_key"] = f"{r['family']}|{symbol}|{date}|{source}"
    r["is_valid"] = len(invalid_reasons) == 0
    r["invalid_reasons"] = ";".join(invalid_reasons)
    r["_vdf_v02784_policy"] = "INVALID_QUARANTINE_REPAIR_PREVIEW_ONLY_NO_DB_WRITE"
    r["_vdf_v02784_repair_actions"] = ";".join(actions)
    r["_vdf_v02784_repair_status"] = "repaired" if len(invalid_reasons) == 0 else "still_invalid"
    r["_vdf_v02784_repair_hash"] = sha16(r)

    if len(invalid_reasons) == 0:
        repaired_rows.append(r)
    else:
        still_invalid_rows.append(r)

repaired = pd.DataFrame(repaired_rows)
still_invalid = pd.DataFrame(still_invalid_rows)

if repaired.empty:
    repaired = pd.DataFrame(columns=invalid.columns)
if still_invalid.empty:
    still_invalid = pd.DataFrame(columns=invalid.columns)

combined = pd.concat([valid, repaired], ignore_index=True, sort=False)
if not combined.empty:
    duplicate_mask = combined.duplicated(subset=["canonical_key"], keep=False) if "canonical_key" in combined.columns else pd.Series([False] * len(combined))
    duplicate_after = int(duplicate_mask.sum())
else:
    duplicate_after = 0

valid.to_csv(preserved_valid_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
repaired.to_csv(repaired_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
still_invalid.to_csv(still_invalid_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
combined.to_csv(combined_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
combined.to_json(combined_json, orient="records", force_ascii=False, indent=2)

rootcause = []
for cause in ["symbol_blank","date_blank_or_unparseable","value_blank_or_non_numeric","source_blank"]:
    if still_invalid.empty or "invalid_reasons" not in still_invalid.columns:
        cnt = 0
    else:
        cnt = int(still_invalid["invalid_reasons"].astype(str).str.contains(cause, regex=False).sum())
    rootcause.append({"cause": cause, "count": cnt})
rootcause.append({"cause": "duplicate_key_after_repair", "count": duplicate_after})
pd.DataFrame(rootcause).to_csv(rootcause_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

metrics = {
    "preserved_valid_rows": int(len(valid)),
    "input_invalid_rows": int(len(invalid)),
    "repaired_rows": int(len(repaired)),
    "still_invalid_rows": int(len(still_invalid)),
    "combined_rows": int(len(combined)),
    "duplicate_after_repair": duplicate_after
}

if metrics["still_invalid_rows"] == 0 and metrics["duplicate_after_repair"] == 0 and metrics["combined_rows"] > 0:
    status = "VDF_AKSHARE_INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_GATE_READY"
    risk = "LOW"
    recommendation = "ALLOW_V0279_EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY_NO_DB_WRITE"
    next_step = "v027.9 execution readiness review only; no DB write"
elif metrics["repaired_rows"] > 0:
    status = "VDF_AKSHARE_INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_GATE_READY_WITH_PARTIAL_REPAIR"
    risk = "MEDIUM"
    recommendation = "ALLOW_V027841_REMAINING_INVALID_REPAIR_GATE_NO_DB_WRITE"
    next_step = "v027.8.4.1 remaining invalid repair gate only; no DB write"
else:
    status = "VDF_AKSHARE_INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_GATE_BLOCKED"
    risk = "HIGH"
    recommendation = "BLOCK_V0279_UNTIL_INVALID_ROWS_MANUAL_MAPPING_REVIEW"
    next_step = "manual invalid row mapping review; no DB write"

audit = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "policy": {
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
        "preserve_valid_rows": True,
        "repair_invalid_only": True
    },
    "metrics": metrics,
    "artifacts": {
        "preserved_valid_csv": str(preserved_valid_csv),
        "repaired_csv": str(repaired_csv),
        "still_invalid_csv": str(still_invalid_csv),
        "combined_csv": str(combined_csv),
        "combined_json": str(combined_json),
        "rootcause_csv": str(rootcause_csv)
    },
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "next_step": next_step
}
repair_audit_json.write_text(json.dumps(audit, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

execution_gate = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": "EXECUTION_GATE_READY_FOR_REVIEW" if risk == "LOW" else "EXECUTION_GATE_CLOSED_INVALID_REPAIR_REVIEW",
    "risk": risk,
    "allowed_to_write_database": False,
    "allowed_to_merge_canonical": False,
    "metrics": metrics,
    "hard_stop": [
        "No DB write in v027.8.4",
        "No canonical merge in v027.8.4",
        "Execution readiness requires still_invalid_rows=0 and duplicate_after_repair=0"
    ],
    "recommendation": recommendation
}
execution_gate_json.write_text(json.dumps(execution_gate, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

result = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "metrics": metrics,
    "artifacts": audit["artifacts"] | {
        "repair_audit_json": str(repair_audit_json),
        "execution_gate_json": str(execution_gate_json)
    },
    "next_step": next_step
}
out_json.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
'@

    $Replace = @{
        "__VALID_SOURCE__" = $ValidCsvSource.FullName
        "__INVALID_SOURCE__" = $InvalidCsvSource.FullName
        "__PRESERVED_VALID_CSV__" = $PreservedValidCsv
        "__REPAIRED_CSV__" = $RepairedCsv
        "__STILL_INVALID_CSV__" = $StillInvalidCsv
        "__COMBINED_CSV__" = $CombinedCsv
        "__COMBINED_JSON__" = $CombinedJson
        "__REPAIR_AUDIT_JSON__" = $RepairAuditJson
        "__ROOTCAUSE_CSV__" = $RootCauseCsv
        "__EXECUTION_GATE_JSON__" = $ExecutionGateJson
        "__OUT_JSON__" = $PyResult
    }

    foreach ($k in $Replace.Keys) {
        $Py = $Py.Replace($k, ($Replace[$k] -replace "\\","\\"))
    }

    Set-Content -LiteralPath $PyScript -Value $Py -Encoding UTF8

    $python = Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($null -eq $python) { throw "python not found in PATH" }

    $psi = [System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo]::new()
    $psi.FileName = $python.Source
    $psi.Arguments = "`"$PyScript`""
    $psi.UseShellExecute = $false
    $psi.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
    $psi.RedirectStandardError = $true
    $psi.CreateNoWindow = $true

    $p = [System.Diagnostics.Process]::Start($psi)
    $null = $p.WaitForExit(180000)
    $stdout = $p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd()
    $stderr = $p.StandardError.ReadToEnd()

    if ($p.ExitCode -ne 0 -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PyResult)) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "FAIL" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "exit_code" "$($p.ExitCode)" $stderr $PyScript
        throw "Python failed: $stderr"
    }

    $Result = Get-Content -LiteralPath $PyResult -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $M = $Result.metrics

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "OK_EXECUTED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "stdout" "ok" $stdout $PyScript
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR" "PRESERVED_VALID_ROWS" "Preserved Valid Rows" "OK_PRESERVED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "rows" "$($M.preserved_valid_rows)" "Valid rows copied forward without mutation." $PreservedValidCsv

    if ([int]$M.repaired_rows -gt 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR" "REPAIRED_ROWS" "Repaired Invalid Rows" "OK_REPAIRED_PREVIEW_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "repaired_rows" "$($M.repaired_rows)" "Invalid rows repaired in preview." $RepairedCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR" "REPAIRED_ROWS" "Repaired Invalid Rows" "WARN_NO_REPAIR" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "repaired_rows" "0" "No invalid row repaired; manual mapping review required." $StillInvalidCsv
    }

    if ([int]$M.still_invalid_rows -gt 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR" "STILL_INVALID_ROWS" "Still Invalid Rows" "WARN_STILL_INVALID" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "still_invalid_rows" "$($M.still_invalid_rows)" "Some invalid rows remain; execution gate remains closed." $StillInvalidCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR" "STILL_INVALID_ROWS" "Still Invalid Rows" "OK_ZERO_INVALID" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "still_invalid_rows" "0" "All invalid rows repaired." $CombinedCsv
    }

    if ([int]$M.duplicate_after_repair -gt 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR" "DUPLICATE_AFTER_REPAIR" "Duplicate After Repair" "WARN_DUPLICATE_REVIEW" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "duplicate_after_repair" "$($M.duplicate_after_repair)" "Duplicates introduced after repair." $CombinedCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR" "DUPLICATE_AFTER_REPAIR" "Duplicate After Repair" "OK_ZERO_DUPLICATE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "duplicate_after_repair" "0" "No duplicate after repair." $CombinedCsv
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "REPAIR_AUDIT" "Invalid Mapping Repair Audit" "OK_AUDIT_CREATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "audit" "created" "Repair audit generated." $RepairAuditJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "EXECUTION_GATE" "Execution Gate Review" "OK_GATE_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "gate" "created" "Execution gate review generated; no DB write." $ExecutionGateJson

    $Top10Libs = @(
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Invalid Mapping Repair"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pandas; polars; pandera; pydantic; jsonschema; pytest; hypothesis; rapidfuzz; orjson; rich"; UseCase="invalid row repair/root-cause/preview validation"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Canonical Contract QA"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pandas; polars; duckdb; pyarrow; pandera; pydantic; pytest; hashlib; rapidfuzz; rich"; UseCase="key contract/check duplicates/lineage"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="PowerShell Orchestration"; Language="PowerShell"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Pester; PSScriptAnalyzer; Microsoft.PowerShell.ThreadJob; ImportExcel; PSWriteHTML; ScheduledTasks; Start-Transcript; ConvertTo-Json; Get-FileHash; Write-Progress"; UseCase="test/debug/report/audit/hash/progress"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="HTML UI Report"; Language="JS/HTML"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Tabulator; DataTables; ECharts; Chart.js; Mermaid; Ajv; DOMPurify; FileSaver.js; Playwright; Lighthouse"; UseCase="matrix/smoke/chart/schema/export"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="VRN Integration Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pdfplumber; PyMuPDF; pdfminer.six; pypdf; camelot; tabula-py; opencv-python; pillow; pandas; pyarrow"; UseCase="future PDF/report extraction alignment"}
    )
    $Top10Libs | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $Top10Json -Encoding UTF8
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "LOCAL_FREE_LIBS" "Top10 Local Free Libs" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P2" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "rows" "$(@($Top10Libs).Count)" "Top10 local free libs matrix generated." $Top10Json

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "RESULT" "READINESS" $Result.status $(if ($Result.risk -eq "LOW") { "OK_READY" } elseif ($Result.risk -eq "MEDIUM") { "WARN_REVIEW_READY" } else { "BLOCKED" }) $Result.risk "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_ONLY" "recommendation" $Result.recommendation "v027.8.4 completed. No DB write. No canonical merge." $PyResult

    $CsvMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_InvalidMappingRepairGate_Matrix_v02784.csv"
    $JsonMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_InvalidMappingRepairGate_Matrix_v02784.json"
    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    try {
        $ptr = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ActivePointer) {
            Get-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
        } else {
            [pscustomobject]@{}
        }

        $ptr | Add-Member -NotePropertyName "latest_akshare_invalid_mapping_repair_gate_v02784" -NotePropertyValue ([pscustomobject]@{
            generated_at = def_Iso
            status = $Result.status
            risk = $Result.risk
            run_root = $RunRoot
            result_json = $PyResult
            preserved_valid_csv = $PreservedValidCsv
            repaired_csv = $RepairedCsv
            still_invalid_csv = $StillInvalidCsv
            combined_csv = $CombinedCsv
            still_invalid_rows = $M.still_invalid_rows
            duplicate_after_repair = $M.duplicate_after_repair
            recommendation = $Result.recommendation
        }) -Force

        $ptr | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Encoding UTF8

PS7 function Invoke-VDF-AkShareCollisionResolverGate-v027841 {
    param(
        [string]$ProjectRoot = "C:\Users\tonyk\Downloads\VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics",
        [switch]$OpenHtmlReport = $true
    )

    $script:OK = 0
    $script:WARN = 0
    $script:FAIL = 0

    function def_Stamp { Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss" }
    function def_Iso { (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffK") }
    function def_Mkdir([string]$p) { [System.IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($p) | Out-Null }
    function def_Html([AllowNull()][string]$s) {
        if ($null -eq $s) { return "" }
        return [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode($s)
    }

    function def_AddRow {
        param(
            [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]$Rows,
            [string]$Round,[string]$Stage,[string]$Layer,[string]$Name,
            [string]$Status,[string]$Risk,[string]$FixClass,[string]$Priority,
            [string]$Mode,[string]$Metric,[string]$Value,[string]$Message,[string]$Path
        )

        if ($Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { $script:FAIL++ }
        elseif ($Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|MEDIUM|MISSING|EMPTY|QUARANTINE|COLLISION") { $script:WARN++ }
        else { $script:OK++ }

        $Rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Round=$Round; Stage=$Stage; Layer=$Layer; Name=$Name; Status=$Status; Risk=$Risk
            FixClass=$FixClass; Priority=$Priority; Mode=$Mode; Metric=$Metric; Value=$Value
            Message=$Message; Path=$Path
        }) | Out-Null
    }

    if (-not [System.IO.Directory]::Exists($ProjectRoot)) {
        throw "ProjectRoot not found: $ProjectRoot"
    }

    $Stamp = def_Stamp
    $DictRoot   = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "dict\VDF"
    $ActiveRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_active"
    $BackupRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_backup"

    foreach ($d in @($DictRoot,$ActiveRoot,$BackupRoot)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Latest = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ActiveRoot -Directory -Filter "VDF_AKSHARE_INVALID_MAPPING_REPAIR_GATE_v02784_*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $Latest) {
        throw "Latest v027.8.4 invalid mapping repair gate run not found."
    }

    $RunRoot = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_AKSHARE_COLLISION_RESOLVER_GATE_v027841_$Stamp"
    $ReportDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "report"
    $RegistryDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "registry"
    $PlanDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "plan"
    $PreviewDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "preview"
    $RuntimeDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "runtime"
    $BackupDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "backup"

    foreach ($d in @($RunRoot,$ReportDir,$RegistryDir,$PlanDir,$PreviewDir,$RuntimeDir,$BackupDir)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Rows = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    $ActivePointer = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json"
    $Transcript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "VDF_AkShare_CollisionResolverGate_Transcript_v027841_$Stamp.txt"

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "def VDF AKSHARE COLLISION RESOLVER GATE v027.8.4.1" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "VERITAS INTELLIGENCE ANALYTICS"
    Write-Host "Resolve duplicate collision · preserve original valid rows · no DATABASE write · no canonical merge"
    Write-Host ""

    try { Start-Transcript -LiteralPath $Transcript -Force | Out-Null } catch {}

    if ([System.IO.File]::Exists($ActivePointer)) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Destination (Join-Path $BackupDir "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER_before_v027841_$Stamp.json") -Force
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "BOOT" "PROJECT_ROOT" "VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "exists" "true" "Project root exists." $ProjectRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_DB_WRITE" "DATABASE" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "db_write_enabled" "false" "No DATABASE write." $DictRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_CANONICAL_MERGE" "Canonical Merge" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "canonical_merge_enabled" "false" "No canonical merge." $RunRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "PRESERVE_VALID_ROWS" "Original Valid Rows" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "preserve_original_valid" "true" "Original 120 valid rows are authoritative when collision occurs." $RunRoot

    $PreservedValidSource = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_InvalidRepair_PreservedValidRows_v02784.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    $RepairedSource = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_InvalidRepair_RepairedRows_v02784.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $PreservedValidSource) { throw "v027.8.4 preserved valid CSV not found." }
    if ($null -eq $RepairedSource) { throw "v027.8.4 repaired rows CSV not found." }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "PRESERVED_VALID" "v027.8.4 Preserved Valid Rows" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "source" "preserved_valid" "Locked preserved valid rows source." $PreservedValidSource.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "REPAIRED_ROWS" "v027.8.4 Repaired Rows" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "source" "repaired_rows" "Locked repaired rows source." $RepairedSource.FullName

    $PyScript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_collision_resolver_gate_v027841.py"
    $PyResult = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_collision_resolver_gate_result_v027841.json"

    $FinalValidCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_CollisionResolver_FinalValidRows_v027841.csv"
    $FinalValidJson = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_CollisionResolver_FinalValidRows_v027841.json"
    $CollisionCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_CollisionResolver_CollisionQuarantineRows_v027841.csv"
    $NonCollisionRepairedCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_CollisionResolver_NonCollisionRepairedRows_v027841.csv"
    $CollisionAuditJson = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_CollisionResolver_Audit_v027841.json"
    $RootCauseCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_CollisionResolver_RootCause_v027841.csv"
    $ExecutionGateJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_CollisionResolver_ExecutionGateReview_v027841.json"
    $Top10Json = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_Top10_LocalFreeLibs_CollisionResolverGate_v027841.json"

    $Py = @'
import json
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import pandas as pd

valid_source = Path(r"__VALID_SOURCE__")
repaired_source = Path(r"__REPAIRED_SOURCE__")
final_valid_csv = Path(r"__FINAL_VALID_CSV__")
final_valid_json = Path(r"__FINAL_VALID_JSON__")
collision_csv = Path(r"__COLLISION_CSV__")
non_collision_repaired_csv = Path(r"__NON_COLLISION_REPAIRED_CSV__")
collision_audit_json = Path(r"__COLLISION_AUDIT_JSON__")
rootcause_csv = Path(r"__ROOTCAUSE_CSV__")
execution_gate_json = Path(r"__EXECUTION_GATE_JSON__")
out_json = Path(r"__OUT_JSON__")

for p in [final_valid_csv, final_valid_json, collision_csv, non_collision_repaired_csv, collision_audit_json, rootcause_csv, execution_gate_json, out_json]:
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

def read_csv(p):
    try:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str, encoding="utf-8-sig").fillna("")
    except Exception:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str).fillna("")

valid = read_csv(valid_source)
repaired = read_csv(repaired_source)

for df in [valid, repaired]:
    for c in ["canonical_key", "is_valid", "invalid_reasons", "family", "symbol", "date", "source", "value"]:
        if c not in df.columns:
            df[c] = ""

valid["_vdf_v027841_source_partition"] = "preserved_valid_authoritative"
valid["_vdf_v027841_collision_policy"] = "keep"

valid_keys = set(valid["canonical_key"].astype(str).tolist())

if not repaired.empty:
    collision = repaired[repaired["canonical_key"].astype(str).isin(valid_keys)].copy()
    non_collision = repaired[~repaired["canonical_key"].astype(str).isin(valid_keys)].copy()
else:
    collision = pd.DataFrame(columns=repaired.columns)
    non_collision = pd.DataFrame(columns=repaired.columns)

if not collision.empty:
    collision["_vdf_v027841_source_partition"] = "repaired_collision_quarantine"
    collision["_vdf_v027841_collision_policy"] = "quarantine_repaired_keep_preserved_valid"
    collision["_vdf_v027841_collision_reason"] = "canonical_key_exists_in_preserved_valid"

if not non_collision.empty:
    non_collision["_vdf_v027841_source_partition"] = "repaired_non_collision"
    non_collision["_vdf_v027841_collision_policy"] = "include_preview_only"
    non_collision["_vdf_v027841_collision_reason"] = ""

final_valid = pd.concat([valid, non_collision], ignore_index=True, sort=False)

if not final_valid.empty:
    dup_after = int(final_valid.duplicated(subset=["canonical_key"], keep=False).sum())
else:
    dup_after = 0

final_valid.to_csv(final_valid_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
final_valid.to_json(final_valid_json, orient="records", force_ascii=False, indent=2)
collision.to_csv(collision_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
non_collision.to_csv(non_collision_repaired_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

rootcause = [
    {"cause": "repaired_rows_collision_with_preserved_valid", "count": int(len(collision))},
    {"cause": "repaired_rows_non_collision", "count": int(len(non_collision))},
    {"cause": "final_valid_duplicate_after_collision_policy", "count": dup_after}
]
pd.DataFrame(rootcause).to_csv(rootcause_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

metrics = {
    "preserved_valid_rows": int(len(valid)),
    "input_repaired_rows": int(len(repaired)),
    "collision_quarantine_rows": int(len(collision)),
    "non_collision_repaired_rows": int(len(non_collision)),
    "final_valid_rows": int(len(final_valid)),
    "duplicate_after_collision_policy": dup_after,
    "still_invalid_rows": 0
}

if metrics["duplicate_after_collision_policy"] == 0 and metrics["final_valid_rows"] > 0:
    if metrics["collision_quarantine_rows"] == 0:
        status = "VDF_AKSHARE_COLLISION_RESOLVER_GATE_READY"
        risk = "LOW"
        recommendation = "ALLOW_V0279_EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY_NO_DB_WRITE"
        next_step = "v027.9 execution readiness review only; no DB write"
    else:
        status = "VDF_AKSHARE_COLLISION_RESOLVER_GATE_READY_WITH_COLLISION_QUARANTINE"
        risk = "MEDIUM"
        recommendation = "ALLOW_V0279_EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_WITH_COLLISION_QUARANTINE_NO_DB_WRITE"
        next_step = "v027.9 execution readiness review with collision quarantine; no DB write"
else:
    status = "VDF_AKSHARE_COLLISION_RESOLVER_GATE_BLOCKED"
    risk = "HIGH"
    recommendation = "BLOCK_V0279_UNTIL_DUPLICATE_REVIEW"
    next_step = "v027.8.4.2 duplicate repair review only; no DB write"

audit = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "policy": {
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
        "preserved_valid_authoritative": True,
        "collision_rows_quarantined": True,
        "repaired_collision_rows_not_included_in_final_valid": True
    },
    "metrics": metrics,
    "artifacts": {
        "final_valid_csv": str(final_valid_csv),
        "final_valid_json": str(final_valid_json),
        "collision_quarantine_csv": str(collision_csv),
        "non_collision_repaired_csv": str(non_collision_repaired_csv),
        "rootcause_csv": str(rootcause_csv)
    },
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "next_step": next_step
}
collision_audit_json.write_text(json.dumps(audit, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

execution_gate = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": "EXECUTION_GATE_READY_FOR_REVIEW_WITH_COLLISION_QUARANTINE" if risk in ["LOW","MEDIUM"] else "EXECUTION_GATE_CLOSED_DUPLICATE_REVIEW",
    "risk": risk,
    "allowed_to_write_database": False,
    "allowed_to_merge_canonical": False,
    "metrics": metrics,
    "hard_stop": [
        "No DB write in v027.8.4.1",
        "No canonical merge in v027.8.4.1",
        "v027.9 is review only unless explicitly approved",
        "Collision quarantine rows must not be written into canonical table"
    ],
    "recommendation": recommendation
}
execution_gate_json.write_text(json.dumps(execution_gate, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

result = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "metrics": metrics,
    "artifacts": audit["artifacts"] | {
        "collision_audit_json": str(collision_audit_json),
        "execution_gate_json": str(execution_gate_json)
    },
    "next_step": next_step
}
out_json.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
'@

    $Replace = @{
        "__VALID_SOURCE__" = $PreservedValidSource.FullName
        "__REPAIRED_SOURCE__" = $RepairedSource.FullName
        "__FINAL_VALID_CSV__" = $FinalValidCsv
        "__FINAL_VALID_JSON__" = $FinalValidJson
        "__COLLISION_CSV__" = $CollisionCsv
        "__NON_COLLISION_REPAIRED_CSV__" = $NonCollisionRepairedCsv
        "__COLLISION_AUDIT_JSON__" = $CollisionAuditJson
        "__ROOTCAUSE_CSV__" = $RootCauseCsv
        "__EXECUTION_GATE_JSON__" = $ExecutionGateJson
        "__OUT_JSON__" = $PyResult
    }

    foreach ($k in $Replace.Keys) {
        $Py = $Py.Replace($k, ($Replace[$k] -replace "\\","\\"))
    }

    Set-Content -LiteralPath $PyScript -Value $Py -Encoding UTF8

    $python = Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($null -eq $python) { throw "python not found in PATH" }

    $psi = [System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo]::new()
    $psi.FileName = $python.Source
    $psi.Arguments = "`"$PyScript`""
    $psi.UseShellExecute = $false
    $psi.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
    $psi.RedirectStandardError = $true
    $psi.CreateNoWindow = $true

    $p = [System.Diagnostics.Process]::Start($psi)
    $null = $p.WaitForExit(180000)
    $stdout = $p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd()
    $stderr = $p.StandardError.ReadToEnd()

    if ($p.ExitCode -ne 0 -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PyResult)) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "COLLISION_RESOLVER" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "FAIL" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "exit_code" "$($p.ExitCode)" $stderr $PyScript
        throw "Python failed: $stderr"
    }

    $Result = Get-Content -LiteralPath $PyResult -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $M = $Result.metrics

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "COLLISION_RESOLVER" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "OK_EXECUTED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "stdout" "ok" $stdout $PyScript
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "COLLISION_RESOLVER" "PRESERVED_VALID" "Preserved Valid Rows" "OK_AUTHORITATIVE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "preserved_valid_rows" "$($M.preserved_valid_rows)" "Preserved valid rows kept authoritative." $PreservedValidSource.FullName

    if ([int]$M.collision_quarantine_rows -gt 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "COLLISION_RESOLVER" "COLLISION_QUARANTINE" "Collision Rows" "WARN_COLLISION_QUARANTINED" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "collision_quarantine_rows" "$($M.collision_quarantine_rows)" "Repaired collision rows quarantined; not included in final valid set." $CollisionCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "COLLISION_RESOLVER" "COLLISION_QUARANTINE" "Collision Rows" "OK_ZERO_COLLISION" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "collision_quarantine_rows" "0" "No repaired collision rows." $FinalValidCsv
    }

    if ([int]$M.duplicate_after_collision_policy -eq 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "COLLISION_RESOLVER" "DUPLICATE_AFTER_POLICY" "Duplicate After Collision Policy" "OK_ZERO_DUPLICATE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "duplicate_after_collision_policy" "0" "No duplicate remains in final valid set." $FinalValidCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "COLLISION_RESOLVER" "DUPLICATE_AFTER_POLICY" "Duplicate After Collision Policy" "BLOCK_DUPLICATE_REMAINS" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "duplicate_after_collision_policy" "$($M.duplicate_after_collision_policy)" "Duplicate remains after collision policy." $FinalValidCsv
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "COLLISION_AUDIT" "Collision Audit" "OK_AUDIT_CREATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "audit" "created" "Collision audit generated." $CollisionAuditJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "EXECUTION_GATE" "Execution Gate Review" "OK_GATE_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "gate" "created" "Execution gate review generated; no DB write." $ExecutionGateJson

    $Top10Libs = @(
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Collision Resolver"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pandas; polars; duckdb; pyarrow; hashlib; pandera; pydantic; pytest; rapidfuzz; rich"; UseCase="key collision isolation/final valid preview/quarantine"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Execution Gate QA"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pandas; polars; duckdb; pyarrow; pandera; pydantic; jsonschema; pytest; orjson; rich"; UseCase="readiness review/schema gate/no-write audit"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="PowerShell Orchestration"; Language="PowerShell"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Pester; PSScriptAnalyzer; Microsoft.PowerShell.ThreadJob; ImportExcel; PSWriteHTML; ScheduledTasks; Start-Transcript; ConvertTo-Json; Get-FileHash; Write-Progress"; UseCase="test/debug/report/audit/hash/progress"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="HTML UI Report"; Language="JS/HTML"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Tabulator; DataTables; ECharts; Chart.js; Mermaid; Ajv; DOMPurify; FileSaver.js; Playwright; Lighthouse"; UseCase="matrix/smoke/chart/schema/export"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="VRN Integration Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pdfplumber; PyMuPDF; pdfminer.six; pypdf; camelot; tabula-py; opencv-python; pillow; pandas; pyarrow"; UseCase="future PDF/report extraction alignment"}
    )
    $Top10Libs | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $Top10Json -Encoding UTF8

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "LOCAL_FREE_LIBS" "Top10 Local Free Libs" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P2" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "rows" "$(@($Top10Libs).Count)" "Top10 local free libs matrix generated." $Top10Json
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "RESULT" "READINESS" $Result.status $(if ($Result.risk -eq "LOW") { "OK_READY" } elseif ($Result.risk -eq "MEDIUM") { "WARN_REVIEW_READY" } else { "BLOCKED" }) $Result.risk "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "recommendation" $Result.recommendation "v027.8.4.1 completed. No DB write. No canonical merge." $PyResult

        def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "POINTER" "ACTIVE_POINTER" "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json" "OK_UPDATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "COLLISION_RESOLVER_ONLY" "update" "complete" "Active pointer updated with v027.8.4.1." $ActivePointer
    } catch {}

    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM

    $trs = foreach ($r in $Rows) {
        $cls = if ($r.Risk -eq "HIGH" -or $r.Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { "bad" }
