PS7 function Invoke-VDF-AkShareDuplicateRepairReviewGate-v027842 {
    param(
        [string]$ProjectRoot = "C:\Users\tonyk\Downloads\VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics",
        [switch]$OpenHtmlReport = $true
    )

    $script:OK = 0
    $script:WARN = 0
    $script:FAIL = 0

    function def_Stamp { Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss" }
    function def_Iso { (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffK") }
    function def_Mkdir([string]$p) { [System.IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($p) | Out-Null }
    function def_Html([AllowNull()][string]$s) {
        if ($null -eq $s) { return "" }
        return [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode($s)
    }

    function def_AddRow {
        param(
            [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]$Rows,
            [string]$Round,[string]$Stage,[string]$Layer,[string]$Name,
            [string]$Status,[string]$Risk,[string]$FixClass,[string]$Priority,
            [string]$Mode,[string]$Metric,[string]$Value,[string]$Message,[string]$Path
        )

        if ($Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { $script:FAIL++ }
        elseif ($Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|MEDIUM|MISSING|EMPTY|QUARANTINE|DUPLICATE") { $script:WARN++ }
        else { $script:OK++ }

        $Rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Round=$Round; Stage=$Stage; Layer=$Layer; Name=$Name; Status=$Status; Risk=$Risk
            FixClass=$FixClass; Priority=$Priority; Mode=$Mode; Metric=$Metric; Value=$Value
            Message=$Message; Path=$Path
        }) | Out-Null
    }

    if (-not [System.IO.Directory]::Exists($ProjectRoot)) {
        throw "ProjectRoot not found: $ProjectRoot"
    }

    $Stamp = def_Stamp
    $DictRoot   = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "dict\VDF"
    $ActiveRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_active"
    $BackupRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_backup"

    foreach ($d in @($DictRoot,$ActiveRoot,$BackupRoot)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Latest = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ActiveRoot -Directory -Filter "VDF_AKSHARE_COLLISION_RESOLVER_GATE_v027841_*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $Latest) {
        throw "Latest v027.8.4.1 collision resolver run not found."
    }

    $RunRoot = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_AKSHARE_DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_GATE_v027842_$Stamp"
    $ReportDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "report"
    $RegistryDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "registry"
    $PlanDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "plan"
    $PreviewDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "preview"
    $RuntimeDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "runtime"
    $BackupDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "backup"

    foreach ($d in @($RunRoot,$ReportDir,$RegistryDir,$PlanDir,$PreviewDir,$RuntimeDir,$BackupDir)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Rows = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    $ActivePointer = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json"
    $Transcript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "VDF_AkShare_DuplicateRepairReviewGate_Transcript_v027842_$Stamp.txt"

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "def VDF AKSHARE DUPLICATE REPAIR REVIEW GATE v027.8.4.2" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "VERITAS INTELLIGENCE ANALYTICS"
    Write-Host "Final-valid duplicate review · duplicate quarantine · no DATABASE write · no canonical merge"
    Write-Host ""

    try { Start-Transcript -LiteralPath $Transcript -Force | Out-Null } catch {}

    if ([System.IO.File]::Exists($ActivePointer)) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Destination (Join-Path $BackupDir "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER_before_v027842_$Stamp.json") -Force
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "BOOT" "PROJECT_ROOT" "VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "exists" "true" "Project root exists." $ProjectRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_DB_WRITE" "DATABASE" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "db_write_enabled" "false" "No DATABASE write." $DictRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_CANONICAL_MERGE" "Canonical Merge" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "canonical_merge_enabled" "false" "No canonical merge." $RunRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "QUARANTINE_DUPLICATES" "Duplicate Policy" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "policy" "keep_first_quarantine_rest" "Keep first row per canonical_key; quarantine duplicate rest." $RunRoot

    $FinalValidSource = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_CollisionResolver_FinalValidRows_v027841.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $FinalValidSource) {
        throw "v027.8.4.1 final valid CSV not found."
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "FINAL_VALID" "v027.8.4.1 Final Valid Rows" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "source" "final_valid_rows" "Locked v027.8.4.1 final valid source." $FinalValidSource.FullName

    $PyScript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_duplicate_repair_review_gate_v027842.py"
    $PyResult = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_duplicate_repair_review_gate_result_v027842.json"

    $UniqueFinalCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_DuplicateRepair_UniqueFinalRows_v027842.csv"
    $UniqueFinalJson = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_DuplicateRepair_UniqueFinalRows_v027842.json"
    $DuplicateQuarantineCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_DuplicateRepair_DuplicateQuarantineRows_v027842.csv"
    $DuplicateGroupsCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_DuplicateRepair_DuplicateGroups_v027842.csv"
    $DuplicateAuditJson = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_DuplicateRepair_Audit_v027842.json"
    $ExecutionGateJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_DuplicateRepair_ExecutionGateReview_v027842.json"
    $Top10Json = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_Top10_LocalFreeLibs_DuplicateRepairReviewGate_v027842.json"

    $Py = @'
import json
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import pandas as pd

source_csv = Path(r"__SOURCE_CSV__")
unique_final_csv = Path(r"__UNIQUE_FINAL_CSV__")
unique_final_json = Path(r"__UNIQUE_FINAL_JSON__")
duplicate_quarantine_csv = Path(r"__DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE_CSV__")
duplicate_groups_csv = Path(r"__DUPLICATE_GROUPS_CSV__")
duplicate_audit_json = Path(r"__DUPLICATE_AUDIT_JSON__")
execution_gate_json = Path(r"__EXECUTION_GATE_JSON__")
out_json = Path(r"__OUT_JSON__")

for p in [unique_final_csv, unique_final_json, duplicate_quarantine_csv, duplicate_groups_csv, duplicate_audit_json, execution_gate_json, out_json]:
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

def read_csv(p):
    try:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str, encoding="utf-8-sig").fillna("")
    except Exception:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str).fillna("")

df = read_csv(source_csv)

for c in ["canonical_key","family","symbol","date","source","value"]:
    if c not in df.columns:
        df[c] = ""

df["_vdf_v027842_original_order"] = range(1, len(df) + 1)

def priority(row):
    part = str(row.get("_vdf_v027841_source_partition", ""))
    repair_status = str(row.get("_vdf_v02784_repair_status", ""))
    policy = str(row.get("_vdf_v027841_collision_policy", ""))

    # Preserve original valid rows over repaired rows.
    if "preserved_valid" in part:
        return 100
    if "preserved" in repair_status:
        return 90
    if "repaired" in repair_status:
        return 50
    if "include_preview" in policy:
        return 40
    return 10

df["_vdf_v027842_keep_priority"] = df.apply(priority, axis=1)

# Sort so authoritative rows come first per key.
sort_cols = ["canonical_key", "_vdf_v027842_keep_priority", "_vdf_v027842_original_order"]
df_sorted = df.sort_values(
    by=sort_cols,
    ascending=[True, False, True],
    kind="mergesort"
).copy()

dup_group_sizes = df_sorted.groupby("canonical_key", dropna=False).size().reset_index(name="group_count")
dup_groups = dup_group_sizes[dup_group_sizes["group_count"] > 1].copy()

df_sorted["_vdf_v027842_is_duplicate_group"] = df_sorted["canonical_key"].isin(set(dup_groups["canonical_key"].astype(str)))
df_sorted["_vdf_v027842_rank_in_key"] = df_sorted.groupby("canonical_key").cumcount() + 1

unique_final = df_sorted[df_sorted["_vdf_v027842_rank_in_key"] == 1].copy()
duplicate_quarantine = df_sorted[df_sorted["_vdf_v027842_rank_in_key"] > 1].copy()

unique_final["_vdf_v027842_duplicate_policy"] = "keep_authoritative_first_per_canonical_key"
duplicate_quarantine["_vdf_v027842_duplicate_policy"] = "duplicate_quarantine_not_for_canonical_write"

duplicate_after = int(unique_final.duplicated(subset=["canonical_key"], keep=False).sum()) if not unique_final.empty else 0

unique_final.to_csv(unique_final_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
unique_final.to_json(unique_final_json, orient="records", force_ascii=False, indent=2)
duplicate_quarantine.to_csv(duplicate_quarantine_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
dup_groups.to_csv(duplicate_groups_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

metrics = {
    "input_rows": int(len(df)),
    "duplicate_group_count": int(len(dup_groups)),
    "duplicate_quarantine_rows": int(len(duplicate_quarantine)),
    "unique_final_rows": int(len(unique_final)),
    "duplicate_after_repair_review": duplicate_after,
    "still_invalid_rows": int((unique_final.get("is_valid", pd.Series(dtype=str)).astype(str).str.lower().isin(["false","0","no"])).sum()) if "is_valid" in unique_final.columns else 0
}

if metrics["unique_final_rows"] > 0 and metrics["duplicate_after_repair_review"] == 0 and metrics["still_invalid_rows"] == 0:
    if metrics["duplicate_quarantine_rows"] == 0:
        status = "VDF_AKSHARE_DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_GATE_READY"
        risk = "LOW"
        recommendation = "ALLOW_V0279_EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY_NO_DB_WRITE"
        next_step = "v027.9 execution readiness review only; no DB write"
    else:
        status = "VDF_AKSHARE_DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_GATE_READY_WITH_DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE"
        risk = "MEDIUM"
        recommendation = "ALLOW_V0279_EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_WITH_DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE_NO_DB_WRITE"
        next_step = "v027.9 execution readiness review with duplicate quarantine; no DB write"
else:
    status = "VDF_AKSHARE_DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_GATE_BLOCKED"
    risk = "HIGH"
    recommendation = "BLOCK_V0279_UNTIL_FINAL_VALID_REVIEW"
    next_step = "v027.8.4.3 final valid review only; no DB write"

audit = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "policy": {
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
        "keep_first_per_canonical_key": True,
        "preserved_valid_priority": True,
        "duplicate_rows_quarantined": True
    },
    "metrics": metrics,
    "artifacts": {
        "unique_final_csv": str(unique_final_csv),
        "unique_final_json": str(unique_final_json),
        "duplicate_quarantine_csv": str(duplicate_quarantine_csv),
        "duplicate_groups_csv": str(duplicate_groups_csv)
    },
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "next_step": next_step
}
duplicate_audit_json.write_text(json.dumps(audit, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

execution_gate = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": "EXECUTION_GATE_READY_FOR_REVIEW_WITH_DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE" if risk in ["LOW","MEDIUM"] else "EXECUTION_GATE_CLOSED_FINAL_VALID_REVIEW",
    "risk": risk,
    "allowed_to_write_database": False,
    "allowed_to_merge_canonical": False,
    "metrics": metrics,
    "hard_stop": [
        "No DB write in v027.8.4.2",
        "No canonical merge in v027.8.4.2",
        "v027.9 is review only unless explicitly approved",
        "Duplicate quarantine rows must not be written into canonical table"
    ],
    "recommendation": recommendation
}
execution_gate_json.write_text(json.dumps(execution_gate, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

result = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "metrics": metrics,
    "artifacts": audit["artifacts"] | {
        "duplicate_audit_json": str(duplicate_audit_json),
        "execution_gate_json": str(execution_gate_json)
    },
    "next_step": next_step
}
out_json.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
'@

    $Replace = @{
        "__SOURCE_CSV__" = $FinalValidSource.FullName
        "__UNIQUE_FINAL_CSV__" = $UniqueFinalCsv
        "__UNIQUE_FINAL_JSON__" = $UniqueFinalJson
        "__DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE_CSV__" = $DuplicateQuarantineCsv
        "__DUPLICATE_GROUPS_CSV__" = $DuplicateGroupsCsv
        "__DUPLICATE_AUDIT_JSON__" = $DuplicateAuditJson
        "__EXECUTION_GATE_JSON__" = $ExecutionGateJson
        "__OUT_JSON__" = $PyResult
    }

    foreach ($k in $Replace.Keys) {
        $Py = $Py.Replace($k, ($Replace[$k] -replace "\\","\\"))
    }

    Set-Content -LiteralPath $PyScript -Value $Py -Encoding UTF8

    $python = Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($null -eq $python) { throw "python not found in PATH" }

    $psi = [System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo]::new()
    $psi.FileName = $python.Source
    $psi.Arguments = "`"$PyScript`""
    $psi.UseShellExecute = $false
    $psi.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
    $psi.RedirectStandardError = $true
    $psi.CreateNoWindow = $true

    $p = [System.Diagnostics.Process]::Start($psi)
    $null = $p.WaitForExit(180000)
    $stdout = $p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd()
    $stderr = $p.StandardError.ReadToEnd()

    if ($p.ExitCode -ne 0 -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PyResult)) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "FAIL" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "exit_code" "$($p.ExitCode)" $stderr $PyScript
        throw "Python failed: $stderr"
    }

    $Result = Get-Content -LiteralPath $PyResult -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $M = $Result.metrics

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "OK_EXECUTED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "stdout" "ok" $stdout $PyScript
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW" "UNIQUE_FINAL" "Unique Final Rows" "OK_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "unique_final_rows" "$($M.unique_final_rows)" "Unique final rows generated by canonical_key." $UniqueFinalCsv

    if ([int]$M.duplicate_quarantine_rows -gt 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW" "DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE" "Duplicate Quarantine Rows" "WARN_DUPLICATE_QUARANTINED" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "duplicate_quarantine_rows" "$($M.duplicate_quarantine_rows)" "Duplicate rows quarantined; not canonical-write eligible." $DuplicateQuarantineCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW" "DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE" "Duplicate Quarantine Rows" "OK_ZERO_DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "duplicate_quarantine_rows" "0" "No duplicate rows quarantined." $UniqueFinalCsv
    }

    if ([int]$M.duplicate_after_repair_review -eq 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW" "DUPLICATE_AFTER_REVIEW" "Duplicate After Review" "OK_ZERO_DUPLICATE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "duplicate_after_repair_review" "0" "No duplicate remains in unique final rows." $UniqueFinalCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW" "DUPLICATE_AFTER_REVIEW" "BLOCK_DUPLICATE_REMAINS" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "duplicate_after_repair_review" "$($M.duplicate_after_repair_review)" "Duplicate remains after repair review." $UniqueFinalCsv
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "DUPLICATE_AUDIT" "Duplicate Audit" "OK_AUDIT_CREATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "audit" "created" "Duplicate repair audit generated." $DuplicateAuditJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "EXECUTION_GATE" "Execution Gate Review" "OK_GATE_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "gate" "created" "Execution gate review generated; no DB write." $ExecutionGateJson

    $Top10Libs = @(
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Duplicate Repair Review"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pandas; polars; duckdb; pyarrow; hashlib; pandera; pydantic; pytest; rapidfuzz; rich"; UseCase="canonical key unique set/duplicate quarantine"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Execution Readiness Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pandas; polars; duckdb; pyarrow; pandera; pydantic; jsonschema; pytest; orjson; rich"; UseCase="review-only readiness/schema gate/no-write audit"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="PowerShell Orchestration"; Language="PowerShell"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Pester; PSScriptAnalyzer; Microsoft.PowerShell.ThreadJob; ImportExcel; PSWriteHTML; ScheduledTasks; Start-Transcript; ConvertTo-Json; Get-FileHash; Write-Progress"; UseCase="test/debug/report/audit/hash/progress"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="HTML UI Report"; Language="JS/HTML"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Tabulator; DataTables; ECharts; Chart.js; Mermaid; Ajv; DOMPurify; FileSaver.js; Playwright; Lighthouse"; UseCase="matrix/smoke/chart/schema/export"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="VRN Integration Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pdfplumber; PyMuPDF; pdfminer.six; pypdf; camelot; tabula-py; opencv-python; pillow; pandas; pyarrow"; UseCase="future PDF/report extraction alignment"}
    )
    $Top10Libs | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $Top10Json -Encoding UTF8

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "LOCAL_FREE_LIBS" "Top10 Local Free Libs" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P2" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "rows" "$(@($Top10Libs).Count)" "Top10 local free libs matrix generated." $Top10Json
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "RESULT" "READINESS" $Result.status $(if ($Result.risk -eq "LOW") { "OK_READY" } elseif ($Result.risk -eq "MEDIUM") { "WARN_REVIEW_READY" } else { "BLOCKED" }) $Result.risk "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "recommendation" $Result.recommendation "v027.8.4.2 completed. No DB write. No canonical merge." $PyResult

    $CsvMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_DuplicateRepairReviewGate_Matrix_v027842.csv"
    $JsonMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_DuplicateRepairReviewGate_Matrix_v027842.json"
    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    try {
        $ptr = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ActivePointer) {
            Get-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
        } else {
            [pscustomobject]@{}
        }

        $ptr | Add-Member -NotePropertyName "latest_akshare_duplicate_repair_review_gate_v027842" -NotePropertyValue ([pscustomobject]@{
            generated_at = def_Iso
            status = $Result.status
            risk = $Result.risk
            run_root = $RunRoot
            result_json = $PyResult
            unique_final_csv = $UniqueFinalCsv
            duplicate_quarantine_csv = $DuplicateQuarantineCsv
            unique_final_rows = $M.unique_final_rows
            duplicate_quarantine_rows = $M.duplicate_quarantine_rows
            duplicate_after_repair_review = $M.duplicate_after_repair_review
            recommendation = $Result.recommendation
        }) -Force

        $ptr | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Encoding UTF8
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "POINTER" "ACTIVE_POINTER" "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json" "OK_UPDATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_ONLY" "update" "complete" "Active pointer updated with v027.8.4.2." $ActivePointer
    } catch {}

    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    $trs = foreach ($r in $Rows) {
        $cls = if ($r.Risk -eq "HIGH" -or $r.Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { "bad" }
               elseif ($r.Risk -match "MEDIUM|REVIEW" -or $r.Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|DUPLICATE|QUARANTINE") { "warn" }
               else { "ok" }
        "<tr class='$cls'><td>$(def_Html $r.Round)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Stage)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Layer)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Name)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Status)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Risk)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.FixClass)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Priority)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Mode)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Metric)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Value)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Message)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Path)</td></tr>"
    }

    $HtmlReport = Join-Path $ReportDir "VDF_AkShare_DuplicateRepairReviewGate_Report_v027842.html"
    $Html = @"
<!doctype html>
<html lang="zh-Hant">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
<title>VDF AkShare Duplicate Repair Review Gate v027.8.4.2</title>
<style>
body{margin:0;background:#f7f4ed;color:#25312f;font-family:Arial,"Microsoft JhengHei",sans-serif}
.wrap{max-width:1500px;margin:0 auto;padding:28px}
.hero{border:1px solid #d8d0c3;border-radius:24px;background:#fffdf8;padding:26px;box-shadow:0 18px 50px rgba(30,40,36,.08)}
h1{margin:0;font-size:28px}h2{margin-top:28px;font-size:20px}.sub{color:#6e7b78;margin-top:8px;line-height:1.7}
.kpis{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(6,minmax(130px,1fr));gap:12px;margin-top:20px}

PS7 function Invoke-VDF-AkShareExecutionReadinessReview-v0279 {
    param(
        [string]$ProjectRoot = "C:\Users\tonyk\Downloads\VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics",
        [switch]$OpenHtmlReport = $true
    )

    $script:OK = 0
    $script:WARN = 0
    $script:FAIL = 0

    function def_Stamp { Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss" }
    function def_Iso { (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffK") }
    function def_Mkdir([string]$p) { [System.IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($p) | Out-Null }
    function def_Html([AllowNull()][string]$s) {
        if ($null -eq $s) { return "" }
        return [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode($s)
    }

    function def_AddRow {
        param(
            [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]$Rows,
            [string]$Round,[string]$Stage,[string]$Layer,[string]$Name,
            [string]$Status,[string]$Risk,[string]$FixClass,[string]$Priority,
            [string]$Mode,[string]$Metric,[string]$Value,[string]$Message,[string]$Path
        )

        if ($Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { $script:FAIL++ }
        elseif ($Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|MEDIUM|MISSING|EMPTY|QUARANTINE|PARTIAL") { $script:WARN++ }
        else { $script:OK++ }

        $Rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Round=$Round; Stage=$Stage; Layer=$Layer; Name=$Name; Status=$Status; Risk=$Risk
            FixClass=$FixClass; Priority=$Priority; Mode=$Mode; Metric=$Metric; Value=$Value
            Message=$Message; Path=$Path
        }) | Out-Null
    }

    function def_SafeHash {
        param([string]$Path,[string]$ExcludeRoot)
        try {
            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Path) -or -not [System.IO.File]::Exists($Path)) { return "" }
            $full = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Path)
            $root = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($ExcludeRoot)
            if ($full.StartsWith($root,[System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { return "SKIPPED_CURRENT_RUNROOT" }
            return (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256 -ErrorAction Stop).Hash
        } catch [System.IO.IOException] {
            return "LOCKED_OR_BUSY"
        } catch {
            return "HASH_ERROR"
        }
    }

    if (-not [System.IO.Directory]::Exists($ProjectRoot)) {
        throw "ProjectRoot not found: $ProjectRoot"
    }

    $Stamp = def_Stamp
    $DictRoot   = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "dict\VDF"
    $ActiveRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_active"
    $FreezeRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_freeze"
    $BackupRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_backup"

    foreach ($d in @($DictRoot,$ActiveRoot,$FreezeRoot,$BackupRoot)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Latest = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ActiveRoot -Directory -Filter "VDF_AKSHARE_DUPLICATE_REPAIR_REVIEW_GATE_v027842_*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $Latest) {
        throw "Latest v027.8.4.2 duplicate repair review gate run not found."
    }

    $RunRoot = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_AKSHARE_EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_v0279_$Stamp"
    $ReportDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "report"
    $RegistryDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "registry"
    $PlanDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "plan"
    $PreviewDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "preview"
    $RuntimeDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "runtime"
    $BackupDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "backup"

    foreach ($d in @($RunRoot,$ReportDir,$RegistryDir,$PlanDir,$PreviewDir,$RuntimeDir,$BackupDir)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Rows = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    $ActivePointer = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json"
    $Transcript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "VDF_AkShare_ExecutionReadinessReview_Transcript_v0279_$Stamp.txt"

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "def VDF AKSHARE EXECUTION READINESS REVIEW v027.9" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "VERITAS INTELLIGENCE ANALYTICS"
    Write-Host "Review only · unique final + duplicate quarantine · no DATABASE write · no canonical merge"
    Write-Host ""

    try { Start-Transcript -LiteralPath $Transcript -Force | Out-Null } catch {}

    if ([System.IO.File]::Exists($ActivePointer)) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Destination (Join-Path $BackupDir "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER_before_v0279_$Stamp.json") -Force
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "BOOT" "PROJECT_ROOT" "VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "exists" "true" "Project root exists." $ProjectRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_DB_WRITE" "DATABASE" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "db_write_enabled" "false" "No DATABASE write in v027.9." $DictRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_CANONICAL_MERGE" "Canonical Merge" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "canonical_merge_enabled" "false" "No canonical merge in v027.9." $RunRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "REVIEW_ONLY" "Execution Readiness" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "write_permission" "false" "This stage can only produce review seal; no execution." $RunRoot

    $UniqueFinalSource = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_DuplicateRepair_UniqueFinalRows_v027842.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    $DuplicateQuarantineSource = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_DuplicateRepair_DuplicateQuarantineRows_v027842.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    $DuplicateAuditSource = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_DuplicateRepair_Audit_v027842.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $UniqueFinalSource) { throw "v027.8.4.2 unique final CSV not found." }
    if ($null -eq $DuplicateQuarantineSource) { throw "v027.8.4.2 duplicate quarantine CSV not found." }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "UNIQUE_FINAL" "v027.8.4.2 Unique Final Rows" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "source" "unique_final_rows" "Locked unique final rows source." $UniqueFinalSource.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE" "v027.8.4.2 Duplicate Quarantine" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "source" "duplicate_quarantine" "Locked duplicate quarantine source." $DuplicateQuarantineSource.FullName

    $PyScript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_execution_readiness_review_v0279.py"
    $PyResult = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_execution_readiness_review_result_v0279.json"

    $ReadinessFinalCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_v0279_ReadinessFinalRows.csv"
    $ReadinessFinalJson = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_v0279_ReadinessFinalRows.json"
    $QuarantineCopyCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_v0279_DuplicateQuarantineRows.csv"
    $SchemaAuditCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v0279_SchemaAudit.csv"
    $KeyAuditCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v0279_KeyAudit.csv"
    $ReadinessSealJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0279_ExecutionReadinessSeal.json"
    $NextPlanJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0280_NextActionPlan.json"
    $Top10Json = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_Top10_LocalFreeLibs_ExecutionReadinessReview_v0279.json"

    $Py = @'
import json, hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import pandas as pd

unique_source = Path(r"__UNIQUE_SOURCE__")
quarantine_source = Path(r"__QUARANTINE_SOURCE__")
final_csv = Path(r"__FINAL_CSV__")
final_json = Path(r"__FINAL_JSON__")
quarantine_copy_csv = Path(r"__QUARANTINE_COPY_CSV__")
schema_audit_csv = Path(r"__SCHEMA_AUDIT_CSV__")
key_audit_csv = Path(r"__KEY_AUDIT_CSV__")
readiness_seal_json = Path(r"__READINESS_SEAL_JSON__")
next_plan_json = Path(r"__NEXT_PLAN_JSON__")
out_json = Path(r"__OUT_JSON__")

for p in [final_csv, final_json, quarantine_copy_csv, schema_audit_csv, key_audit_csv, readiness_seal_json, next_plan_json, out_json]:
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

def read_csv(p):
    try:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str, encoding="utf-8-sig").fillna("")
    except Exception:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str).fillna("")

def sha256_file(p):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(p, "rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()

df = read_csv(unique_source)
q = read_csv(quarantine_source)

required = ["family","symbol","date","value","source","canonical_key","is_valid","invalid_reasons"]

for c in required:
    if c not in df.columns:
        df[c] = ""

schema_rows = []
for c in required:
    blank_count = int((df[c].astype(str).str.strip() == "").sum()) if c in df.columns else len(df)
    schema_rows.append({
        "field": c,
        "exists": c in df.columns,
        "blank_count": blank_count,
        "row_count": int(len(df)),
        "status": "OK" if c in df.columns and blank_count == 0 and c != "invalid_reasons" else ("OK_OPTIONAL_BLANK" if c == "invalid_reasons" else "WARN")
    })

duplicate_after = int(df.duplicated(subset=["canonical_key"], keep=False).sum()) if not df.empty else 0
still_invalid = int((df["is_valid"].astype(str).str.lower().isin(["false","0","no"])).sum()) if "is_valid" in df.columns else len(df)
empty_key = int((df["canonical_key"].astype(str).str.strip() == "").sum()) if "canonical_key" in df.columns else len(df)

key_audit = [
    {"metric":"unique_final_rows","value":int(len(df))},
    {"metric":"duplicate_quarantine_rows","value":int(len(q))},
    {"metric":"duplicate_after_review","value":duplicate_after},
    {"metric":"still_invalid_rows","value":still_invalid},
    {"metric":"empty_canonical_key_rows","value":empty_key}
]

df["_vdf_v0279_readiness_policy"] = "REVIEW_ONLY_NO_DB_WRITE_NO_CANONICAL_MERGE"
df["_vdf_v0279_source_stage"] = "v027842_unique_final"
q["_vdf_v0279_quarantine_policy"] = "DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE_NOT_CANONICAL_WRITE_ELIGIBLE"

df.to_csv(final_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
df.to_json(final_json, orient="records", force_ascii=False, indent=2)
q.to_csv(quarantine_copy_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
pd.DataFrame(schema_rows).to_csv(schema_audit_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
pd.DataFrame(key_audit).to_csv(key_audit_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

metrics = {
    "unique_final_rows": int(len(df)),
    "duplicate_quarantine_rows": int(len(q)),
    "duplicate_after_review": duplicate_after,
    "still_invalid_rows": still_invalid,
    "empty_canonical_key_rows": empty_key,
    "schema_warn_count": int(sum(1 for x in schema_rows if str(x["status"]).startswith("WARN")))
}

if metrics["unique_final_rows"] > 0 and metrics["duplicate_after_review"] == 0 and metrics["still_invalid_rows"] == 0 and metrics["empty_canonical_key_rows"] == 0:
    status = "VDF_AKSHARE_EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_READY_WITH_QUARANTINE"
    risk = "MEDIUM" if metrics["duplicate_quarantine_rows"] > 0 else "LOW"
    recommendation = "ALLOW_V0280_FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY_NO_DB_WRITE"
    next_step = "v028.0 freeze seal review only; no DB write"
else:
    status = "VDF_AKSHARE_EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_BLOCKED"
    risk = "HIGH"
    recommendation = "BLOCK_V0280_UNTIL_READINESS_DEFECTS_REPAIRED"
    next_step = "repair readiness defects; no DB write"

seal = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "policy": {
        "review_only": True,
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
        "freeze_seal_allowed_next": status.endswith("WITH_QUARANTINE") or status.endswith("READY"),
        "duplicate_quarantine_must_not_write": True
    },
    "metrics": metrics,
    "artifacts": {
        "readiness_final_csv": str(final_csv),
        "readiness_final_json": str(final_json),
        "duplicate_quarantine_csv": str(quarantine_copy_csv),
        "schema_audit_csv": str(schema_audit_csv),
        "key_audit_csv": str(key_audit_csv)
    },
    "hashes": {
        "readiness_final_csv_sha256": sha256_file(final_csv),
        "readiness_final_json_sha256": sha256_file(final_json),
        "duplicate_quarantine_csv_sha256": sha256_file(quarantine_copy_csv)
    },
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "next_step": next_step
}
readiness_seal_json.write_text(json.dumps(seal, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

next_plan = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "stage": "v028.0",
    "title": "Freeze seal review only",
    "allowed": status != "VDF_AKSHARE_EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_BLOCKED",
    "rules": [
        "No DATABASE write",
        "No canonical merge",
        "Freeze readiness artifacts",
        "Preserve duplicate quarantine",
        "Only after user explicit approval may a later write-stage be designed"
    ],
    "inputs": {
        "readiness_final_csv": str(final_csv),
        "duplicate_quarantine_csv": str(quarantine_copy_csv),
        "readiness_seal_json": str(readiness_seal_json)
    }
}
next_plan_json.write_text(json.dumps(next_plan, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

result = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "metrics": metrics,
    "artifacts": seal["artifacts"] | {
        "readiness_seal_json": str(readiness_seal_json),
        "next_plan_json": str(next_plan_json)
    },
    "next_step": next_step
}
out_json.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
'@

    $Replace = @{
        "__UNIQUE_SOURCE__" = $UniqueFinalSource.FullName
        "__QUARANTINE_SOURCE__" = $DuplicateQuarantineSource.FullName
        "__FINAL_CSV__" = $ReadinessFinalCsv
        "__FINAL_JSON__" = $ReadinessFinalJson
        "__QUARANTINE_COPY_CSV__" = $QuarantineCopyCsv
        "__SCHEMA_AUDIT_CSV__" = $SchemaAuditCsv
        "__KEY_AUDIT_CSV__" = $KeyAuditCsv
        "__READINESS_SEAL_JSON__" = $ReadinessSealJson
        "__NEXT_PLAN_JSON__" = $NextPlanJson
        "__OUT_JSON__" = $PyResult
    }

    foreach ($k in $Replace.Keys) {
        $Py = $Py.Replace($k, ($Replace[$k] -replace "\\","\\"))
    }

    Set-Content -LiteralPath $PyScript -Value $Py -Encoding UTF8

    $python = Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($null -eq $python) { throw "python not found in PATH" }

    $psi = [System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo]::new()
    $psi.FileName = $python.Source
    $psi.Arguments = "`"$PyScript`""
    $psi.UseShellExecute = $false
    $psi.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
    $psi.RedirectStandardError = $true
    $psi.CreateNoWindow = $true

    $p = [System.Diagnostics.Process]::Start($psi)
    $null = $p.WaitForExit(180000)
    $stdout = $p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd()
    $stderr = $p.StandardError.ReadToEnd()

    if ($p.ExitCode -ne 0 -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PyResult)) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "READINESS_REVIEW" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "FAIL" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "exit_code" "$($p.ExitCode)" $stderr $PyScript
        throw "Python failed: $stderr"
    }

    $Result = Get-Content -LiteralPath $PyResult -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $M = $Result.metrics

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "READINESS_REVIEW" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "OK_EXECUTED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "stdout" "ok" $stdout $PyScript
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "READINESS_REVIEW" "UNIQUE_FINAL" "Readiness Final Rows" "OK_READY_SOURCE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "unique_final_rows" "$($M.unique_final_rows)" "Readiness final rows produced." $ReadinessFinalCsv

    if ([int]$M.duplicate_after_review -eq 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "READINESS_REVIEW" "DUPLICATE_CHECK" "Duplicate After Review" "OK_ZERO_DUPLICATE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "duplicate_after_review" "0" "No duplicate in readiness final rows." $ReadinessFinalCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "READINESS_REVIEW" "DUPLICATE_CHECK" "BLOCK_DUPLICATE_REMAINS" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "duplicate_after_review" "$($M.duplicate_after_review)" "Duplicate remains." $ReadinessFinalCsv
    }

    if ([int]$M.still_invalid_rows -eq 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "READINESS_REVIEW" "INVALID_CHECK" "Still Invalid Rows" "OK_ZERO_INVALID" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "still_invalid_rows" "0" "No invalid row in readiness final rows." $ReadinessFinalCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "READINESS_REVIEW" "INVALID_CHECK" "Still Invalid Rows" "BLOCK_INVALID_REMAINS" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "still_invalid_rows" "$($M.still_invalid_rows)" "Invalid rows remain." $ReadinessFinalCsv
    }

    if ([int]$M.duplicate_quarantine_rows -gt 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "READINESS_REVIEW" "DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE" "Duplicate Quarantine Rows" "WARN_QUARANTINE_PRESERVED" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "duplicate_quarantine_rows" "$($M.duplicate_quarantine_rows)" "Duplicate quarantine preserved and excluded from write eligibility." $QuarantineCopyCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "READINESS_REVIEW" "DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE" "Duplicate Quarantine Rows" "OK_ZERO_QUARANTINE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "duplicate_quarantine_rows" "0" "No quarantine rows." $QuarantineCopyCsv
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "SCHEMA_AUDIT" "Schema Audit" "OK_AUDIT_CREATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "schema_warn_count" "$($M.schema_warn_count)" "Schema audit generated." $SchemaAuditCsv
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "KEY_AUDIT" "Key Audit" "OK_AUDIT_CREATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "empty_canonical_key_rows" "$($M.empty_canonical_key_rows)" "Key audit generated." $KeyAuditCsv
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "READINESS_SEAL" "Readiness Seal" "OK_SEAL_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "seal" "created" "Execution readiness review seal created; no DB write." $ReadinessSealJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "NEXT_PLAN" "v028.0 Next Action Plan" "OK_PLAN_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "next" "$($Result.next_step)" "Next action plan generated." $NextPlanJson

    $Top10Libs = @(
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Execution Readiness Review"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pandas; polars; duckdb; pyarrow; pandera; pydantic; jsonschema; pytest; orjson; rich"; UseCase="readiness review/schema/key/no-write audit"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Freeze Seal"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="hashlib; json; pathlib; pandas; pyarrow; duckdb; pydantic; pandera; pytest; rich"; UseCase="hash manifest/freeze artifacts/seal QA"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="PowerShell Orchestration"; Language="PowerShell"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Pester; PSScriptAnalyzer; Microsoft.PowerShell.ThreadJob; ImportExcel; PSWriteHTML; ScheduledTasks; Start-Transcript; ConvertTo-Json; Get-FileHash; Write-Progress"; UseCase="test/debug/report/audit/hash/progress"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="HTML UI Report"; Language="JS/HTML"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Tabulator; DataTables; ECharts; Chart.js; Mermaid; Ajv; DOMPurify; FileSaver.js; Playwright; Lighthouse"; UseCase="matrix/smoke/chart/schema/export"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="VRN Integration Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pdfplumber; PyMuPDF; pdfminer.six; pypdf; camelot; tabula-py; opencv-python; pillow; pandas; pyarrow"; UseCase="future PDF/report extraction alignment"}
    )
    $Top10Libs | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $Top10Json -Encoding UTF8
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "LOCAL_FREE_LIBS" "Top10 Local Free Libs" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P2" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "rows" "$(@($Top10Libs).Count)" "Top10 local free libs matrix generated." $Top10Json

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "RESULT" "READINESS" $Result.status $(if ($Result.risk -eq "LOW") { "OK_READY" } elseif ($Result.risk -eq "MEDIUM") { "WARN_REVIEW_READY" } else { "BLOCKED" }) $Result.risk "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "recommendation" $Result.recommendation "v027.9 completed. Review only. No DB write. No canonical merge." $PyResult

    $CsvMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_ExecutionReadinessReview_Matrix_v0279.csv"
    $JsonMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_ExecutionReadinessReview_Matrix_v0279.json"
    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    try {
        $ptr = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ActivePointer) {
            Get-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
        } else {
            [pscustomobject]@{}
        }

        $ptr | Add-Member -NotePropertyName "latest_akshare_execution_readiness_review_v0279" -NotePropertyValue ([pscustomobject]@{
            generated_at = def_Iso
            status = $Result.status
            risk = $Result.risk
            run_root = $RunRoot
            result_json = $PyResult
            readiness_final_csv = $ReadinessFinalCsv
            duplicate_quarantine_csv = $QuarantineCopyCsv
            readiness_seal_json = $ReadinessSealJson

            duplicate_after_review = $M.duplicate_after_review
            still_invalid_rows = $M.still_invalid_rows
            recommendation = $Result.recommendation
        }) -Force

        $ptr | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Encoding UTF8
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "POINTER" "ACTIVE_POINTER" "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json" "OK_UPDATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_ONLY" "update" "complete" "Active pointer updated with v027.9." $ActivePointer
    } catch {}

    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    $trs = foreach ($r in $Rows) {
        $cls = if ($r.Risk -eq "HIGH" -or $r.Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { "bad" }
               elseif ($r.Risk -match "MEDIUM|REVIEW" -or $r.Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|QUARANTINE") { "warn" }
               else { "ok" }
        "<tr class='$cls'><td>$(def_Html $r.Round)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Stage)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Layer)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Name)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Status)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Risk)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.FixClass)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Priority)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Mode)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Metric)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Value)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Message)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Path)</td></tr>"
    }

PS7 function Invoke-VDF-AkShareFreezeSealReview-v0280 {
    param(
        [string]$ProjectRoot = "C:\Users\tonyk\Downloads\VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics",
        [switch]$OpenHtmlReport = $true
    )

    $script:OK = 0
    $script:WARN = 0
    $script:FAIL = 0

    function def_Stamp { Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss" }
    function def_Iso { (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffK") }
    function def_Mkdir([string]$p) { [System.IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($p) | Out-Null }
    function def_Html([AllowNull()][string]$s) {
        if ($null -eq $s) { return "" }
        return [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode($s)
    }

    function def_AddRow {
        param(
            [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]$Rows,
            [string]$Round,[string]$Stage,[string]$Layer,[string]$Name,
            [string]$Status,[string]$Risk,[string]$FixClass,[string]$Priority,
            [string]$Mode,[string]$Metric,[string]$Value,[string]$Message,[string]$Path
        )

        if ($Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { $script:FAIL++ }
        elseif ($Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|MEDIUM|MISSING|EMPTY|QUARANTINE") { $script:WARN++ }
        else { $script:OK++ }

        $Rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Round=$Round; Stage=$Stage; Layer=$Layer; Name=$Name; Status=$Status; Risk=$Risk
            FixClass=$FixClass; Priority=$Priority; Mode=$Mode; Metric=$Metric; Value=$Value
            Message=$Message; Path=$Path
        }) | Out-Null
    }

    function def_SafeHash {
        param([string]$Path,[string]$ExcludeRoot)
        try {
            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Path) -or -not [System.IO.File]::Exists($Path)) { return "" }
            $full = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Path)
            $root = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($ExcludeRoot)
            if ($full.StartsWith($root,[System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { return "SKIPPED_CURRENT_RUNROOT" }
            return (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256 -ErrorAction Stop).Hash
        } catch [System.IO.IOException] {
            return "LOCKED_OR_BUSY"
        } catch {
            return "HASH_ERROR"
        }
    }

    if (-not [System.IO.Directory]::Exists($ProjectRoot)) {
        throw "ProjectRoot not found: $ProjectRoot"
    }

    $Stamp = def_Stamp
    $DictRoot   = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "dict\VDF"
    $ActiveRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_active"
    $FreezeRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_freeze"
    $BackupRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_backup"

    foreach ($d in @($DictRoot,$ActiveRoot,$FreezeRoot,$BackupRoot)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Latest = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ActiveRoot -Directory -Filter "VDF_AKSHARE_EXECUTION_READINESS_REVIEW_v0279_*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $Latest) {
        throw "Latest v027.9 execution readiness review run not found."
    }

    $RunRoot = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_AKSHARE_FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_v0280_$Stamp"
    $FreezeSealRoot = Join-Path $FreezeRoot "VDF_AKSHARE_FREEZE_SEAL_v0280_$Stamp"

    $ReportDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "report"
    $RegistryDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "registry"
    $PlanDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "plan"
    $RuntimeDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "runtime"
    $BackupDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "backup"

    foreach ($d in @($RunRoot,$FreezeSealRoot,$ReportDir,$RegistryDir,$PlanDir,$RuntimeDir,$BackupDir)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Rows = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    $ActivePointer = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json"
    $Transcript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "VDF_AkShare_FreezeSealReview_Transcript_v0280_$Stamp.txt"

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "def VDF AKSHARE FREEZE SEAL REVIEW v028.0" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "VERITAS INTELLIGENCE ANALYTICS"
    Write-Host "Freeze seal review only · copy/hash/seal artifacts · no DATABASE write · no canonical merge"
    Write-Host ""

    try { Start-Transcript -LiteralPath $Transcript -Force | Out-Null } catch {}

    if ([System.IO.File]::Exists($ActivePointer)) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Destination (Join-Path $BackupDir "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER_before_v0280_$Stamp.json") -Force
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "BOOT" "PROJECT_ROOT" "VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "exists" "true" "Project root exists." $ProjectRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_DB_WRITE" "DATABASE" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "db_write_enabled" "false" "No DATABASE write in v028.0." $DictRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_CANONICAL_MERGE" "Canonical Merge" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "canonical_merge_enabled" "false" "No canonical merge in v028.0." $RunRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "FREEZE_ONLY" "Freeze Seal" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "write_permission" "false" "Freeze seal copies artifacts only; no execution." $FreezeSealRoot

    $ReadinessFinalCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0279_ReadinessFinalRows.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $ReadinessFinalJson = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0279_ReadinessFinalRows.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $DuplicateQuarantineCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0279_DuplicateQuarantineRows.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $ReadinessSealJson = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0279_ExecutionReadinessSeal.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $RuntimeResultJson = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "vdf_akshare_execution_readiness_review_result_v0279.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $SchemaAuditCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0279_SchemaAudit.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $KeyAuditCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0279_KeyAudit.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $RequiredFiles = @(
        $ReadinessFinalCsv,
        $ReadinessFinalJson,
        $DuplicateQuarantineCsv,
        $ReadinessSealJson,
        $RuntimeResultJson,
        $SchemaAuditCsv,
        $KeyAuditCsv
    )

    foreach ($f in $RequiredFiles) {
        if ($null -eq $f) {
            throw "Missing required v027.9 freeze artifact."
        }
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "V0279_ROOT" "Latest v027.9 Run" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "source" "v0279" "Locked latest v027.9 source." $Latest.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "READINESS_FINAL" "Readiness Final CSV" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "source" "readiness_final" "Readiness final source locked." $ReadinessFinalCsv.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE" "Duplicate Quarantine CSV" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "source" "duplicate_quarantine" "Duplicate quarantine source locked." $DuplicateQuarantineCsv.FullName

    $FreezeArtifactsDir = Join-Path $FreezeSealRoot "artifacts"
    def_Mkdir $FreezeArtifactsDir

    $Copied = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]

    foreach ($f in $RequiredFiles) {
        $dest = Join-Path $FreezeArtifactsDir $f.Name
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $dest -Force

        $Copied.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            name = $f.Name
            source = $f.FullName
            frozen_path = $dest
            bytes = (Get-Item -LiteralPath $dest).Length
            sha256 = def_SafeHash $dest $RunRoot
        }) | Out-Null
    }

    $FrozenManifestCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v0280_FrozenArtifactManifest.csv"
    $FrozenManifestJson = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v0280_FrozenArtifactManifest.json"
    $Copied | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $FrozenManifestCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Copied | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $FrozenManifestJson -Encoding UTF8

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "FREEZE" "ARTIFACT_COPY" "Frozen Artifacts" "OK_FROZEN" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "artifact_count" "$($Copied.Count)" "Required v027.9 artifacts copied into freeze seal folder." $FreezeArtifactsDir

    $PyScript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_freeze_seal_review_v0280.py"
    $PyResult = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_freeze_seal_review_result_v0280.json"

    $FreezeSealJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0280_FreezeSeal.json"
    $FreezeSealCopyJson = Join-Path $FreezeSealRoot "VDF_AkShare_v0280_FreezeSeal.json"
    $FreezeIndexJson = Join-Path $FreezeSealRoot "VDF_AkShare_v0280_FreezeIndex.json"
    $NextGateJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0281_NextGatePlan.json"
    $Top10Json = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_Top10_LocalFreeLibs_FreezeSealReview_v0280.json"

    $Py = @'
import json, hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import pandas as pd

readiness_csv = Path(r"__READINESS_CSV__")
quarantine_csv = Path(r"__QUARANTINE_CSV__")
manifest_json = Path(r"__MANIFEST_JSON__")
freeze_seal_json = Path(r"__FREEZE_SEAL_JSON__")
freeze_seal_copy_json = Path(r"__FREEZE_SEAL_COPY_JSON__")
freeze_index_json = Path(r"__FREEZE_INDEX_JSON__")
next_gate_json = Path(r"__NEXT_GATE_JSON__")
out_json = Path(r"__OUT_JSON__")

for p in [freeze_seal_json, freeze_seal_copy_json, freeze_index_json, next_gate_json, out_json]:
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

def read_csv(p):
    try:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str, encoding="utf-8-sig").fillna("")
    except Exception:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str).fillna("")

def sha256_file(p):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(p, "rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()

r = read_csv(readiness_csv)
q = read_csv(quarantine_csv)

for c in ["canonical_key","is_valid","family","symbol","date","value","source"]:
    if c not in r.columns:
        r[c] = ""

metrics = {
    "readiness_final_rows": int(len(r)),
    "duplicate_quarantine_rows": int(len(q)),
    "duplicate_after_freeze_check": int(r.duplicated(subset=["canonical_key"], keep=False).sum()) if not r.empty else 0,
    "still_invalid_rows": int((r["is_valid"].astype(str).str.lower().isin(["false","0","no"])).sum()) if "is_valid" in r.columns else len(r),
    "empty_canonical_key_rows": int((r["canonical_key"].astype(str).str.strip() == "").sum()) if "canonical_key" in r.columns else len(r),
    "empty_value_rows": int((r["value"].astype(str).str.strip() == "").sum()) if "value" in r.columns else len(r)
}

status = "VDF_AKSHARE_FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_READY_WITH_QUARANTINE"
risk = "MEDIUM"
recommendation = "ALLOW_V0281_USER_APPROVAL_GATE_FOR_WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_NO_DB_WRITE"

if metrics["readiness_final_rows"] <= 0 or metrics["duplicate_after_freeze_check"] > 0 or metrics["still_invalid_rows"] > 0 or metrics["empty_canonical_key_rows"] > 0:
    status = "VDF_AKSHARE_FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_BLOCKED"
    risk = "HIGH"
    recommendation = "BLOCK_V0281_UNTIL_FREEZE_DEFECTS_REPAIRED"

artifact_manifest = json.loads(Path(manifest_json).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

seal = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "policy": {
        "freeze_only": True,
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
        "duplicate_quarantine_preserved": True,
        "write_stage_requires_explicit_user_approval": True
    },
    "metrics": metrics,
    "hashes": {
        "readiness_final_csv_sha256": sha256_file(readiness_csv),
        "duplicate_quarantine_csv_sha256": sha256_file(quarantine_csv)
    },
    "artifact_manifest": artifact_manifest,
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "next_step": "v028.1 user approval gate for write-stage design only; no DB write" if risk != "HIGH" else "repair freeze defects; no DB write"
}

freeze_seal_json.write_text(json.dumps(seal, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
freeze_seal_copy_json.write_text(json.dumps(seal, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

freeze_index = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "title": "VDF AkShare Freeze Seal v028.0",
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "readiness_final_rows": metrics["readiness_final_rows"],
    "duplicate_quarantine_rows": metrics["duplicate_quarantine_rows"],
    "seal": str(freeze_seal_copy_json),
    "policy": seal["policy"]
}
freeze_index_json.write_text(json.dumps(freeze_index, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

next_gate = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "stage": "v028.1",
    "title": "User approval gate for write-stage design only",
    "allowed": risk != "HIGH",
    "still_no_db_write": True,
    "still_no_canonical_merge": True,
    "required_user_approval_before_write_stage": True,
    "candidate_write_inputs": {
        "readiness_final_rows": metrics["readiness_final_rows"],
        "duplicate_quarantine_rows": metrics["duplicate_quarantine_rows"]
    },
    "rules": [
        "v028.1 may design write-stage only; no write.",
        "duplicate quarantine must remain excluded.",
        "write-stage must create backup, dry-run compare, and rollback plan.",
        "actual DB write requires separate explicit approval."
    ]
}
next_gate_json.write_text(json.dumps(next_gate, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

result = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "metrics": metrics,
    "artifacts": {
        "freeze_seal_json": str(freeze_seal_json),
        "freeze_seal_copy_json": str(freeze_seal_copy_json),
        "freeze_index_json": str(freeze_index_json),
        "next_gate_json": str(next_gate_json)
    },
    "next_step": seal["next_step"]
}
out_json.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
'@

    $Replace = @{
        "__READINESS_CSV__" = $ReadinessFinalCsv.FullName
        "__QUARANTINE_CSV__" = $DuplicateQuarantineCsv.FullName
        "__MANIFEST_JSON__" = $FrozenManifestJson
        "__FREEZE_SEAL_JSON__" = $FreezeSealJson
        "__FREEZE_SEAL_COPY_JSON__" = $FreezeSealCopyJson
        "__FREEZE_INDEX_JSON__" = $FreezeIndexJson
        "__NEXT_GATE_JSON__" = $NextGateJson
        "__OUT_JSON__" = $PyResult
    }

    foreach ($k in $Replace.Keys) {
        $Py = $Py.Replace($k, ($Replace[$k] -replace "\\","\\"))
    }

    Set-Content -LiteralPath $PyScript -Value $Py -Encoding UTF8

    $python = Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($null -eq $python) { throw "python not found in PATH" }

    $psi = [System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo]::new()
    $psi.FileName = $python.Source
    $psi.Arguments = "`"$PyScript`""
    $psi.UseShellExecute = $false
    $psi.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
    $psi.RedirectStandardError = $true
    $psi.CreateNoWindow = $true

    $p = [System.Diagnostics.Process]::Start($psi)
    $null = $p.WaitForExit(180000)
    $stdout = $p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd()
    $stderr = $p.StandardError.ReadToEnd()

    if ($p.ExitCode -ne 0 -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PyResult)) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "FREEZE_SEAL" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "FAIL" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "exit_code" "$($p.ExitCode)" $stderr $PyScript
        throw "Python failed: $stderr"
    }

    $Result = Get-Content -LiteralPath $PyResult -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $M = $Result.metrics

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "FREEZE_SEAL" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "OK_EXECUTED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "stdout" "ok" $stdout $PyScript
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "FREEZE_SEAL" "READINESS_FINAL" "Readiness Final Rows" "OK_FROZEN" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "readiness_final_rows" "$($M.readiness_final_rows)" "Readiness final rows frozen." $ReadinessFinalCsv.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "FREEZE_SEAL" "DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE" "Duplicate Quarantine Rows" "WARN_QUARANTINE_FROZEN" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "duplicate_quarantine_rows" "$($M.duplicate_quarantine_rows)" "Duplicate quarantine frozen and preserved." $DuplicateQuarantineCsv.FullName

    if ([int]$M.duplicate_after_freeze_check -eq 0 -and [int]$M.still_invalid_rows -eq 0 -and [int]$M.empty_canonical_key_rows -eq 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "FREEZE_SEAL" "FREEZE_CHECK" "Freeze Integrity Check" "OK_READY" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "defects" "0" "Freeze integrity checks passed." $FreezeSealJson
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "FREEZE_SEAL" "FREEZE_CHECK" "Freeze Integrity Check" "BLOCK_DEFECTS" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "defects" "nonzero" "Freeze integrity defects remain." $FreezeSealJson
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "FREEZE_MANIFEST" "Frozen Artifact Manifest" "OK_MANIFEST_CREATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "artifacts" "$($Copied.Count)" "Frozen artifact manifest created." $FrozenManifestJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "FREEZE_SEAL" "Freeze Seal JSON" "OK_SEAL_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "seal" "created" "Freeze seal created." $FreezeSealJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "FREEZE_INDEX" "Freeze Index JSON" "OK_INDEX_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "index" "created" "Freeze index created." $FreezeIndexJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "NEXT_GATE" "v028.1 Next Gate" "OK_PLAN_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "next" "$($Result.next_step)" "Next gate plan created." $NextGateJson

    $Top10Libs = @(
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Freeze Seal"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="hashlib; json; pathlib; pandas; pyarrow; duckdb; pydantic; pandera; pytest; rich"; UseCase="hash manifest/freeze artifacts/seal QA"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Write-stage Design Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="duckdb; pandas; polars; pyarrow; pydantic; pandera; pytest; hypothesis; orjson; rich"; UseCase="future dry-run compare/rollback/write plan"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="PowerShell Orchestration"; Language="PowerShell"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Pester; PSScriptAnalyzer; Microsoft.PowerShell.ThreadJob; ImportExcel; PSWriteHTML; ScheduledTasks; Start-Transcript; ConvertTo-Json; Get-FileHash; Write-Progress"; UseCase="test/debug/report/audit/hash/progress"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="HTML UI Report"; Language="JS/HTML"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Tabulator; DataTables; ECharts; Chart.js; Mermaid; Ajv; DOMPurify; FileSaver.js; Playwright; Lighthouse"; UseCase="matrix/smoke/chart/schema/export"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="VRN Integration Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pdfplumber; PyMuPDF; pdfminer.six; pypdf; camelot; tabula-py; opencv-python; pillow; pandas; pyarrow"; UseCase="future PDF/report extraction alignment"}
    )
    $Top10Libs | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $Top10Json -Encoding UTF8
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "LOCAL_FREE_LIBS" "Top10 Local Free Libs" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P2" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "rows" "$(@($Top10Libs).Count)" "Top10 local free libs matrix generated." $Top10Json

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "RESULT" "READINESS" $Result.status $(if ($Result.risk -eq "LOW") { "OK_READY" } elseif ($Result.risk -eq "MEDIUM") { "WARN_REVIEW_READY" } else { "BLOCKED" }) $Result.risk "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "recommendation" $Result.recommendation "v028.0 completed. Freeze only. No DB write. No canonical merge." $PyResult

    $CsvMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_FreezeSealReview_Matrix_v0280.csv"
    $JsonMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_FreezeSealReview_Matrix_v0280.json"
    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    try {
        $ptr = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ActivePointer) {
            Get-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
        } else {
            [pscustomobject]@{}
        }

        $ptr | Add-Member -NotePropertyName "latest_akshare_freeze_seal_review_v0280" -NotePropertyValue ([pscustomobject]@{
            generated_at = def_Iso
            status = $Result.status
            risk = $Result.risk
            run_root = $RunRoot
            freeze_seal_root = $FreezeSealRoot
            result_json = $PyResult
            freeze_seal_json = $FreezeSealJson
            freeze_index_json = $FreezeIndexJson
            readiness_final_rows = $M.readiness_final_rows
            duplicate_quarantine_rows = $M.duplicate_quarantine_rows
            recommendation = $Result.recommendation
        }) -Force

        $ptr | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Encoding UTF8
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "POINTER" "ACTIVE_POINTER" "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json" "OK_UPDATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_ONLY" "update" "complete" "Active pointer updated with v028.0." $ActivePointer
    } catch {}

    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    $trs = foreach ($r in $Rows) {
        $cls = if ($r.Risk -eq "HIGH" -or $r.Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { "bad" }
               elseif ($r.Risk -match "MEDIUM|REVIEW" -or $r.Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|QUARANTINE") { "warn" }
               else { "ok" }
        "<tr class='$cls'><td>$(def_Html $r.Round)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Stage)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Layer)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Name)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Status)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Risk)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.FixClass)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Priority)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Mode)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Metric)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Value)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Message)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Path)</td></tr>"
    }

    $HtmlReport = Join-Path $ReportDir "VDF_AkShare_FreezeSealReview_Report_v0280.html"
    $Html = @"
