PS7 function Invoke-VDF-AkShareUserApprovalGateForWriteStageDesign-v0281 {
    param(
        [string]$ProjectRoot = "C:\Users\tonyk\Downloads\VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics",
        [switch]$OpenHtmlReport = $true
    )

    $script:OK = 0
    $script:WARN = 0
    $script:FAIL = 0

    function def_Stamp { Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss" }
    function def_Iso { (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffK") }
    function def_Mkdir([string]$p) { [System.IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($p) | Out-Null }
    function def_Html([AllowNull()][string]$s) {
        if ($null -eq $s) { return "" }
        return [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode($s)
    }

    function def_AddRow {
        param(
            [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]$Rows,
            [string]$Round,[string]$Stage,[string]$Layer,[string]$Name,
            [string]$Status,[string]$Risk,[string]$FixClass,[string]$Priority,
            [string]$Mode,[string]$Metric,[string]$Value,[string]$Message,[string]$Path
        )

        if ($Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { $script:FAIL++ }
        elseif ($Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|MEDIUM|MISSING|EMPTY|QUARANTINE|APPROVAL") { $script:WARN++ }
        else { $script:OK++ }

        $Rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Round=$Round; Stage=$Stage; Layer=$Layer; Name=$Name; Status=$Status; Risk=$Risk
            FixClass=$FixClass; Priority=$Priority; Mode=$Mode; Metric=$Metric; Value=$Value
            Message=$Message; Path=$Path
        }) | Out-Null
    }

    function def_SafeHash {
        param([string]$Path,[string]$ExcludeRoot)
        try {
            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Path) -or -not [System.IO.File]::Exists($Path)) { return "" }
            $full = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Path)
            $root = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($ExcludeRoot)
            if ($full.StartsWith($root,[System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { return "SKIPPED_CURRENT_RUNROOT" }
            return (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256 -ErrorAction Stop).Hash
        } catch [System.IO.IOException] {
            return "LOCKED_OR_BUSY"
        } catch {
            return "HASH_ERROR"
        }
    }

    if (-not [System.IO.Directory]::Exists($ProjectRoot)) {
        throw "ProjectRoot not found: $ProjectRoot"
    }

    $Stamp = def_Stamp
    $DictRoot   = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "dict\VDF"
    $ActiveRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_active"
    $FreezeRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_freeze"
    $BackupRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "_backup"
    $DatabaseRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "DATABASE"

    foreach ($d in @($DictRoot,$ActiveRoot,$FreezeRoot,$BackupRoot,$DatabaseRoot)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $LatestFreeze = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $FreezeRoot -Directory -Filter "VDF_AKSHARE_FREEZE_SEAL_v0280_*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    $LatestReview = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ActiveRoot -Directory -Filter "VDF_AKSHARE_FREEZE_SEAL_REVIEW_v0280_*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $LatestFreeze) { throw "Latest v028.0 freeze seal folder not found." }
    if ($null -eq $LatestReview) { throw "Latest v028.0 freeze seal review folder not found." }

    $RunRoot = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_AKSHARE_USER_APPROVAL_GATE_WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_v0281_$Stamp"
    $ReportDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "report"
    $RegistryDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "registry"
    $PlanDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "plan"
    $RuntimeDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "runtime"
    $BackupDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "backup"

    foreach ($d in @($RunRoot,$ReportDir,$RegistryDir,$PlanDir,$RuntimeDir,$BackupDir)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Rows = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    $ActivePointer = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json"
    $Transcript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "VDF_AkShare_UserApprovalGate_WriteStageDesign_Transcript_v0281_$Stamp.txt"

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "def VDF AKSHARE USER APPROVAL GATE FOR WRITE-STAGE DESIGN v028.1" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "VERITAS INTELLIGENCE ANALYTICS"
    Write-Host "Design only · approval gate · dry-run/rollback plan · no DATABASE write · no canonical merge"
    Write-Host ""

    try { Start-Transcript -LiteralPath $Transcript -Force | Out-Null } catch {}

    if ([System.IO.File]::Exists($ActivePointer)) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Destination (Join-Path $BackupDir "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER_before_v0281_$Stamp.json") -Force
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "BOOT" "PROJECT_ROOT" "VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "exists" "true" "Project root exists." $ProjectRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_DB_WRITE" "DATABASE" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "db_write_enabled" "false" "No DATABASE write in v028.1." $DatabaseRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_CANONICAL_MERGE" "Canonical Merge" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "canonical_merge_enabled" "false" "No canonical merge in v028.1." $RunRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "USER_APPROVAL_REQUIRED" "Write Stage" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "actual_write_allowed" "false" "Actual write requires separate explicit approval after this design gate." $RunRoot

    $FreezeSealJson = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $LatestFreeze.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0280_FreezeSeal.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $FreezeIndexJson = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $LatestFreeze.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0280_FreezeIndex.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $ReadinessFinalCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $LatestFreeze.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0279_ReadinessFinalRows.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $DuplicateQuarantineCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $LatestFreeze.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0279_DuplicateQuarantineRows.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $FreezeSealJson) { throw "Freeze seal JSON not found." }
    if ($null -eq $FreezeIndexJson) { throw "Freeze index JSON not found." }
    if ($null -eq $ReadinessFinalCsv) { throw "Frozen readiness final CSV not found." }
    if ($null -eq $DuplicateQuarantineCsv) { throw "Frozen duplicate quarantine CSV not found." }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "FREEZE_SEAL" "v028.0 Freeze Seal" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "source" "freeze_seal" "Locked v028.0 freeze seal." $FreezeSealJson.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "READINESS_FINAL" "Frozen Readiness Final CSV" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "source" "readiness_final" "Locked frozen readiness final rows." $ReadinessFinalCsv.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE" "Frozen Duplicate Quarantine CSV" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "source" "duplicate_quarantine" "Locked duplicate quarantine rows." $DuplicateQuarantineCsv.FullName

    $PyScript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_user_approval_gate_write_stage_design_v0281.py"
    $PyResult = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_user_approval_gate_write_stage_design_result_v0281.json"

    $WriteDesignJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0281_WriteStageDesign.json"
    $DryRunComparePlanJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0281_DryRunComparePlan.json"
    $RollbackPlanJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0281_RollbackPlan.json"
    $ApprovalGateJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0281_UserApprovalGate.json"
    $PreWriteChecklistCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v0281_PreWriteChecklist.csv"
    $InputHashManifestCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v0281_InputHashManifest.csv"
    $InputHashManifestJson = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v0281_InputHashManifest.json"
    $Top10Json = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_Top10_LocalFreeLibs_UserApprovalGateWriteStageDesign_v0281.json"

    $CandidateFiles = @(
        $FreezeSealJson.FullName,
        $FreezeIndexJson.FullName,
        $ReadinessFinalCsv.FullName,
        $DuplicateQuarantineCsv.FullName
    )

    $HashManifest = foreach ($p in $CandidateFiles) {
        $fi = Get-Item -LiteralPath $p
        [pscustomobject]@{
            name = $fi.Name
            path = $fi.FullName
            bytes = $fi.Length
            sha256 = def_SafeHash $fi.FullName $RunRoot
        }
    }

    $HashManifest | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $InputHashManifestCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $HashManifest | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $InputHashManifestJson -Encoding UTF8

    $Py = @'
import json, hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import pandas as pd

freeze_seal_json = Path(r"__FREEZE_SEAL_JSON__")
readiness_csv = Path(r"__READINESS_CSV__")
quarantine_csv = Path(r"__QUARANTINE_CSV__")
write_design_json = Path(r"__WRITE_DESIGN_JSON__")
dryrun_compare_plan_json = Path(r"__DRYRUN_COMPARE_PLAN_JSON__")
rollback_plan_json = Path(r"__ROLLBACK_PLAN_JSON__")
approval_gate_json = Path(r"__APPROVAL_GATE_JSON__")
prewrite_checklist_csv = Path(r"__PREWRITE_CHECKLIST_CSV__")
out_json = Path(r"__OUT_JSON__")

for p in [write_design_json, dryrun_compare_plan_json, rollback_plan_json, approval_gate_json, prewrite_checklist_csv, out_json]:
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

def read_csv(p):
    try:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str, encoding="utf-8-sig").fillna("")
    except Exception:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str).fillna("")

def sha256_file(p):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(p, "rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()

seal = json.loads(freeze_seal_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
ready = read_csv(readiness_csv)
quarantine = read_csv(quarantine_csv)

for c in ["family","symbol","date","value","source","canonical_key","is_valid"]:
    if c not in ready.columns:
        ready[c] = ""

metrics = {
    "candidate_write_rows": int(len(ready)),
    "duplicate_quarantine_rows": int(len(quarantine)),
    "duplicate_after_check": int(ready.duplicated(subset=["canonical_key"], keep=False).sum()) if not ready.empty else 0,
    "still_invalid_rows": int((ready["is_valid"].astype(str).str.lower().isin(["false","0","no"])).sum()),
    "empty_key_rows": int((ready["canonical_key"].astype(str).str.strip() == "").sum()),
    "empty_value_rows": int((ready["value"].astype(str).str.strip() == "").sum()),
}

ready_for_design = (
    metrics["candidate_write_rows"] > 0 and
    metrics["duplicate_after_check"] == 0 and
    metrics["still_invalid_rows"] == 0 and
    metrics["empty_key_rows"] == 0 and
    metrics["empty_value_rows"] == 0
)

status = "VDF_AKSHARE_USER_APPROVAL_GATE_WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_READY" if ready_for_design else "VDF_AKSHARE_USER_APPROVAL_GATE_WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_BLOCKED"
risk = "MEDIUM" if ready_for_design else "HIGH"
recommendation = "ALLOW_V0282_WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY_NO_DB_WRITE" if ready_for_design else "BLOCK_V0282_UNTIL_DESIGN_DEFECTS_REPAIRED"
next_step = "v028.2 write dry-run compare only; no DB write" if ready_for_design else "repair design defects; no DB write"

write_design = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "stage": "v028.1",
    "title": "Write-stage design only; no DB write",
    "policy": {
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
        "actual_write_allowed": False,
        "requires_separate_explicit_user_approval": True,
        "duplicate_quarantine_excluded": True,
        "backup_required_before_any_future_write": True,
        "rollback_required_before_any_future_write": True
    },
    "target": {
        "database_root": "C:\\Users\\tonyk\\Downloads\\VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics\\dict\\VDF\\DATABASE",
        "canonical_family": "VDF_Global_Commodity",
        "primary_key": ["family","symbol","date","source"],
        "write_mode_candidate": "upsert_after_dryrun_compare_only"
    },
    "inputs": {
        "readiness_final_csv": str(readiness_csv),
        "duplicate_quarantine_csv": str(quarantine_csv),
        "freeze_seal_json": str(freeze_seal_json)
    },
    "metrics": metrics,
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "next_step": next_step
}
write_design_json.write_text(json.dumps(write_design, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

dryrun_compare_plan = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "stage": "v028.2",
    "title": "Write dry-run compare only; no DB write",
    "allowed": ready_for_design,
    "policy": {
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
        "compare_only": True
    },
    "compare_steps": [
        "Load existing canonical table if present.",
        "Load readiness final rows.",
        "Compare primary-key overlap.",
        "Classify insert/update/unchanged/conflict.",
        "Emit dry-run delta CSV/JSON.",
        "Do not write DATABASE."
    ],
    "required_outputs": [
        "dryrun_delta_insert.csv",
        "dryrun_delta_update.csv",
        "dryrun_conflicts.csv",
        "dryrun_summary.json",
        "html matrix report"
    ]
}
dryrun_compare_plan_json.write_text(json.dumps(dryrun_compare_plan, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

rollback_plan = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "stage": "future_write_stage",
    "title": "Rollback plan before actual DB write",
    "required_before_write": True,
    "steps": [
        "Create full DATABASE backup.",
        "Create target table snapshot.",
        "Write to staging table first.",
        "Validate row count and key uniqueness.",
        "Promote staging table only after validation.",
        "On failure restore previous DATABASE snapshot.",
        "Open HTML report."
    ],
    "forbidden_in_v0281": [
        "No DB write",
        "No canonical merge",
        "No destructive delete",
        "No Stop-Process"
    ]
}
rollback_plan_json.write_text(json.dumps(rollback_plan, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

approval_gate = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "approval_required": True,
    "approved_now": False,
    "what_user_may_approve_next": "v028.2 write dry-run compare only; still no DB write",
    "what_is_not_approved": "actual database write or canonical merge",
    "metrics": metrics,
    "hashes": {
        "readiness_csv_sha256": sha256_file(readiness_csv),
        "quarantine_csv_sha256": sha256_file(quarantine_csv),
        "freeze_seal_json_sha256": sha256_file(freeze_seal_json)
    },
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "next_step": next_step
}
approval_gate_json.write_text(json.dumps(approval_gate, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

checklist = [
    {"item":"freeze_seal_exists","status":"OK" if freeze_seal_json.exists() else "FAIL"},
    {"item":"candidate_write_rows_gt_0","status":"OK" if metrics["candidate_write_rows"] > 0 else "FAIL"},
    {"item":"duplicate_after_check_zero","status":"OK" if metrics["duplicate_after_check"] == 0 else "FAIL"},
    {"item":"still_invalid_rows_zero","status":"OK" if metrics["still_invalid_rows"] == 0 else "FAIL"},
    {"item":"empty_key_rows_zero","status":"OK" if metrics["empty_key_rows"] == 0 else "FAIL"},
    {"item":"empty_value_rows_zero","status":"OK" if metrics["empty_value_rows"] == 0 else "FAIL"},
    {"item":"duplicate_quarantine_preserved","status":"OK" if metrics["duplicate_quarantine_rows"] >= 0 else "FAIL"},
    {"item":"db_write_disabled","status":"OK"},
    {"item":"canonical_merge_disabled","status":"OK"},
    {"item":"actual_write_requires_future_approval","status":"OK"}
]
pd.DataFrame(checklist).to_csv(prewrite_checklist_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

result = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "metrics": metrics,
    "artifacts": {
        "write_design_json": str(write_design_json),
        "dryrun_compare_plan_json": str(dryrun_compare_plan_json),
        "rollback_plan_json": str(rollback_plan_json),
        "approval_gate_json": str(approval_gate_json),
        "prewrite_checklist_csv": str(prewrite_checklist_csv)
    },
    "next_step": next_step
}
out_json.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
'@

    $Replace = @{
        "__FREEZE_SEAL_JSON__" = $FreezeSealJson.FullName
        "__READINESS_CSV__" = $ReadinessFinalCsv.FullName
        "__QUARANTINE_CSV__" = $DuplicateQuarantineCsv.FullName
        "__WRITE_DESIGN_JSON__" = $WriteDesignJson
        "__DRYRUN_COMPARE_PLAN_JSON__" = $DryRunComparePlanJson
        "__ROLLBACK_PLAN_JSON__" = $RollbackPlanJson
        "__APPROVAL_GATE_JSON__" = $ApprovalGateJson
        "__PREWRITE_CHECKLIST_CSV__" = $PreWriteChecklistCsv
        "__OUT_JSON__" = $PyResult
    }

    foreach ($k in $Replace.Keys) {
        $Py = $Py.Replace($k, ($Replace[$k] -replace "\\","\\"))
    }

    Set-Content -LiteralPath $PyScript -Value $Py -Encoding UTF8

    $python = Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($null -eq $python) { throw "python not found in PATH" }

    $psi = [System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo]::new()
    $psi.FileName = $python.Source
    $psi.Arguments = "`"$PyScript`""
    $psi.UseShellExecute = $false
    $psi.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
    $psi.RedirectStandardError = $true
    $psi.CreateNoWindow = $true

    $p = [System.Diagnostics.Process]::Start($psi)
    $null = $p.WaitForExit(180000)
    $stdout = $p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd()
    $stderr = $p.StandardError.ReadToEnd()

    if ($p.ExitCode -ne 0 -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PyResult)) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "FAIL" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "exit_code" "$($p.ExitCode)" $stderr $PyScript
        throw "Python failed: $stderr"
    }

    $Result = Get-Content -LiteralPath $PyResult -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $M = $Result.metrics

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "OK_EXECUTED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "stdout" "ok" $stdout $PyScript
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN" "CANDIDATE_ROWS" "Candidate Write Rows" "OK_READY_SOURCE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "candidate_write_rows" "$($M.candidate_write_rows)" "Candidate write rows verified for design only." $ReadinessFinalCsv.FullName

    if ([int]$M.duplicate_after_check -eq 0 -and [int]$M.still_invalid_rows -eq 0 -and [int]$M.empty_key_rows -eq 0 -and [int]$M.empty_value_rows -eq 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN" "PREWRITE_CHECK" "Pre-write Checklist" "OK_DESIGN_READY" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "defects" "0" "Write-stage design gate passed; still no DB write." $PreWriteChecklistCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN" "PREWRITE_CHECK" "Pre-write Checklist" "BLOCK_DEFECTS" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "defects" "nonzero" "Design defects remain." $PreWriteChecklistCsv
    }

    if ([int]$M.duplicate_quarantine_rows -gt 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN" "DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE" "Duplicate Quarantine" "WARN_QUARANTINE_EXCLUDED" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "duplicate_quarantine_rows" "$($M.duplicate_quarantine_rows)" "Duplicate quarantine preserved and excluded from future write candidate." $DuplicateQuarantineCsv.FullName
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN" "DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE" "Duplicate Quarantine" "OK_ZERO_QUARANTINE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "duplicate_quarantine_rows" "0" "No duplicate quarantine rows." $DuplicateQuarantineCsv.FullName
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "WRITE_DESIGN" "Write Stage Design JSON" "OK_PLAN_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "plan" "created" "Write-stage design created; no DB write." $WriteDesignJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "DRYRUN_COMPARE_PLAN" "Dry-run Compare Plan" "OK_PLAN_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "plan" "created" "v028.2 dry-run compare plan created." $DryRunComparePlanJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "ROLLBACK_PLAN" "Rollback Plan" "OK_PLAN_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "plan" "created" "Rollback plan created for future write stage." $RollbackPlanJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "APPROVAL_GATE" "User Approval Gate" "WARN_APPROVAL_REQUIRED" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "approved_now" "false" "User approval still required before any later write-stage." $ApprovalGateJson

    $Top10Libs = @(
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Write-stage Design Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="duckdb; pandas; polars; pyarrow; pydantic; pandera; pytest; hypothesis; orjson; rich"; UseCase="future dry-run compare/rollback/write plan"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Dry-run Compare"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="duckdb; pandas; polars; pyarrow; datacompy; deepdiff; pydantic; pandera; pytest; rich"; UseCase="insert/update/conflict/unchanged compare"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="PowerShell Orchestration"; Language="PowerShell"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Pester; PSScriptAnalyzer; Microsoft.PowerShell.ThreadJob; ImportExcel; PSWriteHTML; ScheduledTasks; Start-Transcript; ConvertTo-Json; Get-FileHash; Write-Progress"; UseCase="test/debug/report/audit/hash/progress"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="HTML UI Report"; Language="JS/HTML"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Tabulator; DataTables; ECharts; Chart.js; Mermaid; Ajv; DOMPurify; FileSaver.js; Playwright; Lighthouse"; UseCase="matrix/smoke/chart/schema/export"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="VRN Integration Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pdfplumber; PyMuPDF; pdfminer.six; pypdf; camelot; tabula-py; opencv-python; pillow; pandas; pyarrow"; UseCase="future PDF/report extraction alignment"}
    )
    $Top10Libs | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $Top10Json -Encoding UTF8
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "LOCAL_FREE_LIBS" "Top10 Local Free Libs" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P2" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "rows" "$(@($Top10Libs).Count)" "Top10 local free libs matrix generated." $Top10Json

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "RESULT" "READINESS" $Result.status $(if ($Result.risk -eq "LOW") { "OK_READY" } elseif ($Result.risk -eq "MEDIUM") { "WARN_REVIEW_READY" } else { "BLOCKED" }) $Result.risk "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "recommendation" $Result.recommendation "v028.1 completed. Design only. No DB write. No canonical merge." $PyResult

    $CsvMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_UserApprovalGate_WriteStageDesign_Matrix_v0281.csv"
    $JsonMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_UserApprovalGate_WriteStageDesign_Matrix_v0281.json"
    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    try {
        $ptr = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ActivePointer) {
            Get-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
        } else {
            [pscustomobject]@{}
        }

        $ptr | Add-Member -NotePropertyName "latest_akshare_user_approval_gate_write_stage_design_v0281" -NotePropertyValue ([pscustomobject]@{
            generated_at = def_Iso
            status = $Result.status
            risk = $Result.risk
            run_root = $RunRoot
            result_json = $PyResult
            write_design_json = $WriteDesignJson
            dryrun_compare_plan_json = $DryRunComparePlanJson
            rollback_plan_json = $RollbackPlanJson
            approval_gate_json = $ApprovalGateJson
            candidate_write_rows = $M.candidate_write_rows
            duplicate_quarantine_rows = $M.duplicate_quarantine_rows
            recommendation = $Result.recommendation
        }) -Force

        $ptr | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Encoding UTF8
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "POINTER" "ACTIVE_POINTER" "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json" "OK_UPDATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_ONLY" "update" "complete" "Active pointer updated with v028.1." $ActivePointer
    } catch {}

    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    $trs = foreach ($r in $Rows) {
        $cls = if ($r.Risk -eq "HIGH" -or $r.Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { "bad" }
               elseif ($r.Risk -match "MEDIUM|REVIEW" -or $r.Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|QUARANTINE|APPROVAL") { "warn" }
               else { "ok" }
        "<tr class='$cls'><td>$(def_Html $r.Round)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Stage)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Layer)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Name)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Status)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Risk)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.FixClass)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Priority)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Mode)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Metric)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Value)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Message)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Path)</td></tr>"
    }

    $HtmlReport = Join-Path $ReportDir "VDF_AkShare_UserApprovalGate_WriteStageDesign_Report_v0281.html"
    $Html = @"

PS7 try {
    Invoke-VDF-AkShareUserApprovalGateForWriteStageDesign-v0281
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Red
    Write-Host "def VDF AKSHARE USER APPROVAL GATE FOR WRITE-STAGE DESIGN v028.1 FATAL BUT POWERSHELL STAYS OPEN" -ForegroundColor Red
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Red
    Write-Host "[FATAL] $($_.Exception.Message)" -ForegroundColor Red
    Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace -ForegroundColor DarkRed
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "PowerShell remains open. No exit was called." -ForegroundColor Yellow
}

PS7 function Invoke-VDF-AkShareWriteDryRunCompare-v0282 {
    param(
        [string]$ProjectRoot = "C:\Users\tonyk\Downloads\VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics",
        [switch]$OpenHtmlReport = $true
    )

    $script:OK = 0
    $script:WARN = 0
    $script:FAIL = 0

    function def_Stamp { Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss" }
    function def_Iso { (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffK") }
    function def_Mkdir([string]$p) { [System.IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($p) | Out-Null }
    function def_Html([AllowNull()][string]$s) {
        if ($null -eq $s) { return "" }
        return [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode($s)
    }

    function def_AddRow {
        param(
            [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]$Rows,
            [string]$Round,[string]$Stage,[string]$Layer,[string]$Name,
            [string]$Status,[string]$Risk,[string]$FixClass,[string]$Priority,
            [string]$Mode,[string]$Metric,[string]$Value,[string]$Message,[string]$Path
        )

        if ($Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { $script:FAIL++ }
        elseif ($Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|MEDIUM|MISSING|EMPTY|CONFLICT|QUARANTINE") { $script:WARN++ }
        else { $script:OK++ }

        $Rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Round=$Round; Stage=$Stage; Layer=$Layer; Name=$Name; Status=$Status; Risk=$Risk
            FixClass=$FixClass; Priority=$Priority; Mode=$Mode; Metric=$Metric; Value=$Value
            Message=$Message; Path=$Path
        }) | Out-Null
    }

    function def_SafeHash {
        param([string]$Path,[string]$ExcludeRoot)
        try {
            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Path) -or -not [System.IO.File]::Exists($Path)) { return "" }
            $full = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Path)
            $root = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($ExcludeRoot)
            if ($full.StartsWith($root,[System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { return "SKIPPED_CURRENT_RUNROOT" }
            return (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256 -ErrorAction Stop).Hash
        } catch [System.IO.IOException] {
            return "LOCKED_OR_BUSY"
        } catch {
            return "HASH_ERROR"
        }
    }

    if (-not [System.IO.Directory]::Exists($ProjectRoot)) {
        throw "ProjectRoot not found: $ProjectRoot"
    }

    $Stamp = def_Stamp
    $DictRoot     = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "dict\VDF"
    $ActiveRoot   = Join-Path $DictRoot "_active"
    $FreezeRoot   = Join-Path $DictRoot "_freeze"
    $BackupRoot   = Join-Path $DictRoot "_backup"
    $DatabaseRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "DATABASE"

    foreach ($d in @($DictRoot,$ActiveRoot,$FreezeRoot,$BackupRoot,$DatabaseRoot)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Latest = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ActiveRoot -Directory -Filter "VDF_AKSHARE_USER_APPROVAL_GATE_WRITE_STAGE_DESIGN_v0281_*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $Latest) {
        throw "Latest v028.1 user approval gate write-stage design run not found."
    }

    $RunRoot = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_AKSHARE_WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_v0282_$Stamp"
    $ReportDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "report"
    $RegistryDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "registry"
    $PlanDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "plan"
    $PreviewDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "preview"
    $RuntimeDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "runtime"
    $BackupDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "backup"

    foreach ($d in @($RunRoot,$ReportDir,$RegistryDir,$PlanDir,$PreviewDir,$RuntimeDir,$BackupDir)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Rows = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    $ActivePointer = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json"
    $Transcript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "VDF_AkShare_WriteDryRunCompare_Transcript_v0282_$Stamp.txt"

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "def VDF AKSHARE WRITE DRY-RUN COMPARE v028.2" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "VERITAS INTELLIGENCE ANALYTICS"
    Write-Host "Compare only · insert/update/conflict preview · no DATABASE write · no canonical merge"
    Write-Host ""

    try { Start-Transcript -LiteralPath $Transcript -Force | Out-Null } catch {}

    if ([System.IO.File]::Exists($ActivePointer)) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Destination (Join-Path $BackupDir "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER_before_v0282_$Stamp.json") -Force
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "BOOT" "PROJECT_ROOT" "VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "exists" "true" "Project root exists." $ProjectRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_DB_WRITE" "DATABASE" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "db_write_enabled" "false" "No DATABASE write in v028.2." $DatabaseRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_CANONICAL_MERGE" "Canonical Merge" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "canonical_merge_enabled" "false" "No canonical merge in v028.2." $RunRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "COMPARE_ONLY" "Dry-run Compare" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "actual_write_allowed" "false" "This stage can only compare and output delta previews." $RunRoot

    $WriteDesignJson = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0281_WriteStageDesign.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $ApprovalGateJson = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0281_UserApprovalGate.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $WriteDesignJson) { throw "v028.1 write design JSON not found." }
    if ($null -eq $ApprovalGateJson) { throw "v028.1 approval gate JSON not found." }

    $Design = Get-Content -LiteralPath $WriteDesignJson.FullName -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $CandidateCsvPath = [string]$Design.inputs.readiness_final_csv
    $QuarantineCsvPath = [string]$Design.inputs.duplicate_quarantine_csv

    if (-not [System.IO.File]::Exists($CandidateCsvPath)) {
        throw "Candidate readiness final CSV not found from v028.1 design: $CandidateCsvPath"
    }
    if (-not [System.IO.File]::Exists($QuarantineCsvPath)) {
        throw "Duplicate quarantine CSV not found from v028.1 design: $QuarantineCsvPath"
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "WRITE_DESIGN" "v028.1 Write Design" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "source" "write_design" "Locked v028.1 write design." $WriteDesignJson.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "CANDIDATE_ROWS" "Readiness Candidate CSV" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "source" "readiness_final" "Locked candidate rows for compare only." $CandidateCsvPath
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE" "Duplicate Quarantine CSV" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "source" "duplicate_quarantine" "Locked duplicate quarantine; excluded from candidate compare." $QuarantineCsvPath

    $CanonicalCandidateFiles = @()
    if ([System.IO.Directory]::Exists($DatabaseRoot)) {
        $CanonicalCandidateFiles = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $DatabaseRoot -Recurse -File -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
            Where-Object {
                $_.Name -match "VDF_Global_Commodity|Global_Commodity|Commodity|AkShare" -and
                $_.Extension -match "\.csv|\.json|\.parquet"
            } |
            Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending
    }

    $CanonicalInventoryCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v0282_CanonicalInventory.csv"
    $CanonicalInventoryJson = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v0282_CanonicalInventory.json"

    $Inv = foreach ($f in $CanonicalCandidateFiles) {
        [pscustomobject]@{
            name = $f.Name
            path = $f.FullName
            extension = $f.Extension
            bytes = $f.Length
            modified = $f.LastWriteTime.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
            sha256 = def_SafeHash $f.FullName $RunRoot
        }
    }
    $Inv | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CanonicalInventoryCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Inv | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $CanonicalInventoryJson -Encoding UTF8

    if (@($CanonicalCandidateFiles).Count -eq 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "CANONICAL_EXISTING" "Existing Canonical Candidate" "WARN_EMPTY_CANONICAL_NOT_FOUND" "MEDIUM" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "candidate_files" "0" "No existing canonical file found; dry-run will classify all candidate rows as insert." $DatabaseRoot
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "CANONICAL_EXISTING" "Existing Canonical Candidate" "OK_FOUND" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "candidate_files" "$(@($CanonicalCandidateFiles).Count)" "Existing canonical candidate files inventoried." $CanonicalInventoryCsv
    }

    $CanonicalListJson = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_v0282_canonical_file_list.json"
    $CanonicalList = @(
        foreach ($f in $CanonicalCandidateFiles) {
            [pscustomobject]@{
                path = $f.FullName
                name = $f.Name
                extension = $f.Extension
                bytes = $f.Length
            }
        }
    )
    ConvertTo-Json -InputObject @($CanonicalList) -Depth 20 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $CanonicalListJson -Encoding UTF8

    $PyScript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_write_dryrun_compare_v0282.py"
    $PyResult = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_write_dryrun_compare_result_v0282.json"

    $InsertCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRun_InsertRows.csv"
    $UpdateCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRun_UpdateRows.csv"
    $UnchangedCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRun_UnchangedRows.csv"
    $ConflictCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRun_ConflictRows.csv"
    $CandidateCopyCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_v0282_CandidateRows_Copy.csv"
    $QuarantineCopyCsv = Join-Path $PreviewDir "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DuplicateQuarantine_Copy.csv"
    $DryRunSummaryJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRunCompareSummary.json"
    $NextGateJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0283_BackupRollbackStagingPlan.json"
    $Top10Json = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_Top10_LocalFreeLibs_WriteDryRunCompare_v0282.json"

    $Py = @'
import json
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import pandas as pd

candidate_csv = Path(r"__CANDIDATE_CSV__")
quarantine_csv = Path(r"__QUARANTINE_CSV__")
canonical_list_json = Path(r"__CANONICAL_LIST_JSON__")
insert_csv = Path(r"__INSERT_CSV__")
update_csv = Path(r"__UPDATE_CSV__")
unchanged_csv = Path(r"__UNCHANGED_CSV__")
conflict_csv = Path(r"__CONFLICT_CSV__")
candidate_copy_csv = Path(r"__CANDIDATE_COPY_CSV__")
quarantine_copy_csv = Path(r"__QUARANTINE_COPY_CSV__")
summary_json = Path(r"__SUMMARY_JSON__")
next_gate_json = Path(r"__NEXT_GATE_JSON__")
out_json = Path(r"__OUT_JSON__")

for p in [insert_csv, update_csv, unchanged_csv, conflict_csv, candidate_copy_csv, quarantine_copy_csv, summary_json, next_gate_json, out_json]:
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

def read_csv(p):
    try:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str, encoding="utf-8-sig").fillna("")
    except Exception:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str).fillna("")

def read_json_records(p):
    try:
        obj = json.loads(Path(p).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if isinstance(obj, list):
            return pd.DataFrame(obj).fillna("")
        if isinstance(obj, dict):
            if "data" in obj and isinstance(obj["data"], list):
                return pd.DataFrame(obj["data"]).fillna("")
            return pd.DataFrame([obj]).fillna("")
    except Exception:
        pass
    return pd.DataFrame()

candidate = read_csv(candidate_csv)
quarantine = read_csv(quarantine_csv)

for c in ["family","symbol","date","value","source","canonical_key"]:
    if c not in candidate.columns:
        candidate[c] = ""

candidate.to_csv(candidate_copy_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
quarantine.to_csv(quarantine_copy_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

canonical_files = json.loads(canonical_list_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
existing_frames = []

for item in canonical_files:
    p = Path(item.get("path",""))
    if not p.exists():
        continue
    try:
        if p.suffix.lower() == ".csv":
            existing_frames.append(read_csv(p))
        elif p.suffix.lower() == ".json":
            existing_frames.append(read_json_records(p))
        elif p.suffix.lower() == ".parquet":
            try:
                existing_frames.append(pd.read_parquet(p).fillna("").astype(str))
            except Exception:
                pass
    except Exception:
        pass

if existing_frames:
    existing = pd.concat(existing_frames, ignore_index=True, sort=False).fillna("")
else:
    existing = pd.DataFrame()

for c in ["family","symbol","date","value","source","canonical_key"]:
    if c not in existing.columns:
        existing[c] = ""

if "canonical_key" not in existing.columns or existing.empty:
    existing_keys = set()
else:
    existing = existing.drop_duplicates(subset=["canonical_key"], keep="first").copy()
    existing_keys = set(existing["canonical_key"].astype(str).tolist())

candidate["_vdf_v0282_compare_policy"] = "DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY_NO_DB_WRITE"

insert_rows = []
update_rows = []
unchanged_rows = []
conflict_rows = []

existing_by_key = {}
if not existing.empty:
    for _, r in existing.iterrows():
        existing_by_key[str(r.get("canonical_key",""))] = r.to_dict()

for _, row in candidate.iterrows():
    r = row.to_dict()
    key = str(r.get("canonical_key",""))
    if not key:
        r["_vdf_v0282_delta_type"] = "conflict"
        r["_vdf_v0282_delta_reason"] = "empty_canonical_key"
        conflict_rows.append(r)
        continue

    if key not in existing_keys:
        r["_vdf_v0282_delta_type"] = "insert"
        r["_vdf_v0282_delta_reason"] = "key_not_found_in_existing"
        insert_rows.append(r)
    else:
        old = existing_by_key.get(key, {})
        old_value = str(old.get("value","")).strip()
        new_value = str(r.get("value","")).strip()
        old_symbol = str(old.get("symbol","")).strip()
        new_symbol = str(r.get("symbol","")).strip()
        old_date = str(old.get("date","")).strip()
        new_date = str(r.get("date","")).strip()
        old_source = str(old.get("source","")).strip()
        new_source = str(r.get("source","")).strip()

        if old_value == new_value and old_symbol == new_symbol and old_date == new_date and old_source == new_source:
            r["_vdf_v0282_delta_type"] = "unchanged"
            r["_vdf_v0282_delta_reason"] = "same_key_same_core_fields"
            unchanged_rows.append(r)
        else:
            r["_vdf_v0282_delta_type"] = "update"
            r["_vdf_v0282_delta_reason"] = "same_key_changed_core_fields"
            r["_vdf_v0282_existing_value"] = old_value
            r["_vdf_v0282_existing_symbol"] = old_symbol
            r["_vdf_v0282_existing_date"] = old_date
            r["_vdf_v0282_existing_source"] = old_source
            update_rows.append(r)

insert_df = pd.DataFrame(insert_rows)
update_df = pd.DataFrame(update_rows)
unchanged_df = pd.DataFrame(unchanged_rows)
conflict_df = pd.DataFrame(conflict_rows)

insert_df.to_csv(insert_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
update_df.to_csv(update_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
unchanged_df.to_csv(unchanged_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
conflict_df.to_csv(conflict_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

metrics = {
    "candidate_rows": int(len(candidate)),
    "duplicate_quarantine_rows": int(len(quarantine)),
    "existing_rows_loaded": int(len(existing)),
    "existing_files_loaded": int(len(existing_frames)),
    "insert_rows": int(len(insert_df)),
    "update_rows": int(len(update_df)),
    "unchanged_rows": int(len(unchanged_df)),
    "conflict_rows": int(len(conflict_df)),
    "db_write_enabled": False,
    "canonical_merge_enabled": False
}

if metrics["conflict_rows"] == 0 and metrics["candidate_rows"] > 0:
    status = "VDF_AKSHARE_WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_READY"
    risk = "MEDIUM" if metrics["insert_rows"] > 0 or metrics["update_rows"] > 0 else "LOW"
    recommendation = "ALLOW_V0283_BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY_NO_DB_WRITE"
    next_step = "v028.3 backup rollback staging plan only; no DB write"
else:
    status = "VDF_AKSHARE_WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_BLOCKED"
    risk = "HIGH"
    recommendation = "BLOCK_V0283_UNTIL_DRYRUN_CONFLICTS_REPAIRED"
    next_step = "repair dry-run conflicts; no DB write"

summary = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "policy": {
        "dryrun_compare_only": True,
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
        "actual_write_allowed": False,
        "duplicate_quarantine_excluded": True
    },
    "metrics": metrics,
    "artifacts": {
        "insert_csv": str(insert_csv),
        "update_csv": str(update_csv),
        "unchanged_csv": str(unchanged_csv),
        "conflict_csv": str(conflict_csv),
        "candidate_copy_csv": str(candidate_copy_csv),
        "duplicate_quarantine_copy_csv": str(quarantine_copy_csv)
    },
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "next_step": next_step
}
summary_json.write_text(json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

next_gate = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "stage": "v028.3",
    "title": "Backup rollback staging plan only",
    "allowed": status == "VDF_AKSHARE_WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_READY",
    "still_no_db_write": True,
    "still_no_canonical_merge": True,
    "required_before_any_future_write": [
        "full DATABASE backup",
        "target table snapshot",
        "staging table plan",
        "rollback plan",
        "post-write validation plan",
        "separate explicit write approval"
    ],
    "dryrun_metrics": metrics
}
next_gate_json.write_text(json.dumps(next_gate, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

result = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "metrics": metrics,
    "artifacts": summary["artifacts"] | {
        "summary_json": str(summary_json),
        "next_gate_json": str(next_gate_json)
    },
    "next_step": next_step
}
out_json.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
'@

    $Replace = @{
        "__CANDIDATE_CSV__" = $CandidateCsvPath
        "__QUARANTINE_CSV__" = $QuarantineCsvPath
        "__CANONICAL_LIST_JSON__" = $CanonicalListJson
        "__INSERT_CSV__" = $InsertCsv
        "__UPDATE_CSV__" = $UpdateCsv
        "__UNCHANGED_CSV__" = $UnchangedCsv
        "__CONFLICT_CSV__" = $ConflictCsv
        "__CANDIDATE_COPY_CSV__" = $CandidateCopyCsv
        "__QUARANTINE_COPY_CSV__" = $QuarantineCopyCsv
        "__SUMMARY_JSON__" = $DryRunSummaryJson
        "__NEXT_GATE_JSON__" = $NextGateJson
        "__OUT_JSON__" = $PyResult
    }

    foreach ($k in $Replace.Keys) {
        $Py = $Py.Replace($k, ($Replace[$k] -replace "\\","\\"))
    }

    Set-Content -LiteralPath $PyScript -Value $Py -Encoding UTF8

    $python = Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($null -eq $python) { throw "python not found in PATH" }

    $psi = [System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo]::new()
    $psi.FileName = $python.Source
    $psi.Arguments = "`"$PyScript`""
    $psi.UseShellExecute = $false
    $psi.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
    $psi.RedirectStandardError = $true
    $psi.CreateNoWindow = $true

    $p = [System.Diagnostics.Process]::Start($psi)
    $null = $p.WaitForExit(180000)
    $stdout = $p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd()
    $stderr = $p.StandardError.ReadToEnd()

    if ($p.ExitCode -ne 0 -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PyResult)) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DRYRUN_COMPARE" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "FAIL" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "exit_code" "$($p.ExitCode)" $stderr $PyScript
        throw "Python failed: $stderr"
    }

    $Result = Get-Content -LiteralPath $PyResult -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $M = $Result.metrics

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DRYRUN_COMPARE" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "OK_EXECUTED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "stdout" "ok" $stdout $PyScript
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DRYRUN_COMPARE" "CANDIDATE_ROWS" "Candidate Rows" "OK_LOADED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "candidate_rows" "$($M.candidate_rows)" "Candidate rows loaded for dry-run compare." $CandidateCopyCsv

    if ([int]$M.existing_files_loaded -eq 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DRYRUN_COMPARE" "EXISTING_CANONICAL" "Existing Canonical" "WARN_EMPTY_EXISTING" "MEDIUM" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "existing_files_loaded" "0" "No existing canonical file loaded; all rows classified as insert." $CanonicalInventoryCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DRYRUN_COMPARE" "EXISTING_CANONICAL" "Existing Canonical" "OK_LOADED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "existing_rows_loaded" "$($M.existing_rows_loaded)" "Existing canonical rows loaded." $CanonicalInventoryCsv
    }

    if ([int]$M.conflict_rows -eq 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DRYRUN_COMPARE" "CONFLICT_ROWS" "Conflict Rows" "OK_ZERO_CONFLICT" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "conflict_rows" "0" "No dry-run conflict." $ConflictCsv
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DRYRUN_COMPARE" "CONFLICT_ROWS" "Conflict Rows" "BLOCK_CONFLICTS" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "conflict_rows" "$($M.conflict_rows)" "Dry-run conflicts remain." $ConflictCsv
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DRYRUN_COMPARE" "INSERT_ROWS" "Insert Rows" "OK_DELTA_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "insert_rows" "$($M.insert_rows)" "Insert delta generated." $InsertCsv
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DRYRUN_COMPARE" "UPDATE_ROWS" "Update Rows" "OK_DELTA_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "update_rows" "$($M.update_rows)" "Update delta generated." $UpdateCsv
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DRYRUN_COMPARE" "UNCHANGED_ROWS" "Unchanged Rows" "OK_DELTA_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "unchanged_rows" "$($M.unchanged_rows)" "Unchanged delta generated." $UnchangedCsv
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "DRYRUN_COMPARE" "DUPLICATE_QUARANTINE" "Duplicate Quarantine" "WARN_QUARANTINE_EXCLUDED" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "duplicate_quarantine_rows" "$($M.duplicate_quarantine_rows)" "Duplicate quarantine excluded from dry-run write candidate." $QuarantineCopyCsv

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "DRYRUN_SUMMARY" "Dry-run Compare Summary" "OK_SUMMARY_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "summary" "created" "Dry-run compare summary generated." $DryRunSummaryJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "NEXT_GATE" "v028.3 Next Gate" "OK_PLAN_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "next" "$($Result.next_step)" "v028.3 backup rollback staging plan generated." $NextGateJson

    $Top10Libs = @(
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Dry-run Compare"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="duckdb; pandas; polars; pyarrow; datacompy; deepdiff; pydantic; pandera; pytest; rich"; UseCase="insert/update/conflict/unchanged compare"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Backup Rollback Staging Plan"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="duckdb; pandas; pyarrow; polars; hashlib; pathlib; pydantic; pandera; pytest; rich"; UseCase="future staging/backup/rollback validation"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="PowerShell Orchestration"; Language="PowerShell"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Pester; PSScriptAnalyzer; Microsoft.PowerShell.ThreadJob; ImportExcel; PSWriteHTML; ScheduledTasks; Start-Transcript; ConvertTo-Json; Get-FileHash; Write-Progress"; UseCase="test/debug/report/audit/hash/progress"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="HTML UI Report"; Language="JS/HTML"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Tabulator; DataTables; ECharts; Chart.js; Mermaid; Ajv; DOMPurify; FileSaver.js; Playwright; Lighthouse"; UseCase="matrix/smoke/chart/schema/export"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="VRN Integration Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pdfplumber; PyMuPDF; pdfminer.six; pypdf; camelot; tabula-py; opencv-python; pillow; pandas; pyarrow"; UseCase="future PDF/report extraction alignment"}
    )
    $Top10Libs | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $Top10Json -Encoding UTF8
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "LOCAL_FREE_LIBS" "Top10 Local Free Libs" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P2" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "rows" "$(@($Top10Libs).Count)" "Top10 local free libs matrix generated." $Top10Json

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "RESULT" "READINESS" $Result.status $(if ($Result.risk -eq "LOW") { "OK_READY" } elseif ($Result.risk -eq "MEDIUM") { "WARN_REVIEW_READY" } else { "BLOCKED" }) $Result.risk "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "recommendation" $Result.recommendation "v028.2 completed. Compare only. No DB write. No canonical merge." $PyResult

    $CsvMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_WriteDryRunCompare_Matrix_v0282.csv"
    $JsonMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_WriteDryRunCompare_Matrix_v0282.json"
    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    try {
        $ptr = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ActivePointer) {
            Get-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
        } else {
            [pscustomobject]@{}
        }

        $ptr | Add-Member -NotePropertyName "latest_akshare_write_dryrun_compare_v0282" -NotePropertyValue ([pscustomobject]@{
            generated_at = def_Iso
            status = $Result.status
            risk = $Result.risk
            run_root = $RunRoot
            result_json = $PyResult
            dryrun_summary_json = $DryRunSummaryJson
            insert_csv = $InsertCsv
            update_csv = $UpdateCsv
            unchanged_csv = $UnchangedCsv
            conflict_csv = $ConflictCsv
            candidate_rows = $M.candidate_rows
            insert_rows = $M.insert_rows
            update_rows = $M.update_rows
            unchanged_rows = $M.unchanged_rows
            conflict_rows = $M.conflict_rows
            recommendation = $Result.recommendation
        }) -Force

        $ptr | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Encoding UTF8
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "POINTER" "ACTIVE_POINTER" "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json" "OK_UPDATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_ONLY" "update" "complete" "Active pointer updated with v028.2." $ActivePointer
    } catch {}

    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    $trs = foreach ($r in $Rows) {
        $cls = if ($r.Risk -eq "HIGH" -or $r.Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { "bad" }
               elseif ($r.Risk -match "MEDIUM|REVIEW" -or $r.Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|CONFLICT|QUARANTINE") { "warn" }
               else { "ok" }
        "<tr class='$cls'><td>$(def_Html $r.Round)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Stage)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Layer)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Name)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Status)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Risk)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.FixClass)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Priority)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Mode)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Metric)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Value)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Message)</td><td>$(def_Html $r.Path)</td></tr>"
    }

    $HtmlReport = Join-Path $ReportDir "VDF_AkShare_WriteDryRunCompare_Report_v0282.html"
    $Html = @"

PS7 function Invoke-VDF-AkShareBackupRollbackStagingPlan-v0283 {
    param(
        [string]$ProjectRoot = "C:\Users\tonyk\Downloads\VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics",
        [switch]$OpenHtmlReport = $true
    )

    $script:OK = 0
    $script:WARN = 0
    $script:FAIL = 0

    function def_Stamp { Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss" }
    function def_Iso { (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffK") }
    function def_Mkdir([string]$p) { [System.IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($p) | Out-Null }
    function def_Html([AllowNull()][string]$s) {
        if ($null -eq $s) { return "" }
        return [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode($s)
    }

    function def_AddRow {
        param(
            [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]$Rows,
            [string]$Round,[string]$Stage,[string]$Layer,[string]$Name,
            [string]$Status,[string]$Risk,[string]$FixClass,[string]$Priority,
            [string]$Mode,[string]$Metric,[string]$Value,[string]$Message,[string]$Path
        )

        if ($Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { $script:FAIL++ }
        elseif ($Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|MEDIUM|MISSING|EMPTY|QUARANTINE|APPROVAL") { $script:WARN++ }
        else { $script:OK++ }

        $Rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Round=$Round; Stage=$Stage; Layer=$Layer; Name=$Name; Status=$Status; Risk=$Risk
            FixClass=$FixClass; Priority=$Priority; Mode=$Mode; Metric=$Metric; Value=$Value
            Message=$Message; Path=$Path
        }) | Out-Null
    }

    function def_SafeHash {
        param([string]$Path,[string]$ExcludeRoot)
        try {
            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Path) -or -not [System.IO.File]::Exists($Path)) { return "" }
            $full = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Path)
            $root = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($ExcludeRoot)
            if ($full.StartsWith($root,[System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { return "SKIPPED_CURRENT_RUNROOT" }
            return (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256 -ErrorAction Stop).Hash
        } catch [System.IO.IOException] {
            return "LOCKED_OR_BUSY"
        } catch {
            return "HASH_ERROR"
        }
    }

    if (-not [System.IO.Directory]::Exists($ProjectRoot)) {
        throw "ProjectRoot not found: $ProjectRoot"
    }

    $Stamp = def_Stamp
    $DictRoot     = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "dict\VDF"
    $ActiveRoot   = Join-Path $DictRoot "_active"
    $FreezeRoot   = Join-Path $DictRoot "_freeze"
    $BackupRoot   = Join-Path $DictRoot "_backup"
    $DatabaseRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "DATABASE"

    foreach ($d in @($DictRoot,$ActiveRoot,$FreezeRoot,$BackupRoot,$DatabaseRoot)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Latest = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ActiveRoot -Directory -Filter "VDF_AKSHARE_WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_v0282_*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $Latest) {
        throw "Latest v028.2 write dry-run compare run not found."
    }

    $RunRoot = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_AKSHARE_BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_v0283_$Stamp"
    $ReportDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "report"
    $RegistryDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "registry"
    $PlanDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "plan"
    $RuntimeDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "runtime"
    $BackupDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "backup"
    $StagingPlanDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "staging_plan"

    foreach ($d in @($RunRoot,$ReportDir,$RegistryDir,$PlanDir,$RuntimeDir,$BackupDir,$StagingPlanDir)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Rows = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    $ActivePointer = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json"
    $Transcript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "VDF_AkShare_BackupRollbackStagingPlan_Transcript_v0283_$Stamp.txt"

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "def VDF AKSHARE BACKUP ROLLBACK STAGING PLAN v028.3" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "VERITAS INTELLIGENCE ANALYTICS"
    Write-Host "Plan only · backup/rollback/staging design · no DATABASE write · no canonical merge"
    Write-Host ""

    try { Start-Transcript -LiteralPath $Transcript -Force | Out-Null } catch {}

    if ([System.IO.File]::Exists($ActivePointer)) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Destination (Join-Path $BackupDir "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER_before_v0283_$Stamp.json") -Force
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "BOOT" "PROJECT_ROOT" "VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "exists" "true" "Project root exists." $ProjectRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_DB_WRITE" "DATABASE" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "db_write_enabled" "false" "No DATABASE write in v028.3." $DatabaseRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_CANONICAL_MERGE" "Canonical Merge" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "canonical_merge_enabled" "false" "No canonical merge in v028.3." $RunRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "PLAN_ONLY" "Staging Plan" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "actual_write_allowed" "false" "This stage creates plan only; actual write still forbidden." $RunRoot

    $DryRunSummaryJson = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRunCompareSummary.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $InsertCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRun_InsertRows.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $UpdateCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRun_UpdateRows.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $ConflictCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRun_ConflictRows.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $CandidateCopyCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_CandidateRows_Copy.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $QuarantineCopyCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DuplicateQuarantine_Copy.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    foreach ($x in @($DryRunSummaryJson,$InsertCsv,$UpdateCsv,$ConflictCsv,$CandidateCopyCsv,$QuarantineCopyCsv)) {
        if ($null -eq $x) { throw "Missing required v028.2 artifact for v028.3 plan." }
    }

    $DryRun = Get-Content -LiteralPath $DryRunSummaryJson.FullName -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $M = $DryRun.metrics

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "DRYRUN_SUMMARY" "v028.2 Dry-run Summary" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "source" "dryrun_summary" "Locked v028.2 dry-run summary." $DryRunSummaryJson.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "INSERT_ROWS" "v028.2 Insert Rows" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "insert_rows" "$($M.insert_rows)" "Locked insert delta source." $InsertCsv.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "CONFLICT_ROWS" "v028.2 Conflict Rows" $(if ([int]$M.conflict_rows -eq 0) { "OK_ZERO_CONFLICT" } else { "BLOCK_CONFLICTS" }) $(if ([int]$M.conflict_rows -eq 0) { "LOW" } else { "HIGH" }) "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "conflict_rows" "$($M.conflict_rows)" "Conflict row status checked." $ConflictCsv.FullName

    $PyScript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_backup_rollback_staging_plan_v0283.py"
    $PyResult = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_backup_rollback_staging_plan_result_v0283.json"

    $DbInventoryCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v0283_DatabaseInventory.csv"
    $BackupPlanJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0283_BackupPlan.json"
    $RollbackPlanJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0283_RollbackPlan.json"
    $StagingPlanJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0283_StagingWritePlan.json"
    $PostWriteValidationJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0283_PostWriteValidationPlan.json"
    $ApprovalGateJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0283_WriteApprovalGate.json"
    $PreWriteSafetyChecklistCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v0283_PreWriteSafetyChecklist.csv"
    $Top10Json = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_Top10_LocalFreeLibs_BackupRollbackStagingPlan_v0283.json"

    $DbInventory = foreach ($f in (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $DatabaseRoot -Recurse -File -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Sort-Object FullName)) {
        [pscustomobject]@{
            name = $f.Name
            path = $f.FullName
            extension = $f.Extension
            bytes = $f.Length
            modified = $f.LastWriteTime.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
            sha256 = def_SafeHash $f.FullName $RunRoot
        }
    }
    $DbInventory | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $DbInventoryCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM

    $Py = @'
import json
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import pandas as pd

dryrun_summary_json = Path(r"__DRYRUN_SUMMARY_JSON__")
insert_csv = Path(r"__INSERT_CSV__")
update_csv = Path(r"__UPDATE_CSV__")
conflict_csv = Path(r"__CONFLICT_CSV__")
candidate_csv = Path(r"__CANDIDATE_CSV__")
quarantine_csv = Path(r"__QUARANTINE_CSV__")
db_inventory_csv = Path(r"__DB_INVENTORY_CSV__")
backup_plan_json = Path(r"__BACKUP_PLAN_JSON__")
rollback_plan_json = Path(r"__ROLLBACK_PLAN_JSON__")
staging_plan_json = Path(r"__STAGING_PLAN_JSON__")
postwrite_validation_json = Path(r"__POSTWRITE_VALIDATION_JSON__")
approval_gate_json = Path(r"__APPROVAL_GATE_JSON__")
checklist_csv = Path(r"__CHECKLIST_CSV__")
out_json = Path(r"__OUT_JSON__")

for p in [backup_plan_json, rollback_plan_json, staging_plan_json, postwrite_validation_json, approval_gate_json, checklist_csv, out_json]:
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

def read_csv(p):
    try:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str, encoding="utf-8-sig").fillna("")
    except Exception:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str).fillna("")

dryrun = json.loads(dryrun_summary_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
metrics = dict(dryrun.get("metrics", {}))

insert_df = read_csv(insert_csv)
update_df = read_csv(update_csv)
conflict_df = read_csv(conflict_csv)
candidate_df = read_csv(candidate_csv)
quarantine_df = read_csv(quarantine_csv)
db_inv = read_csv(db_inventory_csv)

plan_metrics = {
    "candidate_rows": int(len(candidate_df)),
    "insert_rows": int(len(insert_df)),
    "update_rows": int(len(update_df)),
    "conflict_rows": int(len(conflict_df)),
    "duplicate_quarantine_rows": int(len(quarantine_df)),
    "database_inventory_files": int(len(db_inv)),
    "db_write_enabled": False,
    "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
    "actual_write_allowed": False
}

ready = (
    plan_metrics["candidate_rows"] > 0 and
    plan_metrics["conflict_rows"] == 0 and
    plan_metrics["insert_rows"] + plan_metrics["update_rows"] + int(metrics.get("unchanged_rows",0)) == plan_metrics["candidate_rows"]
)

status = "VDF_AKSHARE_BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_READY" if ready else "VDF_AKSHARE_BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_BLOCKED"
risk = "MEDIUM" if ready else "HIGH"
recommendation = "ALLOW_V0284_USER_APPROVAL_FOR_STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_NO_DB_WRITE" if ready else "BLOCK_V0284_UNTIL_STAGING_PLAN_DEFECTS_REPAIRED"
next_step = "v028.4 user approval for staging-write script generation only; no DB write" if ready else "repair staging plan defects; no DB write"

backup_plan = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "title": "Full DATABASE backup plan before any future write",
    "policy": {
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
        "actual_backup_execution": False,
        "backup_plan_only": True
    },
    "steps": [
        "Create timestamped DATABASE backup folder under dict/VDF/_backup.",
        "Copy all existing DATABASE files into backup folder.",
        "Hash every copied file.",
        "Verify backup file count equals source file count.",
        "Abort future write if backup hash manifest has any failure."
    ],
    "metrics": plan_metrics
}
backup_plan_json.write_text(json.dumps(backup_plan, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

rollback_plan = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "title": "Rollback plan before any future write",
    "policy": {
        "rollback_required": True,
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False
    },
    "rollback_steps": [
        "Do not overwrite original database files directly.",
        "Write candidate data to staging table/file first.",
        "Validate staging table row count, key uniqueness, required fields, and hashes.",
        "Only after validation may future approved write promote staging artifact.",
        "If validation fails, restore from backup manifest."
    ]
}
rollback_plan_json.write_text(json.dumps(rollback_plan, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

staging_plan = {

        "update_csv": str(update_csv),

        "duplicate_quarantine_csv": str(quarantine_csv)
    },
    "staging_outputs_for_future_stage": [
        "VDF_Global_Commodity_staging.parquet",
        "VDF_Global_Commodity_staging.csv",
        "VDF_Global_Commodity_write_delta.json",
        "post_write_validation_report.html"
    ],
    "metrics": plan_metrics
}
staging_plan_json.write_text(json.dumps(staging_plan, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

postwrite_validation = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "title": "Post-write validation plan for future approved write",
    "policy": {
        "validation_plan_only": True,
        "db_write_enabled": False
    },
    "checks": [

        "Primary key uniqueness is zero duplicate.",

    ]

postwrite_validation_json.write_text(json.dumps(postwrite_validation, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

approval_gate = {

    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "approval_required": True,
    "approved_now": False,
    "what_user_may_approve_next": "v028.4 generate staging-write script only; still no DB write",

    "metrics": plan_metrics,

approval_gate_json.write_text(json.dumps(approval_gate, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

checklist = [
    {"item":"candidate_rows_gt_0","status":"OK" if plan_metrics["candidate_rows"] > 0 else "FAIL"},
    {"item":"conflict_rows_zero","status":"OK" if plan_metrics["conflict_rows"] == 0 else "FAIL"},
    {"item":"duplicate_quarantine_excluded","status":"OK"},
    {"item":"backup_plan_created","status":"OK"},
    {"item":"rollback_plan_created","status":"OK"},

result = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "metrics": plan_metrics,
    "artifacts": {
        "backup_plan_json": str(backup_plan_json),

}
out_json.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
'@

    $Replace = @{
        "__DRYRUN_SUMMARY_JSON__" = $DryRunSummaryJson.FullName
        "__INSERT_CSV__" = $InsertCsv.FullName
        "__UPDATE_CSV__" = $UpdateCsv.FullName
        "__CONFLICT_CSV__" = $ConflictCsv.FullName
        "__CANDIDATE_CSV__" = $CandidateCopyCsv.FullName
        "__QUARANTINE_CSV__" = $QuarantineCopyCsv.FullName
        "__DB_INVENTORY_CSV__" = $DbInventoryCsv
        "__BACKUP_PLAN_JSON__" = $BackupPlanJson
        "__ROLLBACK_PLAN_JSON__" = $RollbackPlanJson
        "__STAGING_PLAN_JSON__" = $StagingPlanJson
        "__POSTWRITE_VALIDATION_JSON__" = $PostWriteValidationJson
        "__APPROVAL_GATE_JSON__" = $ApprovalGateJson
        "__CHECKLIST_CSV__" = $PreWriteSafetyChecklistCsv
        "__OUT_JSON__" = $PyResult
    }

    foreach ($k in $Replace.Keys) {
        $Py = $Py.Replace($k, ($Replace[$k] -replace "\\","\\"))
    }

    Set-Content -LiteralPath $PyScript -Value $Py -Encoding UTF8

    $python = Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($null -eq $python) { throw "python not found in PATH" }

    $psi = [System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo]::new()
    $psi.FileName = $python.Source
    $psi.Arguments = "`"$PyScript`""
    $psi.UseShellExecute = $false
    $psi.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
    $psi.RedirectStandardError = $true
    $psi.CreateNoWindow = $true

    $p = [System.Diagnostics.Process]::Start($psi)
    $null = $p.WaitForExit(180000)
    $stdout = $p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd()
    $stderr = $p.StandardError.ReadToEnd()

    if ($p.ExitCode -ne 0 -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PyResult)) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "FAIL" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "exit_code" "$($p.ExitCode)" $stderr $PyScript
        throw "Python failed: $stderr"
    }

    $Result = Get-Content -LiteralPath $PyResult -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $R = $Result.metrics

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "OK_EXECUTED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "stdout" "ok" $stdout $PyScript
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "CANDIDATE_ROWS" "Candidate Rows" "OK_READY_SOURCE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "candidate_rows" "$($R.candidate_rows)" "Candidate rows verified for staging plan." $CandidateCopyCsv.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "INSERT_ROWS" "Insert Rows" "OK_INSERT_PLAN" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "insert_rows" "$($R.insert_rows)" "Insert rows included in staging plan." $InsertCsv.FullName

    if ([int]$R.conflict_rows -eq 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "CONFLICT_ROWS" "Conflict Rows" "OK_ZERO_CONFLICT" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "conflict_rows" "0" "No conflict rows." $ConflictCsv.FullName
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "CONFLICT_ROWS" "Conflict Rows" "BLOCK_CONFLICTS" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "conflict_rows" "$($R.conflict_rows)" "Conflict rows block staging plan." $ConflictCsv.FullName
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "BACKUP_PLAN" "Backup Plan JSON" "OK_PLAN_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "plan" "created" "Backup plan created. No backup executed." $BackupPlanJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "ROLLBACK_PLAN" "Rollback Plan JSON" "OK_PLAN_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "plan" "created" "Rollback plan created." $RollbackPlanJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "STAGING_PLAN" "Staging Write Plan JSON" "OK_PLAN_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "plan" "created" "Staging write plan created. No staging write executed." $StagingPlanJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "POSTWRITE_VALIDATION" "Post-write Validation Plan JSON" "OK_PLAN_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "plan" "created" "Post-write validation plan created." $PostWriteValidationJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "APPROVAL_GATE" "Approval Gate JSON" "WARN_APPROVAL_REQUIRED" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "approved_now" "false" "Future stage approval still required." $ApprovalGateJson

    $Top10Libs = @(
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Backup Rollback Staging Plan"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="duckdb; pandas; pyarrow; polars; hashlib; pathlib; pydantic; pandera; pytest; rich"; UseCase="future staging/backup/rollback validation"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Staging Write Script Generation"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="duckdb; pandas; polars; pyarrow; pydantic; pandera; pytest; hypothesis; orjson; rich"; UseCase="generate script/staging table/future safe write"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="PowerShell Orchestration"; Language="PowerShell"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Pester; PSScriptAnalyzer; Microsoft.PowerShell.ThreadJob; ImportExcel; PSWriteHTML; ScheduledTasks; Start-Transcript; ConvertTo-Json; Get-FileHash; Write-Progress"; UseCase="test/debug/report/audit/hash/progress"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="HTML UI Report"; Language="JS/HTML"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Tabulator; DataTables; ECharts; Chart.js; Mermaid; Ajv; DOMPurify; FileSaver.js; Playwright; Lighthouse"; UseCase="matrix/smoke/chart/schema/export"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="VRN Integration Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pdfplumber; PyMuPDF; pdfminer.six; pypdf; camelot; tabula-py; opencv-python; pillow; pandas; pyarrow"; UseCase="future PDF/report extraction alignment"}
    )
    $Top10Libs | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $Top10Json -Encoding UTF8
