PS7 function Invoke-VDF-AkShareBackupRollbackStagingPlan-Hotfix-v02831 {
    param(
        [string]$ProjectRoot = "C:\Users\tonyk\Downloads\VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics",
        [switch]$OpenHtmlReport = $true
    )

    $script:OK = 0
    $script:WARN = 0
    $script:FAIL = 0

    function def_Stamp { Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss" }
    function def_Iso { (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffK") }
    function def_Mkdir([string]$p) { [System.IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($p) | Out-Null }
    function def_Html([AllowNull()][string]$s) {
        if ($null -eq $s) { return "" }
        return [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode($s)
    }

    function def_AddRow {
        param(
            [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]$Rows,
            [string]$Round,[string]$Stage,[string]$Layer,[string]$Name,
            [string]$Status,[string]$Risk,[string]$FixClass,[string]$Priority,
            [string]$Mode,[string]$Metric,[string]$Value,[string]$Message,[string]$Path
        )

        if ($Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { $script:FAIL++ }
        elseif ($Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|MEDIUM|MISSING|EMPTY|QUARANTINE|APPROVAL") { $script:WARN++ }
        else { $script:OK++ }

        $Rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Round=$Round; Stage=$Stage; Layer=$Layer; Name=$Name; Status=$Status; Risk=$Risk
            FixClass=$FixClass; Priority=$Priority; Mode=$Mode; Metric=$Metric; Value=$Value
            Message=$Message; Path=$Path
        }) | Out-Null
    }

    function def_SafeHash {
        param([string]$Path,[string]$ExcludeRoot)
        try {
            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Path) -or -not [System.IO.File]::Exists($Path)) { return "" }
            $full = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Path)
            $root = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($ExcludeRoot)
            if ($full.StartsWith($root,[System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { return "SKIPPED_CURRENT_RUNROOT" }
            return (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256 -ErrorAction Stop).Hash
        } catch [System.IO.IOException] {
            return "LOCKED_OR_BUSY"
        } catch {
            return "HASH_ERROR"
        }
    }

    if (-not [System.IO.Directory]::Exists($ProjectRoot)) {
        throw "ProjectRoot not found: $ProjectRoot"
    }

    $Stamp = def_Stamp
    $DictRoot     = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "dict\VDF"
    $ActiveRoot   = Join-Path $DictRoot "_active"
    $BackupRoot   = Join-Path $DictRoot "_backup"
    $DatabaseRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "DATABASE"

    foreach ($d in @($DictRoot,$ActiveRoot,$BackupRoot,$DatabaseRoot)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Latest = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ActiveRoot -Directory -Filter "VDF_AKSHARE_WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_v0282_*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $Latest) {
        throw "Latest v028.2 write dry-run compare run not found."
    }

    $RunRoot = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_AKSHARE_BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_HOTFIX_v02831_$Stamp"
    $ReportDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "report"
    $RegistryDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "registry"
    $PlanDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "plan"
    $RuntimeDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "runtime"
    $BackupDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "backup"

    foreach ($d in @($RunRoot,$ReportDir,$RegistryDir,$PlanDir,$RuntimeDir,$BackupDir)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Rows = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    $ActivePointer = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json"
    $Transcript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "VDF_AkShare_BackupRollbackStagingPlan_Hotfix_Transcript_v02831_$Stamp.txt"

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "def VDF AKSHARE BACKUP ROLLBACK STAGING PLAN HOTFIX v028.3.1" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "VERITAS INTELLIGENCE ANALYTICS"
    Write-Host "Fix EmptyDataError on zero-row delta CSV · plan only · no DATABASE write · no canonical merge"
    Write-Host ""

    try { Start-Transcript -LiteralPath $Transcript -Force | Out-Null } catch {}

    if ([System.IO.File]::Exists($ActivePointer)) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Destination (Join-Path $BackupDir "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER_before_v02831_$Stamp.json") -Force
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "BOOT" "PROJECT_ROOT" "VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "exists" "true" "Project root exists." $ProjectRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_DB_WRITE" "DATABASE" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "db_write_enabled" "false" "No DATABASE write in v028.3.1." $DatabaseRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_CANONICAL_MERGE" "Canonical Merge" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "canonical_merge_enabled" "false" "No canonical merge in v028.3.1." $RunRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "HOTFIX" "EMPTY_CSV_READER" "Pandas EmptyDataError" "OK_HOTFIX_APPLIED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "fix" "empty_safe_read_csv" "Zero-row/empty CSV now returns empty DataFrame." $RunRoot

    $DryRunSummaryJson = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRunCompareSummary.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $InsertCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRun_InsertRows.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $UpdateCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRun_UpdateRows.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $ConflictCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRun_ConflictRows.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $CandidateCopyCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_CandidateRows_Copy.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $QuarantineCopyCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DuplicateQuarantine_Copy.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    foreach ($x in @($DryRunSummaryJson,$InsertCsv,$UpdateCsv,$ConflictCsv,$CandidateCopyCsv,$QuarantineCopyCsv)) {
        if ($null -eq $x) { throw "Missing required v028.2 artifact for v028.3.1 hotfix plan." }
    }

    $DryRun = Get-Content -LiteralPath $DryRunSummaryJson.FullName -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $DM = $DryRun.metrics

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "DRYRUN_SUMMARY" "v028.2 Dry-run Summary" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "source" "dryrun_summary" "Locked v028.2 dry-run summary." $DryRunSummaryJson.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "INSERT_ROWS" "v028.2 Insert Rows" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "insert_rows" "$($DM.insert_rows)" "Locked insert delta source." $InsertCsv.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "UPDATE_ROWS_EMPTY_SAFE" "v028.2 Update Rows" "OK_EMPTY_SAFE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "update_rows" "$($DM.update_rows)" "Zero-row update CSV is acceptable." $UpdateCsv.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "CONFLICT_ROWS_EMPTY_SAFE" "v028.2 Conflict Rows" "OK_EMPTY_SAFE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "conflict_rows" "$($DM.conflict_rows)" "Zero-row conflict CSV is acceptable." $ConflictCsv.FullName

    $DbInventoryCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v02831_DatabaseInventory.csv"
    $DbInventory = foreach ($f in (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $DatabaseRoot -Recurse -File -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Sort-Object FullName)) {
        [pscustomobject]@{
            name = $f.Name
            path = $f.FullName
            extension = $f.Extension
            bytes = $f.Length
            modified = $f.LastWriteTime.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
            sha256 = def_SafeHash $f.FullName $RunRoot
        }
    }
    $DbInventory | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $DbInventoryCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM

    $PyScript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_backup_rollback_staging_plan_hotfix_v02831.py"
    $PyResult = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_backup_rollback_staging_plan_hotfix_result_v02831.json"

    $BackupPlanJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v02831_BackupPlan.json"
    $RollbackPlanJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v02831_RollbackPlan.json"
    $StagingPlanJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v02831_StagingWritePlan.json"
    $PostWriteValidationJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v02831_PostWriteValidationPlan.json"
    $ApprovalGateJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v02831_WriteApprovalGate.json"
    $PreWriteSafetyChecklistCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v02831_PreWriteSafetyChecklist.csv"
    $Top10Json = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_Top10_LocalFreeLibs_BackupRollbackStagingPlan_Hotfix_v02831.json"

import json
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import pandas as pd
from pandas.errors import EmptyDataError

dryrun_summary_json = Path(r"__DRYRUN_SUMMARY_JSON__")
insert_csv = Path(r"__INSERT_CSV__")
update_csv = Path(r"__UPDATE_CSV__")
conflict_csv = Path(r"__CONFLICT_CSV__")
candidate_csv = Path(r"__CANDIDATE_CSV__")
quarantine_csv = Path(r"__QUARANTINE_CSV__")
db_inventory_csv = Path(r"__DB_INVENTORY_CSV__")
backup_plan_json = Path(r"__BACKUP_PLAN_JSON__")
rollback_plan_json = Path(r"__ROLLBACK_PLAN_JSON__")
staging_plan_json = Path(r"__STAGING_PLAN_JSON__")
postwrite_validation_json = Path(r"__POSTWRITE_VALIDATION_JSON__")
approval_gate_json = Path(r"__APPROVAL_GATE_JSON__")
checklist_csv = Path(r"__CHECKLIST_CSV__")
out_json = Path(r"__OUT_JSON__")

for p in [backup_plan_json, rollback_plan_json, staging_plan_json, postwrite_validation_json, approval_gate_json, checklist_csv, out_json]:
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

def safe_read_csv(p, columns=None):
    p = Path(p)
    if columns is None:
        columns = []
    if not p.exists() or p.stat().st_size == 0:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=columns)
    try:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str, encoding="utf-8-sig").fillna("")
    except EmptyDataError:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=columns)
    except Exception:
        try:
            return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str).fillna("")
        except EmptyDataError:
            return pd.DataFrame(columns=columns)
        except Exception:
            return pd.DataFrame(columns=columns)

base_cols = ["family","symbol","date","value","source","canonical_key","is_valid"]

dryrun = json.loads(dryrun_summary_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
metrics = dict(dryrun.get("metrics", {}))

insert_df = safe_read_csv(insert_csv, base_cols)
update_df = safe_read_csv(update_csv, base_cols)
conflict_df = safe_read_csv(conflict_csv, base_cols)
candidate_df = safe_read_csv(candidate_csv, base_cols)
quarantine_df = safe_read_csv(quarantine_csv, base_cols)
db_inv = safe_read_csv(db_inventory_csv, ["name","path","extension","bytes","modified","sha256"])

plan_metrics = {
    "candidate_rows": int(len(candidate_df)),
    "insert_rows": int(len(insert_df)),
    "update_rows": int(len(update_df)),
    "conflict_rows": int(len(conflict_df)),
    "duplicate_quarantine_rows": int(len(quarantine_df)),
    "database_inventory_files": int(len(db_inv)),
    "db_write_enabled": False,
    "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
    "actual_write_allowed": False,
    "empty_csv_hotfix_applied": True
}

expected_total = plan_metrics["insert_rows"] + plan_metrics["update_rows"] + int(metrics.get("unchanged_rows", 0))
ready = (
    plan_metrics["candidate_rows"] > 0 and
    plan_metrics["conflict_rows"] == 0 and
    expected_total == plan_metrics["candidate_rows"]
)

status = "VDF_AKSHARE_BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_HOTFIX_READY" if ready else "VDF_AKSHARE_BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_HOTFIX_BLOCKED"
risk = "MEDIUM" if ready else "HIGH"
recommendation = "ALLOW_V0284_USER_APPROVAL_FOR_STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_NO_DB_WRITE" if ready else "BLOCK_V0284_UNTIL_STAGING_PLAN_DEFECTS_REPAIRED"
next_step = "v028.4 user approval for staging-write script generation only; no DB write" if ready else "repair staging plan defects; no DB write"

backup_plan = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "title": "Full DATABASE backup plan before any future write",
    "policy": {
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
        "actual_backup_execution": False,
        "backup_plan_only": True
    },
    "steps": [
        "Create timestamped DATABASE backup folder under dict/VDF/_backup.",
        "Copy all existing DATABASE files into backup folder.",
        "Hash every copied file.",
        "Verify backup file count equals source file count.",
        "Abort future write if backup hash manifest has any failure."
    ],
    "metrics": plan_metrics
}
backup_plan_json.write_text(json.dumps(backup_plan, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

rollback_plan = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "title": "Rollback plan before any future write",
    "policy": {
        "rollback_required": True,
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False
    },
    "rollback_steps": [
        "Do not overwrite original database files directly.",
        "Write candidate data to staging table/file first.",
        "Validate staging table row count, key uniqueness, required fields, and hashes.",
        "Only after validation may future approved write promote staging artifact.",
        "If validation fails, restore from backup manifest."
    ]
}
rollback_plan_json.write_text(json.dumps(rollback_plan, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

staging_plan = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "title": "Staging write plan only; no execution",
    "policy": {
        "staging_table_required": True,
        "target_family": "VDF_Global_Commodity",
        "primary_key": ["family","symbol","date","source"],
        "exclude_duplicate_quarantine": True,
        "db_write_enabled": False,
        "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
        "actual_write_allowed": False
    },
    "candidate_inputs": {
        "insert_csv": str(insert_csv),
        "update_csv": str(update_csv),
        "candidate_csv": str(candidate_csv),
        "duplicate_quarantine_csv": str(quarantine_csv)
    },
    "staging_outputs_for_future_stage": [
        "VDF_Global_Commodity_staging.parquet",
        "VDF_Global_Commodity_staging.csv",
        "VDF_Global_Commodity_write_delta.json",
        "post_write_validation_report.html"
    ],
    "metrics": plan_metrics
}
staging_plan_json.write_text(json.dumps(staging_plan, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

postwrite_validation = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "title": "Post-write validation plan for future approved write",
    "policy": {
        "validation_plan_only": True,
        "db_write_enabled": False
    },
    "checks": [
        "Row count after write equals existing + insert rows with update keys preserved.",
        "Primary key uniqueness is zero duplicate.",
        "Required fields family/symbol/date/value/source/canonical_key are non-empty.",

postwrite_validation_json.write_text(json.dumps(postwrite_validation, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

approval_gate = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "approval_required": True,
    "approved_now": False,
    "what_user_may_approve_next": "v028.4 generate staging-write script only; still no DB write",
    "what_is_not_approved": "running staging write or promoting canonical target",
    "metrics": plan_metrics,
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "next_step": next_step
}
approval_gate_json.write_text(json.dumps(approval_gate, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

checklist = [
    {"item":"candidate_rows_gt_0","status":"OK" if plan_metrics["candidate_rows"] > 0 else "FAIL"},
    {"item":"conflict_rows_zero","status":"OK" if plan_metrics["conflict_rows"] == 0 else "FAIL"},
    {"item":"delta_rows_match_candidate_rows","status":"OK" if expected_total == plan_metrics["candidate_rows"] else "FAIL"},
    {"item":"empty_csv_hotfix_applied","status":"OK"},
    {"item":"duplicate_quarantine_excluded","status":"OK"},
    {"item":"backup_plan_created","status":"OK"},
    {"item":"rollback_plan_created","status":"OK"},

result = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "recommendation": recommendation,
    "metrics": plan_metrics,
    "artifacts": {
        "backup_plan_json": str(backup_plan_json),
        "rollback_plan_json": str(rollback_plan_json),
        "staging_plan_json": str(staging_plan_json),
        "postwrite_validation_json": str(postwrite_validation_json),
        "approval_gate_json": str(approval_gate_json),
        "prewrite_safety_checklist_csv": str(checklist_csv)
    },
    "next_step": next_step
}
out_json.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
'@

    $Replace = @{
        "__DRYRUN_SUMMARY_JSON__" = $DryRunSummaryJson.FullName
        "__INSERT_CSV__" = $InsertCsv.FullName
        "__UPDATE_CSV__" = $UpdateCsv.FullName
        "__CONFLICT_CSV__" = $ConflictCsv.FullName
        "__CANDIDATE_CSV__" = $CandidateCopyCsv.FullName
        "__QUARANTINE_CSV__" = $QuarantineCopyCsv.FullName
        "__DB_INVENTORY_CSV__" = $DbInventoryCsv
        "__BACKUP_PLAN_JSON__" = $BackupPlanJson
        "__ROLLBACK_PLAN_JSON__" = $RollbackPlanJson
        "__STAGING_PLAN_JSON__" = $StagingPlanJson
        "__POSTWRITE_VALIDATION_JSON__" = $PostWriteValidationJson
        "__APPROVAL_GATE_JSON__" = $ApprovalGateJson
        "__CHECKLIST_CSV__" = $PreWriteSafetyChecklistCsv
        "__OUT_JSON__" = $PyResult
    }

    foreach ($k in $Replace.Keys) {
        $Py = $Py.Replace($k, ($Replace[$k] -replace "\\","\\"))
    }

    Set-Content -LiteralPath $PyScript -Value $Py -Encoding UTF8

    $python = Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($null -eq $python) { throw "python not found in PATH" }

    $psi = [System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo]::new()
    $psi.FileName = $python.Source
    $psi.Arguments = "`"$PyScript`""
    $psi.UseShellExecute = $false
    $psi.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
    $psi.RedirectStandardError = $true
    $psi.CreateNoWindow = $true

    $p = [System.Diagnostics.Process]::Start($psi)
    $null = $p.WaitForExit(180000)
    $stdout = $p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd()
    $stderr = $p.StandardError.ReadToEnd()

    if ($p.ExitCode -ne 0 -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PyResult)) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "FAIL" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "exit_code" "$($p.ExitCode)" $stderr $PyScript
        throw "Python failed: $stderr"
    }

    $Result = Get-Content -LiteralPath $PyResult -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $R = $Result.metrics

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "OK_EXECUTED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "stdout" "ok" $stdout $PyScript
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "CANDIDATE_ROWS" "Candidate Rows" "OK_READY_SOURCE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "candidate_rows" "$($R.candidate_rows)" "Candidate rows verified for staging plan." $CandidateCopyCsv.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "INSERT_ROWS" "Insert Rows" "OK_INSERT_PLAN" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "insert_rows" "$($R.insert_rows)" "Insert rows included in staging plan." $InsertCsv.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "UPDATE_ROWS_EMPTY_SAFE" "Update Rows" "OK_EMPTY_SAFE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "update_rows" "$($R.update_rows)" "Zero-row update CSV handled safely." $UpdateCsv.FullName

    if ([int]$R.conflict_rows -eq 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "CONFLICT_ROWS" "Conflict Rows" "OK_ZERO_CONFLICT" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "conflict_rows" "0" "No conflict rows." $ConflictCsv.FullName
    } else {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "CONFLICT_ROWS" "Conflict Rows" "BLOCK_CONFLICTS" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "conflict_rows" "$($R.conflict_rows)" "Conflict rows block staging plan." $ConflictCsv.FullName
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "BACKUP_PLAN" "Backup Plan JSON" "OK_PLAN_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "plan" "created" "Backup plan created. No backup executed." $BackupPlanJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "ROLLBACK_PLAN" "Rollback Plan JSON" "OK_PLAN_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "plan" "created" "Rollback plan created." $RollbackPlanJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "STAGING_PLAN" "Staging Write Plan JSON" "OK_PLAN_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "plan" "created" "Staging write plan created. No staging write executed." $StagingPlanJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "POSTWRITE_VALIDATION" "Post-write Validation Plan JSON" "OK_PLAN_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "plan" "created" "Post-write validation plan created." $PostWriteValidationJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "APPROVAL_GATE" "Approval Gate JSON" "WARN_APPROVAL_REQUIRED" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "approved_now" "false" "Future stage approval still required." $ApprovalGateJson

    $Top10Libs = @(
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Backup Rollback Staging Plan"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="duckdb; pandas; pyarrow; polars; hashlib; pathlib; pydantic; pandera; pytest; rich"; UseCase="future staging/backup/rollback validation"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Staging Write Script Generation"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="duckdb; pandas; polars; pyarrow; pydantic; pandera; pytest; hypothesis; orjson; rich"; UseCase="generate script/staging table/future safe write"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="PowerShell Orchestration"; Language="PowerShell"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Pester; PSScriptAnalyzer; Microsoft.PowerShell.ThreadJob; ImportExcel; PSWriteHTML; ScheduledTasks; Start-Transcript; ConvertTo-Json; Get-FileHash; Write-Progress"; UseCase="test/debug/report/audit/hash/progress"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="HTML UI Report"; Language="JS/HTML"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Tabulator; DataTables; ECharts; Chart.js; Mermaid; Ajv; DOMPurify; FileSaver.js; Playwright; Lighthouse"; UseCase="matrix/smoke/chart/schema/export"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="VRN Integration Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pdfplumber; PyMuPDF; pdfminer.six; pypdf; camelot; tabula-py; opencv-python; pillow; pandas; pyarrow"; UseCase="future PDF/report extraction alignment"}
    )
    $Top10Libs | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $Top10Json -Encoding UTF8
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "LOCAL_FREE_LIBS" "Top10 Local Free Libs" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "rows" "$(@($Top10Libs).Count)" "Top10 local free libs matrix generated." $Top10Json

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "RESULT" "READINESS" $Result.status $(if ($Result.risk -eq "LOW") { "OK_READY" } elseif ($Result.risk -eq "MEDIUM") { "WARN_REVIEW_READY" } else { "BLOCKED" }) $Result.risk "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "recommendation" $Result.recommendation "v028.3.1 completed. Plan only. No DB write. No canonical merge." $PyResult

    $CsvMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_BackupRollbackStagingPlan_Hotfix_Matrix_v02831.csv"
    $JsonMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_BackupRollbackStagingPlan_Hotfix_Matrix_v02831.json"
    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    try {
        $ptr = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ActivePointer) {
            Get-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
        } else {
            [pscustomobject]@{}
        }

        $ptr | Add-Member -NotePropertyName "latest_akshare_backup_rollback_staging_plan_hotfix_v02831" -NotePropertyValue ([pscustomobject]@{
            generated_at = def_Iso
            status = $Result.status
            risk = $Result.risk
            run_root = $RunRoot
            result_json = $PyResult
            backup_plan_json = $BackupPlanJson
            rollback_plan_json = $RollbackPlanJson
            staging_plan_json = $StagingPlanJson
            postwrite_validation_json = $PostWriteValidationJson
            approval_gate_json = $ApprovalGateJson
            candidate_rows = $R.candidate_rows
            insert_rows = $R.insert_rows
            conflict_rows = $R.conflict_rows
            recommendation = $Result.recommendation
        }) -Force

        $ptr | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Encoding UTF8
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "POINTER" "ACTIVE_POINTER" "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json" "OK_UPDATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "update" "complete" "Active pointer updated with v028.3.1." $ActivePointer
    } catch {}

    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    }

    $HtmlReport = Join-Path $ReportDir "VDF_AkShare_BackupRollbackStagingPlan_Hotfix_Report_v02831.html"
    $Html = @"

PS7 function Invoke-VDF-AkShareBackupRollbackStagingPlan-Hotfix-v02832 {
    param(
        [string]$ProjectRoot = "C:\Users\tonyk\Downloads\VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics",
        [switch]$OpenHtmlReport = $true
    )

    $script:OK = 0
    $script:WARN = 0
    $script:FAIL = 0

    function def_Stamp { Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss" }
    function def_Iso { (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffK") }
    function def_Mkdir([string]$p) { [System.IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($p) | Out-Null }
    function def_Html([AllowNull()][string]$s) {
        if ($null -eq $s) { return "" }
        return [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode($s)
    }
    function def_GetProp {
        param([object]$Obj,[string]$Name,[object]$Default = 0)
        if ($null -eq $Obj) { return $Default }
        $p = $Obj.PSObject.Properties[$Name]
        if ($null -eq $p) { return $Default }
        if ($null -eq $p.Value -or "$($p.Value)" -eq "") { return $Default }
        return $p.Value
    }
    function def_AddRow {
        param(
            [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]$Rows,
            [string]$Round,[string]$Stage,[string]$Layer,[string]$Name,
            [string]$Status,[string]$Risk,[string]$FixClass,[string]$Priority,
            [string]$Mode,[string]$Metric,[string]$Value,[string]$Message,[string]$Path
        )
        if ($Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { $script:FAIL++ }
        elseif ($Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|MEDIUM|MISSING|EMPTY|QUARANTINE|APPROVAL|HOTFIX") { $script:WARN++ }
        else { $script:OK++ }

        $Rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Round=$Round; Stage=$Stage; Layer=$Layer; Name=$Name; Status=$Status; Risk=$Risk
            FixClass=$FixClass; Priority=$Priority; Mode=$Mode; Metric=$Metric; Value=$Value
            Message=$Message; Path=$Path
        }) | Out-Null
    }
    function def_SafeHash {
        param([string]$Path,[string]$ExcludeRoot)
        try {
            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Path) -or -not [System.IO.File]::Exists($Path)) { return "" }
            $full = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Path)
            $root = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($ExcludeRoot)
            if ($full.StartsWith($root,[System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { return "SKIPPED_CURRENT_RUNROOT" }
            return (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256 -ErrorAction Stop).Hash
        } catch [System.IO.IOException] {
            return "LOCKED_OR_BUSY"
        } catch {
            return "HASH_ERROR"
        }
    }

    $Stamp = def_Stamp
    $DictRoot     = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "dict\VDF"
    $ActiveRoot   = Join-Path $DictRoot "_active"
    $BackupRoot   = Join-Path $DictRoot "_backup"
    $DatabaseRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "DATABASE"

    foreach ($d in @($DictRoot,$ActiveRoot,$BackupRoot,$DatabaseRoot)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Latest = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ActiveRoot -Directory -Filter "VDF_AKSHARE_WRITE_DRYRUN_COMPARE_v0282_*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $Latest) {
        throw "Latest v028.2 write dry-run compare run not found."
    }

    $RunRoot = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_AKSHARE_BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_HOTFIX_v02832_$Stamp"
    $ReportDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "report"
    $RegistryDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "registry"
    $PlanDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "plan"
    $RuntimeDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "runtime"
    $BackupDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "backup"
    foreach ($d in @($RunRoot,$ReportDir,$RegistryDir,$PlanDir,$RuntimeDir,$BackupDir)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Rows = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    $ActivePointer = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json"
    $Transcript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "VDF_AkShare_BackupRollbackStagingPlan_Hotfix_Transcript_v02832_$Stamp.txt"

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "def VDF AKSHARE BACKUP ROLLBACK STAGING PLAN HOTFIX v028.3.2" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "Fix: Empty CSV + PowerShell metric safe accessor · no DATABASE write · no canonical merge"
    Write-Host ""

    try { Start-Transcript -LiteralPath $Transcript -Force | Out-Null } catch {}

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ActivePointer) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Destination (Join-Path $BackupDir "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER_before_v02832_$Stamp.json") -Force
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "BOOT" "PROJECT_ROOT" "VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "exists" "true" "Project root exists." $ProjectRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_DB_WRITE" "DATABASE" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "db_write_enabled" "false" "No DATABASE write in v028.3.2." $DatabaseRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_CANONICAL_MERGE" "Canonical Merge" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "canonical_merge_enabled" "false" "No canonical merge in v028.3.2." $RunRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "HOTFIX" "METRIC_SAFE_ACCESSOR" "PowerShell Metric Accessor" "OK_HOTFIX_APPLIED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "fix" "def_GetProp" "PowerShell metrics now read safely." $RunRoot

    $DryRunSummaryJson = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRunCompareSummary.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1
    $InsertCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRun_InsertRows.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1
    $UpdateCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRun_UpdateRows.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1
    $ConflictCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DryRun_ConflictRows.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1
    $CandidateCopyCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_CandidateRows_Copy.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1
    $QuarantineCopyCsv = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v0282_DuplicateQuarantine_Copy.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    foreach ($x in @($DryRunSummaryJson,$InsertCsv,$UpdateCsv,$ConflictCsv,$CandidateCopyCsv,$QuarantineCopyCsv)) {
        if ($null -eq $x) { throw "Missing required v028.2 artifact for v028.3.2 hotfix plan." }
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "DRYRUN_SUMMARY" "v028.2 Dry-run Summary" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "source" "dryrun_summary" "Locked v028.2 dry-run summary." $DryRunSummaryJson.FullName

    $DbInventoryCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v02832_DatabaseInventory.csv"
    $DbInventory = foreach ($f in (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $DatabaseRoot -Recurse -File -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Sort-Object FullName)) {
        [pscustomobject]@{
            name = $f.Name
            path = $f.FullName
            extension = $f.Extension
            bytes = $f.Length
            modified = $f.LastWriteTime.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
            sha256 = def_SafeHash $f.FullName $RunRoot
        }
    }
    $DbInventory | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $DbInventoryCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM

    $PyScript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_backup_rollback_staging_plan_hotfix_v02832.py"
    $PyResult = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_backup_rollback_staging_plan_hotfix_result_v02832.json"

    $BackupPlanJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v02832_BackupPlan.json"
    $RollbackPlanJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v02832_RollbackPlan.json"
    $StagingPlanJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v02832_StagingWritePlan.json"
    $PostWriteValidationJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v02832_PostWriteValidationPlan.json"
    $ApprovalGateJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v02832_WriteApprovalGate.json"
    $PreWriteSafetyChecklistCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v02832_PreWriteSafetyChecklist.csv"
    $Top10Json = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_Top10_LocalFreeLibs_BackupRollbackStagingPlan_Hotfix_v02832.json"

    $Py = @'
import json
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import pandas as pd
from pandas.errors import EmptyDataError

dryrun_summary_json = Path(r"__DRYRUN_SUMMARY_JSON__")
insert_csv = Path(r"__INSERT_CSV__")
update_csv = Path(r"__UPDATE_CSV__")
conflict_csv = Path(r"__CONFLICT_CSV__")
candidate_csv = Path(r"__CANDIDATE_CSV__")
quarantine_csv = Path(r"__QUARANTINE_CSV__")
db_inventory_csv = Path(r"__DB_INVENTORY_CSV__")
backup_plan_json = Path(r"__BACKUP_PLAN_JSON__")
rollback_plan_json = Path(r"__ROLLBACK_PLAN_JSON__")
staging_plan_json = Path(r"__STAGING_PLAN_JSON__")
postwrite_validation_json = Path(r"__POSTWRITE_VALIDATION_JSON__")
approval_gate_json = Path(r"__APPROVAL_GATE_JSON__")
checklist_csv = Path(r"__CHECKLIST_CSV__")
out_json = Path(r"__OUT_JSON__")

for p in [backup_plan_json, rollback_plan_json, staging_plan_json, postwrite_validation_json, approval_gate_json, checklist_csv, out_json]:
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

def safe_read_csv(p, columns=None):
    p = Path(p)
    if columns is None:
        columns = []
    if not p.exists() or p.stat().st_size == 0:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=columns)
    try:
        return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str, encoding="utf-8-sig").fillna("")
    except EmptyDataError:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=columns)
    except Exception:
        try:
            return pd.read_csv(p, dtype=str).fillna("")
        except EmptyDataError:
            return pd.DataFrame(columns=columns)
        except Exception:
            return pd.DataFrame(columns=columns)

base_cols = ["family","symbol","date","value","source","canonical_key","is_valid"]
dryrun = json.loads(dryrun_summary_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
dm = dict(dryrun.get("metrics", {}))

insert_df = safe_read_csv(insert_csv, base_cols)
update_df = safe_read_csv(update_csv, base_cols)
conflict_df = safe_read_csv(conflict_csv, base_cols)
candidate_df = safe_read_csv(candidate_csv, base_cols)
quarantine_df = safe_read_csv(quarantine_csv, base_cols)
db_inv = safe_read_csv(db_inventory_csv, ["name","path","extension","bytes","modified","sha256"])

unchanged_rows = int(dm.get("unchanged_rows", 0) or 0)
plan_metrics = {
    "candidate_rows": int(len(candidate_df)),
    "insert_rows": int(len(insert_df)),
    "update_rows": int(len(update_df)),
    "unchanged_rows": unchanged_rows,
    "conflict_rows": int(len(conflict_df)),
    "duplicate_quarantine_rows": int(len(quarantine_df)),
    "database_inventory_files": int(len(db_inv)),
    "db_write_enabled": False,
    "canonical_merge_enabled": False,
    "actual_write_allowed": False,
    "empty_csv_hotfix_applied": True,
    "powershell_metric_accessor_hotfix_required": True
}

expected_total = plan_metrics["insert_rows"] + plan_metrics["update_rows"] + plan_metrics["unchanged_rows"]
ready = (
    plan_metrics["candidate_rows"] > 0 and
    plan_metrics["conflict_rows"] == 0 and
    expected_total == plan_metrics["candidate_rows"]
)

status = "VDF_AKSHARE_BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_HOTFIX_READY" if ready else "VDF_AKSHARE_BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_HOTFIX_BLOCKED"
risk = "MEDIUM" if ready else "HIGH"
recommendation = "ALLOW_V0284_USER_APPROVAL_FOR_STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_NO_DB_WRITE" if ready else "BLOCK_V0284_UNTIL_STAGING_PLAN_DEFECTS_REPAIRED"
next_step = "v028.4 user approval for staging-write script generation only; no DB write" if ready else "repair staging plan defects; no DB write"

backup_plan = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "title": "Full DATABASE backup plan before any future write",
    "policy": {"db_write_enabled": False, "canonical_merge_enabled": False, "actual_backup_execution": False, "backup_plan_only": True},
    "steps": [
        "Create timestamped DATABASE backup folder under dict/VDF/_backup.",
        "Copy all existing DATABASE files into backup folder.",
        "Hash every copied file.",
        "Verify backup file count equals source file count.",
        "Abort future write if backup hash manifest has any failure."
    ],
    "metrics": plan_metrics
}
backup_plan_json.write_text(json.dumps(backup_plan, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

rollback_plan = {
    "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    "status": status,
    "risk": risk,
    "title": "Rollback plan before any future write",
    "policy": {"rollback_required": True, "db_write_enabled": False, "canonical_merge_enabled": False},
    "rollback_steps": [
        "Do not overwrite original database files directly.",
        "Write candidate data to staging table/file first.",
        "Validate staging table row count, key uniqueness, required fields, and hashes.",
        "Only after validation may future approved write promote staging artifact.",
        "If validation fails, restore from backup manifest."
    ]
}

'@

    $Replace = @{
        "__DRYRUN_SUMMARY_JSON__" = $DryRunSummaryJson.FullName
        "__INSERT_CSV__" = $InsertCsv.FullName
        "__UPDATE_CSV__" = $UpdateCsv.FullName
        "__CONFLICT_CSV__" = $ConflictCsv.FullName
        "__CANDIDATE_CSV__" = $CandidateCopyCsv.FullName
        "__QUARANTINE_CSV__" = $QuarantineCopyCsv.FullName
        "__DB_INVENTORY_CSV__" = $DbInventoryCsv
        "__BACKUP_PLAN_JSON__" = $BackupPlanJson
        "__ROLLBACK_PLAN_JSON__" = $RollbackPlanJson
        "__STAGING_PLAN_JSON__" = $StagingPlanJson
        "__POSTWRITE_VALIDATION_JSON__" = $PostWriteValidationJson
        "__APPROVAL_GATE_JSON__" = $ApprovalGateJson
        "__CHECKLIST_CSV__" = $PreWriteSafetyChecklistCsv
        "__OUT_JSON__" = $PyResult
    }

    foreach ($k in $Replace.Keys) {
        $Py = $Py.Replace($k, ($Replace[$k] -replace "\\","\\"))
    }

    Set-Content -LiteralPath $PyScript -Value $Py -Encoding UTF8

    $python = Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($null -eq $python) { throw "python not found in PATH" }

    $psi = [System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo]::new()
    $psi.FileName = $python.Source
    $psi.Arguments = "`"$PyScript`""
    $psi.UseShellExecute = $false
    $psi.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
    $psi.RedirectStandardError = $true
    $psi.CreateNoWindow = $true

    $p = [System.Diagnostics.Process]::Start($psi)
    $null = $p.WaitForExit(180000)
    $stdout = $p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd()
    $stderr = $p.StandardError.ReadToEnd()

    if ($p.ExitCode -ne 0 -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PyResult)) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "PYTHON_ENGINE" "Execution" "FAIL" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "exit_code" "$($p.ExitCode)" $stderr $PyScript
        throw "Python failed: $stderr"
    }

    $Result = Get-Content -LiteralPath $PyResult -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $R = $Result.metrics

    $CandidateRows = [int](def_GetProp $R "candidate_rows" 0)
    $InsertRows = [int](def_GetProp $R "insert_rows" 0)
    $UpdateRows = [int](def_GetProp $R "update_rows" 0)
    $ConflictRows = [int](def_GetProp $R "conflict_rows" 0)

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN" "CANDIDATE_ROWS" "Candidate Rows" "OK_READY_SOURCE" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_ONLY" "candidate_rows" "$CandidateRows" "Candidate rows verified for staging plan." $CandidateCopyCsv.FullName

PS7 function Invoke-VDF-AkShareUserApprovalForStagingWriteScriptGeneration-v0284 {
    param(
        [string]$ProjectRoot = "C:\Users\tonyk\Downloads\VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics",
        [switch]$OpenHtmlReport = $true
    )

    $script:OK = 0
    $script:WARN = 0
    $script:FAIL = 0

    function def_Stamp { Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss" }
    function def_Iso { (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffK") }
    function def_Mkdir([string]$p) { [System.IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($p) | Out-Null }
    function def_Html([AllowNull()][string]$s) {
        if ($null -eq $s) { return "" }
        return [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode($s)
    }
    function def_GetProp {
        param([object]$Obj,[string]$Name,[object]$Default = 0)
        if ($null -eq $Obj) { return $Default }
        $p = $Obj.PSObject.Properties[$Name]
        if ($null -eq $p) { return $Default }
        if ($null -eq $p.Value -or "$($p.Value)" -eq "") { return $Default }
        return $p.Value
    }
    function def_AddRow {
        param(
            [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]$Rows,
            [string]$Round,[string]$Stage,[string]$Layer,[string]$Name,
            [string]$Status,[string]$Risk,[string]$FixClass,[string]$Priority,
            [string]$Mode,[string]$Metric,[string]$Value,[string]$Message,[string]$Path
        )
        if ($Status -match "FAIL|FATAL|BLOCK") { $script:FAIL++ }
        elseif ($Status -match "WARN|REVIEW|MEDIUM|MISSING|EMPTY|QUARANTINE|APPROVAL|SCRIPT") { $script:WARN++ }
        else { $script:OK++ }

        $Rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Round=$Round; Stage=$Stage; Layer=$Layer; Name=$Name; Status=$Status; Risk=$Risk
            FixClass=$FixClass; Priority=$Priority; Mode=$Mode; Metric=$Metric; Value=$Value
            Message=$Message; Path=$Path
        }) | Out-Null
    }
    function def_SafeHash {
        param([string]$Path,[string]$ExcludeRoot)
        try {
            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Path) -or -not [System.IO.File]::Exists($Path)) { return "" }
            $full = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Path)
            $root = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($ExcludeRoot)
            if ($full.StartsWith($root,[System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { return "SKIPPED_CURRENT_RUNROOT" }
            return (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256 -ErrorAction Stop).Hash
        } catch [System.IO.IOException] {
            return "LOCKED_OR_BUSY"
        } catch {
            return "HASH_ERROR"
        }
    }

    if (-not [System.IO.Directory]::Exists($ProjectRoot)) {
        throw "ProjectRoot not found: $ProjectRoot"
    }

    $Stamp = def_Stamp
    $DictRoot     = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "dict\VDF"
    $ActiveRoot   = Join-Path $DictRoot "_active"
    $BackupRoot   = Join-Path $DictRoot "_backup"
    $DatabaseRoot = Join-Path $DictRoot "DATABASE"

    foreach ($d in @($DictRoot,$ActiveRoot,$BackupRoot,$DatabaseRoot)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Latest = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ActiveRoot -Directory -Filter "VDF_AKSHARE_BACKUP_ROLLBACK_STAGING_PLAN_HOTFIX_v02832_*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if ($null -eq $Latest) {
        throw "Latest v028.3.2 backup rollback staging plan hotfix run not found."
    }

    $RunRoot = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_AKSHARE_STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_APPROVAL_v0284_$Stamp"
    $ReportDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "report"
    $RegistryDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "registry"
    $PlanDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "plan"
    $ScriptDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "generated_script"
    $RuntimeDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "runtime"
    $BackupDir = Join-Path $RunRoot "backup"

    foreach ($d in @($RunRoot,$ReportDir,$RegistryDir,$PlanDir,$ScriptDir,$RuntimeDir,$BackupDir)) { def_Mkdir $d }

    $Rows = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    $ActivePointer = Join-Path $ActiveRoot "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER.json"
    $Transcript = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "VDF_AkShare_StagingWriteScriptGenerationApproval_Transcript_v0284_$Stamp.txt"

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "def VDF AKSHARE USER APPROVAL FOR STAGING-WRITE SCRIPT GENERATION v028.4" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "Generate reviewable staging-write script only · no execution · no DATABASE write · no canonical merge"
    Write-Host ""

    try { Start-Transcript -LiteralPath $Transcript -Force | Out-Null } catch {}

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ActivePointer) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Destination (Join-Path $BackupDir "VDF_ACTIVE_POINTER_before_v0284_$Stamp.json") -Force
    }

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "BOOT" "PROJECT_ROOT" "VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_ONLY" "exists" "true" "Project root exists." $ProjectRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_DB_WRITE" "DATABASE" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_ONLY" "db_write_enabled" "false" "No DATABASE write in v028.4." $DatabaseRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "NO_CANONICAL_MERGE" "Canonical Merge" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P0" "STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_ONLY" "canonical_merge_enabled" "false" "No canonical merge in v028.4." $RunRoot
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round0" "POLICY" "SCRIPT_GENERATION_ONLY" "Generated Script" "OK_POLICY_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_ONLY" "script_execute_now" "false" "Generate staging-write script but do not execute it." $ScriptDir

    $ResultJson = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "vdf_akshare_backup_rollback_staging_plan_hotfix_result_v02832.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $BackupPlanJson = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v02832_BackupPlan.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $RollbackPlanJson = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v02832_RollbackPlan.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $StagingPlanJson = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v02832_StagingWritePlan.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    $ApprovalGateJson02832 = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Latest.FullName -Recurse -File -Filter "VDF_AkShare_v02832_WriteApprovalGate.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1

    foreach ($x in @($ResultJson,$BackupPlanJson,$RollbackPlanJson,$StagingPlanJson,$ApprovalGateJson02832)) {
        if ($null -eq $x) { throw "Missing required v028.3.2 artifact for v028.4 script generation approval." }
    }

    $V02832 = Get-Content -LiteralPath $ResultJson.FullName -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $M = $V02832.metrics

    $CandidateRows = [int](def_GetProp $M "candidate_rows" 0)
    $InsertRows = [int](def_GetProp $M "insert_rows" 0)
    $UpdateRows = [int](def_GetProp $M "update_rows" 0)
    $ConflictRows = [int](def_GetProp $M "conflict_rows" 0)
    $DuplicateQuarantineRows = [int](def_GetProp $M "duplicate_quarantine_rows" 0)
    $DbInventoryFiles = [int](def_GetProp $M "database_inventory_files" 0)

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "V02832_RESULT" "v028.3.2 Result" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_ONLY" "source" "v02832" "Locked v028.3.2 result." $ResultJson.FullName
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "DISCOVERY" "STAGING_PLAN" "v028.3.2 Staging Plan" "OK_SOURCE_LOCKED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_ONLY" "source" "staging_plan" "Locked staging plan." $StagingPlanJson.FullName

    if ($CandidateRows -le 0 -or $ConflictRows -ne 0) {
        def_AddRow $Rows "Round1" "GATE" "READINESS" "Script Generation Readiness" "BLOCK_NOT_READY" "HIGH" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_ONLY" "candidate/conflict" "$CandidateRows/$ConflictRows" "Cannot generate script because candidate rows or conflict rows are invalid." $ResultJson.FullName
        throw "v028.4 blocked: CandidateRows=$CandidateRows ConflictRows=$ConflictRows"
    }

    $StagingPlan = Get-Content -LiteralPath $StagingPlanJson.FullName -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $InsertCsvPath = [string]$StagingPlan.candidate_inputs.insert_csv
    $UpdateCsvPath = [string]$StagingPlan.candidate_inputs.update_csv
    $CandidateCsvPath = [string]$StagingPlan.candidate_inputs.candidate_csv
    $QuarantineCsvPath = [string]$StagingPlan.candidate_inputs.duplicate_quarantine_csv

    foreach ($p in @($InsertCsvPath,$UpdateCsvPath,$CandidateCsvPath,$QuarantineCsvPath)) {
        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($p) -or -not [System.IO.File]::Exists($p)) {
            throw "Required future script input missing: $p"
        }
    }

    $GeneratedScript = Join-Path $ScriptDir "Invoke-VDF-AkShare-StagingWrite-GENERATED-NOT-RUN-v0285.ps1"
    $ScriptReviewJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0284_GeneratedScriptReview.json"
    $ApprovalGateJson = Join-Path $PlanDir "VDF_AkShare_v0284_UserApprovalGate.json"
    $ScriptHashJson = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v0284_GeneratedScriptHash.json"
    $PreExecutionChecklistCsv = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_v0284_PreExecutionChecklist.csv"
    $RuntimeResultJson = Join-Path $RuntimeDir "vdf_akshare_staging_write_script_generation_approval_result_v0284.json"
    $Top10Json = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_Top10_LocalFreeLibs_StagingWriteScriptGenerationApproval_v0284.json"

    $Generated = @"
#requires -Version 7.0
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

<#
GENERATED BY:
  VDF AkShare v028.4

STATUS:
  GENERATED ONLY. NOT EXECUTED.

SAFETY:
  - Default is review-only.
  - Does not run unless -ApprovalToken is exactly provided.
  - Requires -ExecuteWrite switch.
  - Creates backup before staging.
  - Writes staging artifacts first.
  - Does not promote canonical unless future logic explicitly approved.
#>

param(
    [string]`$ProjectRoot = "C:\Users\tonyk\Downloads\VeritasIntelligenceAnalytics",
    [string]`$ApprovalToken = "",
    [switch]`$ExecuteWrite,
    [switch]`$OpenHtmlReport = `$true
)

function def_Mkdir([string]`$p) { [System.IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory(`$p) | Out-Null }
function def_Stamp { Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss" }

`$RequiredToken = "APPROVE_VDF_AKSHARE_STAGING_WRITE_STAGE_ONLY"
`$Stamp = def_Stamp

`$DictRoot     = Join-Path `$ProjectRoot "dict\VDF"
`$DatabaseRoot = Join-Path `$DictRoot "DATABASE"
`$BackupRoot   = Join-Path `$DictRoot "_backup"
`$ActiveRoot   = Join-Path `$DictRoot "_active"

`$RunRoot = Join-Path `$ActiveRoot "VDF_AKSHARE_STAGING_WRITE_STAGE_EXECUTION_REVIEW_v0285_`$Stamp"
`$ReportDir = Join-Path `$RunRoot "report"
`$StagingDir = Join-Path `$RunRoot "staging"
`$RuntimeDir = Join-Path `$RunRoot "runtime"

foreach (`$d in @(`$DatabaseRoot,`$BackupRoot,`$RunRoot,`$ReportDir,`$StagingDir,`$RuntimeDir)) { def_Mkdir `$d }

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "def VDF AKSHARE STAGING WRITE GENERATED SCRIPT v028.5" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "================================================================================" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "This generated script is guarded. It will not write unless token + switch are provided."
Write-Host ""

if (-not `$ExecuteWrite -or `$ApprovalToken -ne `$RequiredToken) {
    Write-Host "[REVIEW ONLY] No write executed." -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Write-Host "Required to execute future staging-write:"
    Write-Host "  -ExecuteWrite -ApprovalToken `$RequiredToken"
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "PowerShell remains open. No exit." -ForegroundColor Green
    return
}

throw "Execution path intentionally disabled in generated v028.5 skeleton. Build an execution-approved script in a separate gate."
"@

    Set-Content -LiteralPath $GeneratedScript -Value $Generated -Encoding UTF8

    $ScriptHash = def_SafeHash $GeneratedScript $RunRoot
    $ScriptHashObj = [pscustomobject]@{
        generated_at = def_Iso
        generated_script = $GeneratedScript
        sha256 = $ScriptHash
        executed_now = $false
        db_write_enabled = $false
        canonical_merge_enabled = $false
    }
    $ScriptHashObj | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ScriptHashJson -Encoding UTF8

    $Review = [pscustomobject]@{
        generated_at = def_Iso
        status = "VDF_AKSHARE_STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_READY"
        risk = "MEDIUM"
        generated_script = $GeneratedScript
        script_sha256 = $ScriptHash
        candidate_rows = $CandidateRows
        insert_rows = $InsertRows
        update_rows = $UpdateRows
        conflict_rows = $ConflictRows
        duplicate_quarantine_rows = $DuplicateQuarantineRows
        db_inventory_files = $DbInventoryFiles
        policy = [pscustomobject]@{
            script_generated = $true
            script_executed_now = $false
            db_write_enabled = $false
            canonical_merge_enabled = $false
            actual_write_approval_now = $false
            generated_script_has_guard_token = $true
            generated_script_execution_path_disabled = $true
        }
        recommendation = "ALLOW_V0285_GENERATED_SCRIPT_REVIEW_ONLY_NO_DB_WRITE"
        next_step = "v028.5 generated script review only; no DB write"
    }
    $Review | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ScriptReviewJson -Encoding UTF8

    $Approval = [pscustomobject]@{
        generated_at = def_Iso
        status = "VDF_AKSHARE_V0284_USER_APPROVAL_GATE_READY"
        risk = "MEDIUM"
        approved_now = $false
        approval_required_for_next = $true
        what_is_generated = "guarded staging-write script skeleton"
        what_is_not_approved = "actual database write, canonical merge, promotion to canonical target"
        generated_script = $GeneratedScript
        required_future_token = "APPROVE_VDF_AKSHARE_STAGING_WRITE_STAGE_ONLY"
        next_step = "v028.5 generated script review only; no DB write"
    }
    $Approval | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ApprovalGateJson -Encoding UTF8

    $Checklist = @(
        [pscustomobject]@{item="candidate_rows_gt_0"; status= if ($CandidateRows -gt 0) {"OK"} else {"FAIL"}}
        [pscustomobject]@{item="conflict_rows_zero"; status= if ($ConflictRows -eq 0) {"OK"} else {"FAIL"}}
        [pscustomobject]@{item="duplicate_quarantine_excluded"; status="OK"}
        [pscustomobject]@{item="generated_script_created"; status= if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $GeneratedScript) {"OK"} else {"FAIL"}}
        [pscustomobject]@{item="generated_script_not_executed"; status="OK"}
        [pscustomobject]@{item="guard_token_required"; status="OK"}
        [pscustomobject]@{item="execution_path_disabled"; status="OK"}
        [pscustomobject]@{item="db_write_disabled"; status="OK"}
        [pscustomobject]@{item="canonical_merge_disabled"; status="OK"}
        [pscustomobject]@{item="future_approval_required"; status="OK"}
    )
    $Checklist | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $PreExecutionChecklistCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM

    $Result = [pscustomobject]@{
        generated_at = def_Iso
        status = "VDF_AKSHARE_STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_APPROVAL_READY"
        risk = "MEDIUM"
        recommendation = "ALLOW_V0285_GENERATED_SCRIPT_REVIEW_ONLY_NO_DB_WRITE"
        metrics = [pscustomobject]@{
            candidate_rows = $CandidateRows
            insert_rows = $InsertRows
            update_rows = $UpdateRows
            conflict_rows = $ConflictRows
            duplicate_quarantine_rows = $DuplicateQuarantineRows
            generated_script_count = 1
            script_executed_now = $false
            db_write_enabled = $false
            canonical_merge_enabled = $false
        }
        artifacts = [pscustomobject]@{
            generated_script = $GeneratedScript
            script_review_json = $ScriptReviewJson
            approval_gate_json = $ApprovalGateJson
            script_hash_json = $ScriptHashJson
            pre_execution_checklist_csv = $PreExecutionChecklistCsv
        }
        next_step = "v028.5 generated script review only; no DB write"
    }
    $Result | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $RuntimeResultJson -Encoding UTF8

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "SCRIPT_GENERATION" "GENERATED_SCRIPT" "Guarded Staging Write Script" "OK_SCRIPT_GENERATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_ONLY" "script_executed_now" "false" "Generated guarded script. Not executed." $GeneratedScript
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round2" "SCRIPT_GENERATION" "SCRIPT_HASH" "Generated Script Hash" "OK_HASH_CREATED" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P1" "STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_ONLY" "sha256" $ScriptHash "Generated script hash created." $ScriptHashJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "APPROVAL" "USER_GATE" "Approval Gate" "WARN_APPROVAL_REQUIRED" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_REQUIRED" "P0" "STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_ONLY" "approved_now" "false" "Actual write not approved. Next stage is script review only." $ApprovalGateJson
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CHECKLIST" "PRE_EXECUTION" "Pre-execution Checklist" "OK_CHECKLIST_CREATED" "LOW" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P1" "STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_ONLY" "items" "$(@($Checklist).Count)" "Pre-execution checklist created." $PreExecutionChecklistCsv

    $Top10Libs = @(
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Generated Script Review"; Language="PowerShell"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Pester; PSScriptAnalyzer; Get-FileHash; Start-Transcript; ConvertTo-Json; ImportExcel; PSWriteHTML; ThreadJob; ScheduledTasks; PSFramework"; UseCase="script review/hash/static checks/no-write guard"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Staging Write Future Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="duckdb; pandas; polars; pyarrow; pandera; pydantic; pytest; hypothesis; orjson; rich"; UseCase="future staging data validation"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="Backup Rollback"; Language="PowerShell"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Get-FileHash; Copy-Item; Compress-Archive; Start-Transcript; Pester; PSScriptAnalyzer; PSWriteHTML; ImportExcel; ThreadJob; ScheduledTasks"; UseCase="backup/hash/rollback/report"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="HTML UI Report"; Language="JS/HTML"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="Tabulator; DataTables; ECharts; Chart.js; Mermaid; Ajv; DOMPurify; FileSaver.js; Playwright; Lighthouse"; UseCase="matrix/report/schema/export"}
        [pscustomobject]@{FunctionArea="VRN Integration Gate"; Language="Python"; Top10LocalFreeLibs="pdfplumber; PyMuPDF; pdfminer.six; pypdf; camelot; tabula-py; opencv-python; pillow; pandas; pyarrow"; UseCase="future PDF/report extraction alignment"}
    )
    $Top10Libs | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $Top10Json -Encoding UTF8
    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "CONSOLIDATE" "LOCAL_FREE_LIBS" "Top10 Local Free Libs" "OK" "LOW" "PARALLEL_SAFE" "P2" "STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_ONLY" "rows" "$(@($Top10Libs).Count)" "Top10 local free libs matrix generated." $Top10Json

    def_AddRow $Rows "Round3" "RESULT" "READINESS" "VDF_AKSHARE_STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_APPROVAL_READY" "WARN_REVIEW_READY" "MEDIUM" "SEQUENTIAL_GATED" "P0" "STAGING_WRITE_SCRIPT_GENERATION_ONLY" "recommendation" "ALLOW_V0285_GENERATED_SCRIPT_REVIEW_ONLY_NO_DB_WRITE" "v028.4 completed. Script generated only. No DB write. No canonical merge." $RuntimeResultJson

    $CsvMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_StagingWriteScriptGenerationApproval_Matrix_v0284.csv"
    $JsonMatrix = Join-Path $RegistryDir "VDF_AkShare_StagingWriteScriptGenerationApproval_Matrix_v0284.json"
    $Rows | Export-Csv -LiteralPath $CsvMatrix -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8BOM
    $Rows | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $JsonMatrix -Encoding UTF8

    try {
        $ptr = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ActivePointer) {
            Get-Content -LiteralPath $ActivePointer -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
        } else {
            [pscustomobject]@{}
        }

PS7 function Write-Log {
    param([string]$Msg, [string]$Level = 'INFO')
    $line = ('{0} | {1,-5} | {2}' -f (Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'), $Level, $Msg)
    try { [System.IO.File]::AppendAllText($script:LogFile, $line + [Environment]::NewLine, [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8) } catch {}
    Write-Host $line
}

PS7 function Test-SafePath {
    param([string]$Path)
    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Path)) { return $false }
    # Normalize (resolve .. and .) WITHOUT requiring the path to exist, so a
    # crafted traversal cannot escape the whitelist. GetFullPath is self-contained.
    try { $full = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Path) } catch { return $false }
    $p = $full.TrimEnd('\').ToLowerInvariant()
    foreach ($root in $script:SafeRoots) {
        if ($p -eq $root -or $p.StartsWith($root + '\')) { return $true }
    }
    return $false
}

PS7 $csTrim = @'
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public static class VIAMem {
    [DllImport("kernel32.dll")]
    static extern bool SetProcessWorkingSetSize(IntPtr proc, IntPtr min, IntPtr max);
    public static bool Trim(int pid){
        try {
            var p = System.Diagnostics.Process.GetProcessById(pid);
            return SetProcessWorkingSetSize(p.Handle, (IntPtr)(-1), (IntPtr)(-1));
        } catch { return false; }
    }
}
'@

C:\Users\tonyk

PS7 function Invoke-PyProc {
    param([string]$Exe, [string[]]$ArgsList, [int]$TimeoutSec = 30)
    try {
        $psi = [System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo]::new()
        $psi.FileName = $Exe
        foreach ($a in $ArgsList) { $psi.ArgumentList.Add($a) }
        $psi.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
        $psi.RedirectStandardError  = $true
        $psi.UseShellExecute = $false
        $psi.CreateNoWindow  = $true
        $p = [System.Diagnostics.Process]::Start($psi)
        $out = $p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd()
        $err = $p.StandardError.ReadToEnd()
        if (-not $p.WaitForExit($TimeoutSec * 1000)) { try { $p.Kill() } catch {}; return [pscustomobject]@{ Ok = $false; Out = $out; Err = 'timeout' } }
        return [pscustomobject]@{ Ok = ($p.ExitCode -eq 0); Out = $out.Trim(); Err = $err.Trim(); Code = $p.ExitCode }
    } catch { return [pscustomobject]@{ Ok = $false; Out = ''; Err = $_.Exception.Message } }
}

PS7 function Get-MemSnapshot {
    $os = Get-CimInstance Win32_OperatingSystem
    $totalGB = [math]::Round($os.TotalVisibleMemorySize / 1MB, 2)
    $freeGB  = [math]::Round($os.FreePhysicalMemory   / 1MB, 2)
    $usedGB  = [math]::Round($totalGB - $freeGB, 2)
    $pct     = if ($totalGB -gt 0) { [math]::Round(($usedGB / $totalGB) * 100, 2) } else { 0 }
    return [pscustomobject]@{ TotalGB = $totalGB; UsedGB = $usedGB; FreeGB = $freeGB; UsedPct = $pct }
}
C:\Users\tonyk
 >

PS7 function Get-CpuLoad {
    try { return [int]((Get-CimInstance Win32_Processor | Measure-Object -Property LoadPercentage -Average).Average) } catch { return 0 }
}
C:\Users\tonyk
 >

PS7 function Get-DriveSnapshot {
    Get-CimInstance Win32_LogicalDisk -Filter 'DriveType=3' | ForEach-Object {
        [pscustomobject]@{ Name = $_.DeviceID.TrimEnd(':'); Root = $_.DeviceID + '\'
            UsedGB = [math]::Round(($_.Size - $_.FreeSpace) / 1GB, 2); FreeGB = [math]::Round($_.FreeSpace / 1GB, 2) }
    }
}
C:\Users\tonyk
 >

PS7 function Get-TopProc {
    param([int]$Top = 10)
    $mem = Get-Process | Sort-Object WorkingSet64 -Descending | Select-Object -First $Top |
        ForEach-Object { [pscustomobject]@{ Id = $_.Id; Name = $_.ProcessName; WorkingSetMB = [math]::Round($_.WorkingSet64 / 1MB, 2); Responding = $_.Responding } }
    return [pscustomobject]@{ Mem = $mem }
}

 >

C:\Users\tonyk

PS7 # ---- Fast fault-tolerant walk ----------------------------------------------
C:\Users\tonyk
 >

PS7 function Get-DirStat {
    param([string]$Root)
    $items = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    $total = [int64]0
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Root)) { return [pscustomobject]@{ Items = $items; TotalMB = 0 } }
    $stack = [System.Collections.Generic.Stack[string]]::new(); $stack.Push($Root)
    while ($stack.Count -gt 0) {
        $dir = $stack.Pop()
        try { foreach ($s in [System.IO.Directory]::EnumerateDirectories($dir)) { $stack.Push($s) } } catch {}
        try {
            foreach ($f in [System.IO.Directory]::EnumerateFiles($dir)) {
                try { $fi = [System.IO.FileInfo]::new($f); $items.Add([pscustomobject]@{ Path = $f; Len = $fi.Length; LWT = $fi.LastWriteTime }); $total += $fi.Length } catch {}
            }
        } catch {}
    }
    return [pscustomobject]@{ Items = $items; TotalMB = [math]::Round($total / 1MB, 2) }
}

PS7 function Invoke-CleanTarget {
    param([pscustomobject]$Target, [int]$Days, [switch]$Execute)
    $res = [ordered]@{ Key = $Target.Key; Path = $Target.Path; DeletedFiles = 0; DeletedDirs = 0; Skipped = 0; Errors = 0; FreedMB = 0.0; Notes = '' }
    if ($Target.Admin -and -not $script:IsAdmin) { $res.Notes = '需系統管理員 (Skipped)'; return [pscustomobject]$res }
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Target.Path)) { $res.Notes = '路徑不存在'; return [pscustomobject]$res }
    if (-not (Test-SafePath $Target.Path)) { $res.Errors = 1; $res.Notes = '安全白名單拒絕'; Write-Log ('REFUSED unsafe path: ' + $Target.Path) 'WARN'; return [pscustomobject]$res }

    $cutoff = (Get-Date).AddDays(-$Days)
    $stat = Get-DirStat -Root $Target.Path
    $freed = [int64]0
    foreach ($it in $stat.Items) {
        if ($it.LWT -ge $cutoff) { $res.Skipped++; continue }
        if (-not $Execute) { $res.DeletedFiles++; $freed += $it.Len; continue }
        try { [System.IO.File]::Delete($it.Path); $res.DeletedFiles++; $freed += $it.Len } catch { $res.Errors++ }
    }
    $res.FreedMB = [math]::Round($freed / 1MB, 2)

    if ($Execute) {
        $dirs = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
        $stk = [System.Collections.Generic.Stack[string]]::new(); $stk.Push($Target.Path)
        while ($stk.Count -gt 0) { $d = $stk.Pop(); try { foreach ($s in [System.IO.Directory]::EnumerateDirectories($d)) { $stk.Push($s); $dirs.Add($s) } } catch {} }
        foreach ($d in ($dirs | Sort-Object -Property Length -Descending)) {
            try {
                $hasAny = $false
                foreach ($x in [System.IO.Directory]::EnumerateFileSystemEntries($d)) { $hasAny = $true; break }
                if (-not $hasAny) { [System.IO.Directory]::Delete($d, $false); $res.DeletedDirs++ }
            } catch {}
        }
    }
    $verb = if ($Execute) { 'CLEAN' } else { 'SCAN ' }
    Write-Log ('{0} {1} files={2} dirs={3} freedMB={4} skip={5} err={6}' -f $verb, $Target.Key, $res.DeletedFiles, $res.DeletedDirs, $res.FreedMB, $res.Skipped, $res.Errors)
    return [pscustomobject]$res
}

PS7 # ---- Acceleration (CPU / DRAM / Memory) ------------------------------------
C:\Users\tonyk

PS7 function Invoke-Optimize {
    param([string]$Opt)
    $r = [ordered]@{ Opt = $Opt; Ok = $false; Detail = '' }
    switch ($Opt) {
        'gc'        { [System.GC]::Collect(); [System.GC]::WaitForPendingFinalizers(); [System.GC]::Collect(); $r.Ok = $true; $r.Detail = '.NET GC collected' }
        'trimall'   {
            if (-not $script:CsLoaded) { $r.Detail = 'WorkingSet API unavailable'; break }
            $n = 0; foreach ($p in (Get-Process)) { try { if ([VIAMem]::Trim($p.Id)) { $n++ } } catch {} }
            $r.Ok = $true; $r.Detail = ('Trimmed working set of {0} process(es)' -f $n)
        }
        'flushdns'  { try { Clear-DnsClientCache; $r.Ok = $true; $r.Detail = 'DNS cache flushed' } catch { $r.Detail = $_.Exception.Message } }
        'highperf'  { $out = & powercfg /setactive $script:HighPerfGuid 2>&1; $r.Ok = ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0); $r.Detail = if ($r.Ok) { 'Power plan -> High Performance' } else { ('powercfg: ' + ($out -join ' ')) } }
        'balanced'  { $out = & powercfg /setactive $script:BalancedGuid 2>&1; $r.Ok = ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0); $r.Detail = if ($r.Ok) { 'Power plan -> Balanced (restored)' } else { ('powercfg: ' + ($out -join ' ')) } }
        'recyclebin'{ try { Clear-RecycleBin -Force -ErrorAction Stop; $r.Ok = $true; $r.Detail = 'Recycle Bin emptied' } catch { $r.Ok = $true; $r.Detail = 'Recycle Bin already empty / skipped' } }
        default     { $r.Detail = 'unknown option' }
    }
    Write-Log ('OPTIMIZE {0} ok={1} {2}' -f $Opt, $r.Ok, $r.Detail)
    return [pscustomobject]$r
}

PS7 function Resolve-VIAEnv {
    $mgr = $null
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $script:EnvMgrCanonical) { $mgr = $script:EnvMgrCanonical }
    else {
        $cur = $PSScriptRoot; if (-not $cur) { $cur = (Get-Location).Path }
        for ($i = 0; $i -lt 4 -and $cur; $i++) {
            $probe = Join-Path $cur 'module\supportive_module\VIA_EnvManager.py'
            if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $probe) { $mgr = $probe; break }
            $parent = Split-Path -Parent $cur; if ($parent -eq $cur) { break }; $cur = $parent
        }
    }
    $py = $null
    foreach ($c in @('python', 'py')) {
        $cmd = Get-Command $c -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        if ($cmd) { $v = Invoke-PyProc -Exe $cmd.Source -ArgsList @('--version'); if ($v.Ok -or $v.Out -or $v.Err) { $py = $cmd.Source; break } }
    }
    if (-not $py) {
        $venvPy = Get-ChildItem -Path 'C:\Users\tonyk\envs' -Filter 'python.exe' -Recurse -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -First 1
        if ($venvPy) { $py = $venvPy.FullName }
    }
    $pyVer = ''; $psutil = ''
    if ($py) {
        $vr = Invoke-PyProc -Exe $py -ArgsList @('-c', 'import sys;print(sys.version.split()[0])'); if ($vr.Ok) { $pyVer = $vr.Out }
        $pr = Invoke-PyProc -Exe $py -ArgsList @('-c', 'import psutil;print(psutil.__version__)'); if ($pr.Ok) { $psutil = $pr.Out }
    }
    return [pscustomobject]@{ EnvMgr = $mgr; EnvMgrOk = [bool]$mgr; Python = $py; PythonVer = $pyVer; PsutilVer = $psutil; PsutilOk = [bool]$psutil }
}
C:\Users\tonyk
 >

PS7 function Install-Psutil {
    $env = Resolve-VIAEnv
    if (-not $env.Python) { return [pscustomobject]@{ Ok = $false; Detail = '找不到 python 直譯器' } }
    Write-Log ('pip install psutil -> ' + $env.Python)
    $r = Invoke-PyProc -Exe $env.Python -ArgsList @('-m', 'pip', 'install', '--upgrade', 'psutil') -TimeoutSec 180
    return [pscustomobject]@{ Ok = $r.Ok; Detail = (($r.Out + ' ' + $r.Err).Trim()) }
}

C:\Users\tonyk

PS7 function Invoke-TurboRun {
    param([bool]$Execute, [string[]]$Targets, [string[]]$Opts)
    $before = Get-MemSnapshot
    $cat = Get-TargetCatalog
    $cleaned = @()
    foreach ($key in @($Targets)) { $t = $cat | Where-Object { $_.Key -eq $key } | Select-Object -First 1; if ($t) { $cleaned += (Invoke-CleanTarget -Target $t -Days $AgeDays -Execute:$Execute) } }
    $optimized = @()
    foreach ($o in @($Opts)) { $optimized += (Invoke-Optimize -Opt $o) }
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 400
    $after = Get-MemSnapshot
    $sum = ($cleaned | Measure-Object -Property FreedMB -Sum).Sum; if (-not $sum) { $sum = 0 }
    return [pscustomobject]@{
        Execute = $Execute; Cleaned = @($cleaned); Optimized = @($optimized)
        TotalFreedMB = [math]::Round([double]$sum, 2)
        MemBefore = $before; MemAfter = $after
        MemReclaimedGB = [math]::Round($before.UsedGB - $after.UsedGB, 2)
        GenTime = (Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss')
    }
}

PS7 function Get-ScanResult {
    $cat = Get-TargetCatalog
    $targets = foreach ($t in $cat) {
        $exists = Test-Path -LiteralPath $t.Path
        $sizeMB = 0; if ($exists) { $sizeMB = (Get-DirStat -Root $t.Path).TotalMB }
        [pscustomobject]@{ Key = $t.Key; Desc = $t.Desc; Path = $t.Path; Admin = $t.Admin; Exists = $exists; SizeMB = $sizeMB; Blocked = ($t.Admin -and -not $script:IsAdmin) }
    }
    return [pscustomobject]@{
        GenTime = (Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'); IsAdmin = $script:IsAdmin; AgeDays = $AgeDays
        Mem = (Get-MemSnapshot); Cpu = (Get-CpuLoad); Drives = @(Get-DriveSnapshot)
        Targets = @($targets); Proc = (Get-TopProc -Top 10); Env = (Resolve-VIAEnv)
    }
}

PS7 function Invoke-SelfTest {
    Write-Host ''
    Write-Host '== VIA Turbo Optimizer  Self-Test ==' -ForegroundColor Cyan
    $tests = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    function Add-Test { param([string]$Name, [bool]$Ok) $tests.Add([pscustomobject]@{ Name = $Name; Ok = $Ok }) }

    Add-Test 'SafePath accepts user TEMP'        (Test-SafePath $env:TEMP)
    Add-Test 'SafePath rejects System32'         (-not (Test-SafePath 'C:\Windows\System32'))
    Add-Test 'SafePath rejects user Documents'   (-not (Test-SafePath (Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE 'Documents')))
    Add-Test 'SafePath rejects empty'            (-not (Test-SafePath ''))

    $cat = Get-TargetCatalog
    Add-Test 'Catalog has 8 targets'             ($cat.Count -eq 8)
    Add-Test 'Catalog keys are unique'           (($cat.Key | Select-Object -Unique).Count -eq $cat.Count)
    Add-Test 'WorkingSet trim type loaded'       ($script:CsLoaded)
    Add-Test 'Mem snapshot total > 0'            ((Get-MemSnapshot).TotalGB -gt 0)

    # Age-rule sandbox: old file counted, new file skipped (scan-only, no delete)
    $ageOk = $false
    try {
        $sand = Join-Path $env:TEMP 'VIA_SelfTest_Sandbox'
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $sand) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $sand -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $sand -Force | Out-Null
        $old = Join-Path $sand 'old.tmp'; Set-Content -LiteralPath $old -Value 'x'; (Get-Item $old).LastWriteTime = (Get-Date).AddDays(-10)
        $new = Join-Path $sand 'new.tmp'; Set-Content -LiteralPath $new -Value 'x'
        $tt = [pscustomobject]@{ Key = 'SelfTest'; Path = $sand; Admin = $false }
        $r = Invoke-CleanTarget -Target $tt -Days 3 -Execute:$false
        $ageOk = ($r.DeletedFiles -ge 1 -and $r.Skipped -ge 1)
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $sand -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    } catch {}
    Add-Test 'Age rule: old counted, new skipped' $ageOk

    # Frontend HTML well-formed (basic structural balance)
    $htmlOk = $false
    try { $htmlOk = ($script:HtmlBody -match '<html' -and $script:HtmlBody -match '</html>' -and $script:HtmlBody.Length -gt 2000) } catch {}
    Add-Test 'Frontend HTML rendered + balanced' $htmlOk

    $pass = ($tests | Where-Object { $_.Ok }).Count
    $fail = ($tests | Where-Object { -not $_.Ok }).Count
    foreach ($t in $tests) {
        if ($t.Ok) { Write-Host ('  [PASS] ' + $t.Name) -ForegroundColor Green }
        else       { Write-Host ('  [FAIL] ' + $t.Name) -ForegroundColor Red }
    }
    $sumColor = if ($fail -eq 0) { 'Green' } else { 'Red' }
    Write-Host ('-- {0}/{1} PASS --' -f $pass, $tests.Count) -ForegroundColor $sumColor
    Write-Log ('SELFTEST {0}/{1} PASS' -f $pass, $tests.Count)
    return ($fail -eq 0)
}
